### [1] [Rényi-2 entanglement of purification of the symmetric Werner state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00144)
*Shokoufe Faraji*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文精确计算了归一化对称Werner态在任意局域维度d≥2下的Rényi-2纯化纠缠(EoP₂)，得到闭式公式log₂[4d(d+1)/(d²+3d+4)]，并证明了张量幂及不同维度对称Werner态有限乘积的可加性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rényi-2纯化纠缠(EoP₂)作为纠缠度量，其精确值仅在极少数态上已知；对称Werner态是对称子空间上的重要高维纠缠态，其EoP₂的精确刻画及可加性此前缺乏完整结果，本文旨在填补这一空白并研究端点处的扰动行为。

Method: 构造规范纯化(canonical purification)并显式计算其Rényi-2熵，得到EoP₂上界；通过对任意联合纯化（允许无限制有限维辅助系统）建立匹配下界以证明最优性；进一步利用扰动分析与连续性论证研究偏离对称端点的行为。

Result: 对任意d≥2，EoP₂(W_{+,d})=log₂[4d(d+1)/(d²+3d+4)]；张量幂及不同局域维度对称Werner态有限乘积满足可加性，且规范纯化及其乘积在允许任意联合纯化时仍全局达到该值；最优值在对称端点处连续，最优纯度呈尖锐的平方根阶变化，且规范纯化在权重趋于零时在熵意义下渐近最优。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了对称Werner态EoP₂的精确公式与可加性定理，确立了规范纯化的全局最优性，并刻画了端点附近的连续性与平方根阶扰动结构，为高维纠缠度量的精确计算与可加性问题提供了新范例。

Abstract: This paper determines exactly the Rényi-2 entanglement of purification of the normalized symmetric Werner state in every local dimension $d\ge2$ \begin{equation*} \mathrm{EoP}_2(W_{+,d})=\log_2\frac{4d(d+1)}{d^2+3d+4}. \end{equation*} We establish additivity for tensor power and, more generally, additivity for finite products of symmetric Werner states with different local dimensions. A canonical purification and its products achieve these values globally, even when arbitrary joint purifications with unrestricted finite dimensional auxiliary systems are allowed. We further examine perturbations away from the symmetric endpoint, and prove the continuity of the optimal value at this endpoint; moreover, we show a sharp square root order of variation in the optimal purity, while the canonical purification remains asymptotically optimal in the achieved entropy as this weight goes to zero.

</details>


### [2] [Quantum simulation of real-time Yukawa dynamics with qubits and qumodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00201)
*Jack Y. Araz,Jeffrey Forshaw,Ross Jenkinson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出在混合qubit-qumode（囚禁离子）平台上模拟(1+1)维Yukawa理论的实时散射方案：费米子用离子的内态（staggered），标量场用集体振动模，Yukawa耦合对应天然的依赖自旋的位移操作；并用张量网络对该器件进行仿真，研究单标量粒子衰变与共振区附近的费米子-反费米子散射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时量子模拟可获取散射与衰变动力学，规避Euclidean格点方法的限制；但玻色场在qubit上需有限维截断且带来高成本的多qubit操作，混合qubit-qumode架构可把玻色场直接编码于谐振子中，从而避免这些问题。

Method: 在(1+1)维Yukawa理论中形式化散射问题：qubit上放置staggered费米子，qumode上放置标量场；选择囚禁离子为天然载体——费米子映射到离子内态、标量映射到集体运动模，使Yukawa耦合表现为本征的自旋依赖位移（spin-dependent displacement）。采用张量网络对器件进行emulation，选用可在器件上原生测量的可观测量，研究单标量粒子衰变及共振区附近的费米子-反费米子散射，并与格点微扰理论结果对比。

Result: 张量网络emulation能够分辨超出单圈自能Dyson求和之外的修正（corrections beyond the Dyson summation of the one-loop self-energy），并与格点微扰理论进行定量比较。

Conclusion: 混合qubit-qumode架构为实时量子模拟格点场论的散射与衰变提供了可行路径，囚禁离子是实现该方案的天然平台，且基于原生可观测量的emulation已能捕捉超越标准微扰重求和的动力学效应。

Abstract: Real-time quantum simulation of field theories offers access to scattering and decay dynamics, circumventing the limitations of Euclidean lattice methods. However, representing bosonic fields on qubits requires a finite-dimensional approximation and can entail costly multi-qubit operations. Hybrid qubit-qumode architectures avoid this by encoding bosonic fields directly in oscillators. We formulate scattering in the (1+1)-dimensional Yukawa theory for such a device, with staggered fermions on qubits and the scalar on qumodes. Trapped ions are a natural candidate, with the fermions on the internal states of the ions and the scalar on their collective motion, so that the Yukawa coupling becomes a native spin-dependent displacement. Emulating the device with tensor networks, we study the decay of a single scalar particle and fermion-antifermion scattering around the resonance region, using observables that can be measured on the device natively. We compare the results with those obtained using lattice perturbation theory. The emulation is able to resolve corrections beyond the Dyson summation of the one-loop self-energy.

</details>


### [3] [Exhaustive Construction of Generalized Parent Hamiltonians for Matrix Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00203)
*He-Ran Wang,Lorenzo Gotta,Sanjay Moudgalya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出将给定平移不变MPS作为本征态的广义父哈密顿量的穷尽构造，突破传统投影算子（无阻挫）框架，通过允许在PBC下相消的非厄米“望远镜”项，建立局域张量方程，把不等价广义父哈密顿量类与MPS转移矩阵谱一一对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画以有趣多体态为本征态的局域哈密顿量是量子多体物理的核心问题。对MPS，已有基于投影算子的父哈密顿量构造使MPS成为无阻挫基态，但量子多体疤痕等近期结果表明，这类构造远未穷尽所有以MPS为本征态的哈密顿量，缺少系统、完备的分类框架。

Method: 在传统“每个局域厄米项均以MPS为本征态”的投影构造之外，引入非厄米的望远镜（telescoping）项，这些项在周期边界条件下彼此相消；由此推导出一个局域张量方程，用于刻画广义父哈密顿量，并对其按“能否用无阻挫哈密顿量的线性组合相联系”划分等价类。

Result: 建立不等价广义父哈密顿量类与MPS转移矩阵谱之间的对应关系（局域张量方程的谱判据）；阐明其与非阿贝尔对称性、零关联长度等内禀物理性质的关联；并将构造推广至分块单射（block-injective）MPS。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了广义父哈密顿量与量子态局域结构之间的系统桥梁，给出以MPS为本征态的哈密顿量空间的完备刻画，涵盖疤痕类等非无阻挫情形，并可推广到更一般的MPS类。

Abstract: Characterizing local Hamiltonians that host interesting many-body states as eigenstates has been an important problem in quantum many-body physics. For matrix product states (MPS), there is a well-known construction of parent Hamiltonians that have the MPS as the frustration-free ground state. However, recent results on quantum many-body scars have shown that such a construction does not exhaust all possible Hamiltonians that have the MPS as an eigenstate. In this work, we develop an exhaustive construction of generalized parent Hamiltonians that have a given translation-invariant MPS as an eigenstate. These go beyond the conventional projector-based construction of frustration-free parent Hamiltonians, where each local Hermitian term has the MPS as an eigenstate, by allowing non-Hermitian telescoping terms that cancel out with periodic boundary conditions (PBC). We derive a local tensor equation that establishes a correspondence between inequivalent classes of generalized parent Hamiltonians, i.e., those that cannot be related using linear combinations with frustration-free Hamiltonians, and the MPS transfer matrix spectrum. We illustrate the connection to intrinsic physical properties, including non-Abelian symmetries and zero correlation length, and further extend this construction to block-injective MPS. Our framework hence provides a systematic bridge between generalized parent Hamiltonians and the local structure of quantum states.

</details>


### [4] [Distance to integrability and complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00233)
*Sean Casey,Marius de Leeuw,Tristan McLoughlin,Sergiy Zhuk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了自旋-1/2链上最近邻形式的可积模型集合，并借助数值算法为给定哈密顿量找到"最近"的可积模型，从而定义与模型无关的"到可积性的距离"，并考察混沌判据随该距离的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统混沌/可积性判据（如能级统计等）只在极端情形下取值明确，缺乏一个统一、模型无关的度量来量化"离可积有多远"，也难以在不同模型间比较。

Method: 限制在自旋-1/2链的最近邻相互作用族；先求出该形式下全部可积模型；再用数值算法在参数空间中搜索与给定哈密顿量"最近"（距离最小）的可积模型，据此定义到可积性的距离；随后将若干标准混沌判据表示为该距离的函数并加以比较。

Result: 随着到可积性距离增大，标准混沌判据在可积值与混沌值之间单调插值；但不同模型间差异很大，且该距离提供了这些诊断量无法直接给出的额外信息。

Conclusion: "到可积性的距离"是一个模型无关、可量化的补充指标：它能再现混沌判据的整体插值趋势，又揭示出判据本身无法区分的模型间差异，可作为刻画量子混沌过渡的新工具。

Abstract: We consider nearest-neighbour interactions on spin-1/2 chains. We find the full set of integrable models of this form and apply a numerical algorithm to determine the "closest" integrable model for a given Hamiltonian. This allows us to define a model-independent distance to integrability. We show that standard measures of chaos interpolate between integrable and chaotic values with increasing values of this distance. However, there is a large variation across models and the distance to integrability provides additional information not directly provided by these diagnostics.

</details>


### [5] [Benchmarking Quantum Convolutional Neural Networks and their classical surrogates on long-range strongly correlated models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00255)
*Tatiana A. Bespalova,Réka Schwengelbeck,Laura Mismetti,Johannes Schachenmayer,Ivano Tavernelli,Francesco Tacchino*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文对量子卷积神经网络（QCNN）及其经典替代模型在一维强关联体系（含超近邻项）的相区分任务上进行基准测试，并用量子-经典混合的Pauli反传方法研究截断参数对精度与开销的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要厘清混合量子-经典训练在何种强关联量子态学习任务中可行，并为Pauli反传替代模型选择合理的截断区间提供实践指导。

Method: 以各向异性Heisenberg自旋链和扩展t-t' Fermi-Hubbard模型作为自旋与费米子测试平台，在标记基态数据集上训练QCNN分类器并绘制有限尺寸相图；在混合设置中用Pauli反传将QCNN测量表示为Pauli串期望值的加权和，直接在输入态上求值，并扫描截断参数。

Result: Heisenberg模型在激进截断下仍稳健收敛；Fermi-Hubbard模型需要明显更宽松的阈值，导致保留的Pauli串数量与求值成本急剧上升。

Conclusion: 结果界定了混合量子-经典训练在强关联量子态学习中的可行范围，并为基于Pauli反传的替代模型给出截断区间选择的实用建议。

Abstract: We benchmark quantum convolutional neural networks (QCNNs) and their classical surrogates for phase differentiation in one-dimensional strongly correlated systems with beyond-nearest-neighbour terms. Using an anisotropic Heisenberg spin chain and an extended $t$-$t'$ Fermi-Hubbard model as representative spin and fermionic testbeds, we train QCNN classifiers on labelled ground-state datasets and map out finite-size phase diagrams. In hybrid setups, we employ Pauli backpropagation to express the QCNN measurement as a weighted sum of Pauli-string expectation values to be evaluated directly on input states, and study how truncation parameters control the trade-off between approximation accuracy and the size of the resulting Pauli expansion. Robust convergence is achieved for the Heisenberg model under aggressive truncation, whereas the Fermi-Hubbard model requires substantially looser thresholds, resulting in a sharp growth in retained Pauli strings and evaluation cost. Our results contribute to charting where hybrid quantum-classical training is viable for learning strongly correlated quantum states, and provide practical guidance for choosing truncation regimes in Pauli backpropagation-based surrogates.

</details>


### [6] [Triple descent in quantum linear regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00272)
*Gabriele Lo Monaco,Salvatore Lorenzo,Alessandro Ferraro,Mauro Paternostro,G. Massimo Palma,Luca Innocenti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文揭示量子线性回归模型中的采样噪声并非单纯的干扰，而会从根本上重塑学习问题的不稳定性：随测量结果数增长，预测误差呈现"三次下降"（triple-descent）曲线，包含来源迥异的两个峰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子极端学习机、量子保真度核等量子线性回归模型只能通过有限次测量射击估计特征，采样噪声通常被视为可用更多射击压制的干扰。作者质疑这一常规认知，旨在探究采样噪声对学习问题稳定性的本质影响。

Method: 将测量结果数作为变量，分析预测误差随其增长的演化；利用随机矩阵理论解析推导两个峰的形状，并纳入岭正则化（ridge regularization）的影响。

Result: 识别出两个性质不同的误差峰：第一个"经典峰"出现在测量刚达到信息完备之时，是经典线性回归双下降的直接类比，源于测量本身的病态性（ill-conditioning），仅通过预测方差影响结果，可通过增加推理射击数抑制；第二个"量子峰"出现在信息完备之后、结果数等于训练态数时，射击噪声填充非物理结果组合，回归以大幅权重拟合这些纯噪声特征，污染所学模型本身，增加训练或测试射击均无法消除，仅在精确训练特征的理想极限下才消失。

Conclusion: 将量子特征视为精确已知会高估经典不稳定性，同时遗漏真正量子的不稳定性；因此需将采样噪声作为学习问题结构的组成部分加以建模。

Abstract: Quantum linear regression models, such as quantum extreme learning machines and quantum fidelity kernels, are trained on features that can only be estimated from finitely many measurement shots. Sampling noise is usually treated as a nuisance that more shots suppress; we show instead that it can fundamentally reshape the instabilities of the learning problem. As the number of measurement outcomes grows, the prediction error displays a triple-descent profile, with two peaks of fundamentally different origins. The first, classical, peak occurs when the measurement first becomes informationally complete, is a direct analogue of double descent in classical linear regression, and is caused by ill-conditioning of the measurement itself. This peak affects predictions only through their variance, and is thus suppressed by increasing the number of shots at inference. The second, quantum, peak occurs beyond informational completeness when the number of outcomes matches the number of training states: shot noise populates unphysical outcome combinations and the regression fits these pure-noise features with large weights, corrupting the learned model itself. More training or test shots cannot remove this quantum peak, which disappears only in the ideal limit of exact training features. Using random-matrix theory, we derive the shape of both peaks analytically, accounting also for ridge regularization. Our results show that treating quantum features as exactly known overestimates classical instabilities while missing genuinely quantum ones.

</details>


### [7] [Application a quantum machine learning algorithm to the applied problem of modeling the thermal regime of a servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00303)
*P. V. Buzin,M. A. Remnev*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将量子机器学习应用于高性能计算集群中GPU温度预测的实际任务，量子模型与经典模型表现相当，并通过模型问题展示其潜在优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算快速发展，量子机器学习（QML）被认为有望超越经典机器学习，但其在真实应用中的适用性尚待验证，作者因此选取GPU温度预测这一实际任务进行探索。

Method: 训练一个量子电路，根据16个物理参数预测集群节点上8块GPU各自的温度，并将结果与经典机器学习方法进行对比；此外构造模型问题（model problems）进一步考察QML的潜力。

Result: 在该温度预测任务上，量子机器学习与经典机器学习性能相当；在模型问题中，量子机器学习显示出可能优于经典机器学习的潜力。

Conclusion: QML可有效用于真实世界的回归类预测任务，表现出与经典方法相当的实用性，并具备潜在的超越能力，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Quantum computing is a rapidly developed field of science. In the NISC era, quantum machine learning stands out, potentially outperforming classical machine learning. In this paper, we explore the applicability of quantum machine learning to a real-world application. We solved the problem of prediction of the temperature of GPUs in a high-performance computing cluster. We trained a quantum circuit to predict the temperature of each of the eight GPU of each cluster node based on 16 physical parameters. We demonstrated that quantum machine learning performs on this task just as well as classical machine learning. Furthermore, using model problems, we demonstrated that quantum machine learning can potentially outperform classical machine learning.

</details>


### [8] [Zero mode, Zero influence: Spatial-derivative coupling in particle detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00309)
*Eduardo Martín-Martínez,Ireneo James Membrere,Adam Teixidó-Bonfill*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在静态周期或Neumann腔中，采用腔系空间导数耦合可消除零模假设带来的因果性破坏与预测歧义，使探测器动力学与纠缠提取仅依赖约化探测器-振子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔QED粒子探测器模型中常忽略零模或直接假定其量子态，这会破坏因果性并使预测出现歧义，需要一种使零模态无关的耦合方式。

Method: 研究任意空间维数的静态周期腔与Neumann腔中无质量自由标量场，比较空间导数耦合、时间导数耦合及运动探测器静止系空间导数耦合对零模算符的作用，并在非微扰层面（含与零模的初始关联）分析探测器动力学与纠缠提取对约化探测器-振子态的依赖。

Result: 腔系空间导数湮灭零模算符且保持微观因果性；探测器动力学与纠缠提取只依赖约化探测器-振子态，该结论非微扰成立并容许初始关联；时间导数与运动探测器静止系空间导数可能保留零模态依赖，但不破坏因果性。

Conclusion: 空间导数耦合可在超导电路中借助电流耦合实现，从而无需对零模态作任何假设。

Abstract: It is common in particle detector models in cavity QED to often omit zero modes or assume their state, compromising causality or introducing ambiguity in their predictions. We show that spatial-derivative coupling eliminates both problems. For free massless scalar fields in static periodic or Neumann cavities of any spatial dimension, cavity-frame spatial derivatives annihilate the zero-mode operator while preserving microcausality. Detector dynamics and entanglement harvesting then depend only on the reduced detector-oscillator state, nonperturbatively and even with initial correlations with the zero mode. Time derivatives and moving-detector rest-frame spatial derivatives can retain zero-mode state dependence not compromising causality. Realized through current coupling in superconducting circuits, spatial-derivative coupling thus makes zero-mode state assumptions unnecessary.

</details>


### [9] [Early Fault-Tolerant Magic State Synthesis and Spacetime Efficient Analog Rotations with Color Code on a Superconducting Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00404)
*Sean Garner,Aaron Hoyt,Shuwen Kan,Samuel Stein,Chenxu Liu,Ang Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在超导量子处理器上实现两类魔术态生成范式——基于色码的STAR注入协议与容错魔术态合成(MSS)工厂，并系统比较了后选择、MLE与级联匹配解码的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 色码在局部连接量子处理器上因综合征提取调度、解码改进及完整横向Clifford门与格点手术的可用性而前景良好，通用计算的关键瓶颈在于以有限资源生成高质量的魔术态，而STAR（面向近期）与MSS（面向长期）此前尚未在真实硬件上实现。

Abstract: Quantum infrared spectroscopy (QIRS), which exploits quantum interference between photon-pair generation processes, enables infrared spectroscopy using only visible and near-infrared (VNIR) light sources and detectors. Extending the spectral coverage of QIRS into the long-wavelength infrared region up to 20 {\textmu}m is crucial for the identification and characterization of a broad range of materials. However, implementing QIRS beyond 10 {\textmu}m has been challenging because the nonlinear materials used to date do not provide suitable photon-pair sources in this spectral region. Here, we design a wavelength-tunable photon-pair source based on a gallium selenide crystal and develop a QIRS system covering a broad spectral range from 8.6 to 20 {\textmu}m. Mutual unbiasedness turns a population change for one basis into a phase change for every other. The worst case already occurs for a qubit encoded in two levels. Abstract: Positive-definite neural-network quantum states cannot directly represent the ground state of the PXP Hamiltonian, since the ground-state wavefunction carries a nontrivial relative-sign structure. Since the configuration graph of allowed configurations is connected, the Perron-Frobenius theorem guarantees a unique, strictly positive ground state of the transformed Hamiltonian, yielding an exact sign rule for the ground state of the original Hamiltonian in terms of the parity of the up-spin number. Variational Monte Carlo performed directly on the transformed Hamiltonian with a positive-definite ansatz therefore requires no architecture-specific modification to represent the true ground state.   <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 对label-symmetric算法的碰撞搜索，给出Magniez-Zur（arXiv:2609.10808）T²S=Ω(N log N)量子时间-空间折衷的简化证明；并在label symmetry假设下证明更强的经典折衷TS=Ω(N log N)。

Abstract: Given a quantum adversary that queries a random oracle, how do we characterize the information that they "know"? The Compressed Oracle method of Zhandry (CRYPTO 2019) is one method that records the queries that the adversary makes; this recording serves as a representation of the information that is learned. However, a significant limitation of the Compressed Oracle method is that it may continue to store information that the adversary has "forgotten." This issue is especially pertinent in the space-bounded setting, where the adversary may uncompute information to free up space, while the information may still reside in the compressed oracle.

By applying an isometry to the memory of a quantum adversary, we obtain a pair of compressed oracles with identical marginal distributions and classical correlations which correspond to the knowledge the adversary retains. In fact, we show that the outcome of measuring the new compressed oracle database corresponds exactly to applying the pretty-good measurement on the adversary's memory.

The proofs under our framework do not require representation theory and instead bound the mutual information between the compressed oracle pairs. This highlights both how the label symmetry assumption bypasses known proof barriers as well as the challenges that remain in achieving unconditional quantum time-space tradeoffs.

</details>

We apply the workflow to three previously published experimental cavity-QED datasets collected from color centers in diamond and silicon carbide. Across the experiments, the agent successfully identifies physical models for the system and characterizes the disorder. In assessing reproducibility, we find that run-to-run variability is concentrated primarily in the research plan generation step, while the computational analysis itself is substantially more consistent. </details>

Abstract: Iterative quantum optimization (IQO) uses quantum-generated information to select a sequence of classical problem reductions. We introduce Bound-Aware Tree-IQO which combines this iterative structure with bounds and reversible search. Feasible measured samples update a shared incumbent, while valid classical bounds prune branches that cannot improve it. Incorporating backtracking yields an anytime method that returns the best feasible solution found when interrupted, or can be run as a complete search to return and certify the optimum. For suitable single-variable or pairwise reductions, an initial IQO path can determine the branching schedule without further quantum calls, though certifying the optimum may take superpolynomial classical time. To motivate our framework, we prove two limitations of irreversible IQO reductions based on the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA). We show an infinite family of unweighted MaxCut instances on which globally optimized level-1 QAOA makes standard Recursive QAOA strictly prefer a first reduction that excludes every optimal solution, and limits that branch to an approximation ratio of at most $2/3+o(1)$. In contrast, a single backtrack recovers an optimal solution using the same quantum information. Causal cone arguments give a complementary obstruction at every fixed depth of standard QAOA: a weighted MaxCut instance is shown to contain two edges with identical two-qubit reduced states, even though every optimum requires opposite edge parities. MaxCut experiments show that QAOA-guided ordering is nonrandom in exact-certification tests through $n=28$, while demonstrating that quantum samples can strengthen the incumbent and enable further pruning. Our results suggest that quantum data could provide useful guidance toward solving hard combinatorial optimization problems more effectively than either quantum or classical algorithms alone.

</details>

Abstract: Photon loss somewhat limits fault-tolerant photonic quantum computing. We investigate its effect on finite-energy Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill (GKP) states by modeling the pure-loss channel directly. We obtain photon-loss threshold estimates for the surface-GKP code using nearest-lattice-point inner GKP decoding and minimum-weight perfect matching for the outer surface code. For GKP states with $ε=0.01$ and $ε=0.05$, corresponding to GKP squeezing of $20$ and $13\,\mathrm{dB}$, the estimated thresholds are $γ^\ast=5.5\%$ ($0.24\,\mathrm{dB}$) and $γ^\ast=5.9\%$ ($0.26\,\mathrm{dB}$), respectively. We then introduce an anti-photon-loss inner decoder that compensates for the contraction of the measured quadrature. It increases the corresponding threshold estimates to $γ^\ast=9.8\%$ ($0.45\,\mathrm{dB}$) and $γ^\ast=6.3\%$ ($0.28\,\mathrm{dB}$). Our results highlight the importance of accounting for loss-induced lattice contraction in inner decoding and demonstrate that quadrature compensation enhances the photon-loss tolerance of surface-GKP codes for both high- and moderate-quality GKP states, providing a route toward more photon-loss-robust optical quantum computing.

### [22] [Quantum algorithm for weakly correlated molecular electronic ground states with sub-quintic scaling in the system size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00751)

Abstract: Weakly correlated electronic structures are predominant among molecules, and are routinely solved with heuristic classical methods such as the ``gold standard'' CCSD(T) coupled cluster model, incurring a formal time complexity $O(n^7)$ in the number of molecular orbitals and with no guarantee of energy error $\varepsilon$ at or below chemical accuracy. In this work, a quantum algorithm is presented for ground state energy estimation of weakly correlated molecules using Trotterized time evolution, with an end-to-end quantum operations cost that scales as $\tilde{O}(n^{4.8}/\varepsilon^2)$ in the limit of high-order product formulae when run as a low-depth, shot-noise limited algorithm, or $\tilde{O}(n^{4.8} + n^{3.8}/\varepsilon)$ when scaling the simulation time $T\propto 1/\varepsilon$. ### [23] [Leaving Quantum Bias on the Table: Pushforwards as a Path to Quantum Advantage in Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00759)
*Chinonso Onah,Stuart Hadfield,Kristel Michielsen*

TL;DR: 提出一种"测量后修复"框架：用约束违反统计构造问题相关的经典可行性映射（repair map），将量子采样的原始分布推前（pushforward）到可行集上，并以排列约束下的匈牙利最大重叠修复（O(n^3)）在TSP上验证其大幅降低采样成本、提升解质量。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受约束的量子采样器常输出不可行解，但这些不可行输出中仍蕴含关于优良可行解的信息，直接丢弃浪费了大量采样资源。作者希望在不改动量子线路的前提下，把不可行样本"抢救"为可行候选，并给出可解释的理论框架来区分修复盆地几何与采样器概率集中两种效应。

Method: 1) 由约束违反统计构造问题相关的经典可行性映射（feasibility map / repair map），将原始量子采样概率律推前至可行集：每个修复解的概率等于其修复原像（preimage）上的原始概率质量总和，从而把"成功采样事件"从目标可行集扩展为完整修复原像。2) 推导修复成功率与期望代价的精确恒等式，并将修复盆地几何与采样器相关的概率集中分离。3) 对有噪实现，区分编码子空间外的泄漏（leakage）与子空间内的集中度损失。4) 对排列约束，采用匈牙利最大重叠修复，复杂度O(n^3)，并在TSP上研究。5) 推导条件修复成功上界，量化盆地聚合如何减少这些上界所需的绝对转移预算。6) 用无噪模拟、n=10 TSP及127比特IBM处理器（最多100比特编码）实验验证。

Abstract: Constrained quantum samplers often produce infeasible outputs that retain information about good feasible solutions. We develop a post-measurement framework that constructs problem-dependent classical feasibility maps from constraint-violation statistics and pushes the raw quantum sampling law forward onto the feasible set. The probability of each repaired solution is the total raw mass of its repair preimage, enlarging the successful sampling event from a target feasible set to its full repair preimage. We derive exact identities for repaired success and expected cost, and separate repair-basin geometry from sampler-dependent probability concentration. For noisy implementations, we distinguish leakage from the encoded subspace from concentration loss within it. For permutation constraints, Hungarian maximum-overlap repair provides an O(n^3) implementation, which we study on traveling-salesman problems (TSP). We derive conditional repaired-success bounds and quantify how basin aggregation reduces the absolute-transfer budget required by these bounds. Noiseless simulations show that optimal repair basins contain selectively overweighted infeasible contributors. For an n=10 TSP instance, pushforward repair recovered the optimum using approximately 10^3 shots, compared with 10^6 shots in the original CE-QAOA workflow without repair. On a 127-qubit IBM processor, using encodings of up to 100 qubits, repaired candidates match reference tours on smaller benchmarks and improve them on larger instances. These results establish concentration of quantum probability on useful repair preimages as a design principle for constrained quantum optimization.

</details>

At larger distances and later times, it remains sample dependent and is described by diffusion in a random potential with Brownian spatial statistics. The covariance of bounded, direction-sensitive position readouts from independent trials in the same field distinguishes these regimes. Simulations resolve their separation at finite dephasing. Abstract: When a variational quantum classifier performs poorly at detecting network attacks, it is hard to tell which part is to blame: the encoding, the trained circuit or the readout, which turns measurements into a class. In this work, we build upon a recent amplitude-encoded classifier, and we try to separate their contributions to the NSL-KDD benchmark in noiseless simulation. We compare angle and amplitude encoding, vary one component at a time, and measure each circuit against a classical yardstick: a support-vector machine on the encoded states, whose accuracy estimates what a well-trained circuit can reach. Most of the shortfall lies in the readout. Reading one qubit, or the parity of all qubits, leaves several percentage points unused, while a readout trained together with the circuit recovers most of them from the same measurements. This also explains why some classifiers stop improving as further layers are added. The encodings suit different needs. Angle encoding gives the more accurate detector on traffic like the training data and, from eight features up, shallower circuits; amplitude encoding needs far fewer qubits and catches more attacks of types never seen in training. Finally, we report that classical models, given the same inputs, are about as accurate as the best circuits, thus, registering no quantum advantage.

Using the entanglement-asymmetry (EA) diagnostic to monitor parity restoration, we compare coherent-product and cat-product initial states. Coherent-product states show conventional relaxation, with larger initial asymmetry decaying more slowly and no Mpemba crossing observed. Cat-product states, by contrast, can reverse this hierarchy over a finite region of initial-state space. This reversal is enabled by initial-state mode filtering: the cat-product superposition suppresses the contributions of the leading slow Liouvillian modes, allowing a more asymmetric state to restore parity through faster decay channels. Our results reveal that symmetry-restoration QME can arise even when parity is preserved only as a weak Liouvillian symmetry, with quantum jumps mixing the Hilbert-space parity sectors. The anomalous relaxation is governed by initial-state-dependent filtering of the leading slow Liouvillian modes, providing a mechanism for symmetry-restoration QME beyond strongly conserved symmetry sectors.

The probabilities of rejection under the two hypotheses are required to vanish exponentially, with exponents at least $K$ and $L$. We show that a quadruple $(a,b,K,L)$ of positive error and rejection exponents is achievable if and only if it satisfies three single-letter conditions: two conditions involve Petz--Rényi quantities and coincide with the converse bound conjectured to be tight by Ji and Regula; the third involves the $α$--$z$ Rényi quantities on the triangle $\max\{α,1-α\}\le z\le1$. The proof combines a sector decomposition inside Schur--Weyl blocks, an explicit one-shot measurement construction, and an exact minimax argument.

</details>

Abstract: Single-mode squeezing of the electromagnetic vacuum is a key resource for mode-selective noise reduction in quantum measurements. At microwave frequencies, single-mode squeezing is typically obtained by using Josephson and Traveling-Wave Parametric Amplifiers designed for three-wave mixing operation. In single-pump degenerate four-wave mixing, the coincidence of pump, signal, and idler frequencies complicates separation of the squeezed field from the strong pump. A dual-pump configuration enables degenerate operation while keeping both pumps spectrally separated from the squeezed mode. Here, we demonstrate single-mode squeezing in a flux-tunable, dual-pump Traveling-Wave Parametric Amplifier, employing reversed-Kerr phase matching. By independently calibrating the single-pump gains, we find that the strongest squeezing occurs along approximately balanced-gain trajectories, while the optimal squeezing does not necessarily correspond to the highest dual-pump gain. At flux biases $Φ_\mathrm{ext}/Φ_0 = 0.0$ and $0.5$, the minimum quadrature noise levels reach $-2.48 \pm 0.18~\mathrm{dB}$ and $-1.62 \pm 0.20~\mathrm{dB}$ relative to vacuum, respectively. These results establish the proposed architecture as a platform for single-mode squeezing in microwave continuous-variable quantum technologies.

Abstract: We develop normal factor graph (NFG) representations of stabilizer quantum error-correcting codes and entanglement-assisted quantum error-correcting codes. We introduce two complementary representations. Quantum-probability NFGs represent the quantum-mechanical probabilities associated with encoding, noise, and measurement. We also introduce a doubleedge formulation that makes the relationship between these two representations explicit. We apply the framework to convolutional and tail-biting convolutional stabilizer codes, as well as to two classes of low-density parity-check stabilizer codes. Finally, we extend the quantum-probability representation to entanglementassisted codes.

Abstract: Symmetric informationally complete positive operator-valued measures (SIC-POVMs, or SICs) are optimal minimal measurements for quantum state tomography. Problem~1 of Horodecki, Rudnicki and Życzkowski's open problems in quantum information asks for SICs in infinitely many dimensions. We state an explicit $GL_2$ reciprocity law for the squared ghost overlaps that implies $c_-=κ$, and we prove it for the computed algebraic overlaps in $d=5,7,11,15$ by Artin reciprocity and a check in every ray class. Zauner's conjecture remains open: the algebraic part of Stark's conjecture and the multiplet hypothesis are not proved here.

Abstract: Continuous quantum error correction depends on which parts of a measurement record remain available to the controller at recovery. Uniform lower and constructive upper bounds establish an optimal fidelity deficit of order $m^{-2}$ for $m$ persistent states in the diffusive model. The associated trace-preserving conditional expectations unify two reconstruction principles: each gives both the maximum-entropy state compatible with the accessible data and the closest state in the corresponding subalgebra in relative entropy. We prove that the charge-line severing map is the conditional expectation onto the local-product subalgebra. It is completely positive and quantum-trace-preserving, and uniquely reconstructs a state in this space from all local-product expectation values. The hierarchy of measurement restrictions then separates total bipartite coherence into three nonnegative contributions: coherence between local charges, inaccessible fusion information, and conventional coherence. The first two decompose the anyonic charge entanglement and distinguish the effects of local-charge superselection from the remaining fusion information. Finally, we relate basis-independent measures of anyonic coherence to entanglement. Abstract: We establish tight lower bounds on the adaptive and non-adaptive quantum query complexities of Promised $k$-to-one Finding on an exactly $k$-to-one input string $x\in\mathbb Z_q^n$, for every fixed $k\ge2$ and every feasible alphabet size $q\ge n/k$. The former matches the adaptive quantum algorithm of Liu and Zhandry [LZ19], whereas the latter matches classical birthday sampling even when the parallel queries are arbitrarily entangled. We also establish the non-adaptive lower bound $Ω_k(\sqrt{n/k})$ for $k$-to-one Testing when $q\ge n$, resolving an open question posed by Koiran, Landes, Portier, and Yao [KLPY10].

The adaptive proof bounds the growth of partial collisions through successive queries. The one-round proof instead analyzes the state after the parallel queries directly, bounding the probability that the compressed database contains a full collision. In both proofs, a short verification step accounts for correct outputs that include unrecorded positions. For the decision problem, we also compare one-round states with independent and distinct output labels.

Identical transmission channels instead give the same recovery at matched collection, regardless of their emission timescales. For a broader class of interacting sources, an ordering of collected and inaccessible coupling spectra forbids unassisted quantum transmission irrespective of internal memory. A separate coding example supports a positive rate where uniform transmission at matched actual yield has zero capacity. Together, these results identify what openness must change to assist recovery: the accessible transmission structure. They supply a physically constrained comparison of evaporation mechanisms, separating information delivered to radiation from coherence retained elsewhere or an increase in the collected output.

Calibrated monthly to the U.S. Treasury curve from 1985 to 2026, the long-term yield follows the 30-year yield with an in-sample correlation of 0.98. The Hamiltonian is stoquastic, so its ground state is positive and the energy can be estimated from computational-basis samples with a single measurement setting. On ibm_cleveland, gate noise appears mainly as bit flips of the high-order qubits. A bit-flip deconvolution with a tail set fixed by a WKB criterion, combined with readout mitigation and zero-noise extrapolation, gives four-qubit estimates within 33-125 bp of the grid ground-state energies for all three models, and within 23-67 bp for Treasury-calibrated parameters. On IonQ Forte Enterprise the same analysis reduces the error in the same proportions. On the same circuits, a Pauli-measurement VQE deviates by 791-1,129 bp, so the single-setting estimator gives about ten times smaller errors with one setting instead of nine. An on-device VQE converges from 4,939 bp to 130 bp.

</details>


### [58] [Tensor Contraction with ZX Calculus, a Graph-Theoretic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01890)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有张量收缩算法主要聚焦于寻找张量键的最优收缩顺序，但该问题是 NP-hard 的，且等价于求张量网络线图树宽的 NP-hard 问题。作者观察到：尽管一般情况下寻找最优逐键收缩顺序很难，但在许多实际场景中并不需要最优顺序，因此存在改进空间。

Method: 核心创新为"重写预处理"（rewrite preprocessing）：利用图论操作（本文聚焦于量子线路的图解语言 ZX calculus）对张量网络施加图变换，得到与原网络等价、但其线图树宽更低的张量网络，随后再应用常规的逐键（bond-by-bond）收缩算法。

Result: 通过大量数值模拟实验，验证了所提出的重写预处理 + 逐键收缩方法在降低树宽、提升收缩效率方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 将张量收缩的最优顺序搜索问题转化为对等价张量网络树宽的间接降低问题，用图重写（如 ZX calculus）预处理可绕开寻找最优收缩顺序的 NP-hard 障碍，构成一种实用且有效的张量收缩新范式。

Abstract: Tensors are higher dimensional generalisations of vectors and matrices. Tensor networks are a diagrammatic representation of complicated relations between tensors, and tensor contraction is the evaluation of a tensor network analogous to matrix multiplication. Tensor networks and tensor contraction are ubiquitous in computer science, physics, and applied mathematics.
  Most existing tensor contraction algorithms are focused on finding an optimal contraction order of the tensors' bonds. However, finding the optimal order is an NP-hard problem. It is equivalent to another possibly more straightforward but still NP-hard problem of finding the treewidth of the line graph of the tensor network.
  While in general finding the optimal bond-by-bond contraction order is hard, in many practical cases there is no need to find an optimal order. This observation leads to the core innovation of this thesis, which is a new tensor contraction paradigm that uses graph theoretic operations to produce an equivalent tensor network whose line graph has lower treewidth than the original tensor network, before applying the bond-by-bond contraction algorithm. We call these graph theoretical operations \textit{rewrite preprocessing}, and we name the algorithms that apply rewrite preprocessing before regular bond-by-bond contraction \textit{Tensor Contraction with Graph Operation}.
  There are many possible ways to perform rewrite preprocessing; in this thesis, we focus on the graph operations of ZX calculus, a diagrammatic language for quantum circuits. Ample numerical simulations have been performed to demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 无条件搜索：两能级分析与数值演化在明确假设下恢复Grover型标度；完全3-XORSAT：可达规范扇区能隙与规模无关，而传统横场绝热线能隙指数闭合；可满足3-SAT：有限规模基准显示在较短经典比较预算下获得高满足子句比例（未声称等成本量子优势）。

Conclusion: QPF构成区别于门电路与绝热演化的独立算法设计范式，其核心是在约束流形上设计路径与干涉；在搜索与XORSAT上展现优于常规绝热路径的能隙行为，3-SAT结果仅为有限规模启发式证据，量子优势仍需等成本比较，并具备超导qutrit实现前景。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms are commonly designed within the circuit paradigm or through adiabatic evolution. Here we introduce \emph{Quantum Path Finder} (QPF), a distinct algorithm-design paradigm based on non-Abelian gauge dynamics in a constraint-protected degenerate manifold. Each Boolean variable is promoted to a qutrit whose additional state represents an unassigned variable. The problem Hamiltonian then defines a connected manifold of feasible partial assignments, and time-dependent local qutrit rotations generate coherent transport on this manifold. Unlike conventional adiabatic quantum computation, QPF does not require the state to follow an instantaneous eigenstate along a prescribed interpolation: adiabaticity with respect to the constraint gap protects the feasible manifold, while adiabatic following within that manifold is optional. The algorithmic task is therefore to design paths and interference in constraint space rather than only to engineer a sequence of gates or a ground-state interpolation. We illustrate the framework with three applications. For unstructured search, a two-level analysis and numerical evolution recover Grover-type scaling under explicitly stated assumptions. For complete $3$-XORSAT, the accessible gauge sector has a size-independent gap, in contrast to the exponentially closing gap of a conventional transverse-field adiabatic path. For satisfiable $3$-SAT instances, finite-size benchmarks demonstrate high satisfied-clause fractions at short classical comparison budgets, without claiming an equal-cost quantum advantage. We also outline a superconducting-qutrit implementation and discuss the conceptual relationship between QPF and nondeterministic Turing machine.

</details>


### [60] [Rule-based hardware-configurable static analysis for quantum programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01995)
*Yi-Ting Chen,Lauren Capelluto,Ryan Shaffer,Jeffrey Heckey*

TL;DR: 提出规则驱动的 OpenQASM 静态分析框架 SHARP，将硬件相关约束从分析引擎中解耦，支持随 ISA 演进的可扩展量子程序验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子硬件快速演进，ISA 频繁更新，传统静态分析引擎与硬件约束紧耦合，需反复修改核心引擎才能适配新硬件，缺乏可移植性与可维护性。

Result: 展示了 SHARP 对硬件特定验证任务的支持，并讨论其在语言解耦、程序级约束验证与特性访问控制等方面的作用。

Conclusion: SHARP 为量子计算提供了可扩展、易维护的编程接口验证路径，使云量子服务无需改动核心引擎即可适配演化中的 ISA。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of quantum hardware necessitates an adaptable static analysis framework for validating quantum programs. In this work, we introduce SHARP, a rule-based static analysis framework designed for OpenQASM that decouples hardware-specific constraints from the validation engine. By employing a rule-based approach, SHARP allows quantum computing services to validate programs against evolving instruction set architectures (ISAs) without having to modify the core analysis engine. SHARP achieves this by ingesting ISA descriptions as standalone data, making the validation engine resilient to hardware updates. Furthermore, the framework employs an analysis-driven validation mechanism that enables multi-stage validation, simplifying rule construction and enhancing extensibility. We demonstrate how SHARP supports hardware-specific validation tasks and discuss its impact on quantum software development, including language decoupling, program-level constraint validation, and feature access control. Our findings suggest that SHARP provides a path toward a scalable and maintainable programming interface for quantum computing.

</details>

Conclusion: 原有结论——量子逻辑与经典逻辑存在多个互不等价、互不同构的模型——仍然成立，相关批评未能动摇之。

Yet, several authors have recently come with criticisms of this result. In this paper, we show that the criticisms are either unfounded or wrong.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 得到最优起始标度 γ(γ/J)^{1/(k-1)}，对网络规模与含噪 ancilla 一致成立；两体相互作用下，对任意固定有限规模 N≥2，主导最大重置率系数为 1/(2√2)，且允许混合重置目标与异质速率；在纯 Pauli 目标与相等重置速率下，该系数对增长网络亦一致保持。

Conclusion: 确立了自主量子比特网络中维持正稳态 magic 的重置成本最优标度，并给出与构造相匹配的紧致系数，揭示该成本对网络规模、ancilla 噪声及速率异质性的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Maintaining quantum magic under continuous noise requires coherent control and a supply of purity. We determine the cost of local stabilizer replacement resets sustaining any positive stationary magic in autonomous qubit networks with depolarization rate $γ$, interactions on at most $k$ sites, and Hamiltonian strength $J$ per site. An exact global stabilizer decomposition and a matching finite construction give the optimal onset scaling $γ(γ/J)^{1/(k-1)}$, uniformly over network size and noisy ancillas. For two-body interactions, the leading maximal-reset-rate coefficient is $1/(2\sqrt2)$ for every fixed finite size $N\ge2$, allowing mixed reset targets and heterogeneous rates. The same coefficient holds uniformly over growing networks with pure Pauli targets and equal reset rates. The converse combines hafnian stabilizer geometry with a positive random-order forest expansion.

### [63] [Detection times are not local observables in quantum field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02135)
*Charis Anastopoulos,Ntina Savvidou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在自然的协变性与一致性要求下，量子实验中记录到的“探测时间”所对应的可观测量无法在任意有界时空区域内实现代数局域化，即便时间记录本身是局域的；因此代数局域性不等同于测量的物理局域性，仅凭微观因果性无法推出量子场论中测量的非信号传递性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子实验通常记录探测时间，且人们习惯用局域代数可观测量刻画场论中的测量。作者质疑这种“代数局域化”能否刻画探测时间这一实际观测量，并澄清为何仅有微观因果性（microcausality）常被误认为足以保证测量层面的非信号传递。

Result: 即使探测记录本身局域于有界区域，描述这些时间的可观测量也不能代数局域于任何有界时空区域。由此，代数局域化与测量的物理局域化不能等同；微观因果性本身不足以建立量子场论中测量操作的非信号传递。

Abstract: We develop a geometric formulation of classical and quantum dynamics in which projective Hilbert space is taken as the primary state space and classical configuration and phase spaces arise as submanifolds of localized quantum states. The Fubini-Study metric induces the Euclidean metric on these submanifolds, while the tangential component of Schrödinger evolution reproduces Hamiltonian and Newtonian dynamics. We then extend the construction to stochastic motion. Brownian dynamics on the localized classical manifold is lifted to a common stochastic unitary evolution in projective Hilbert space; under homogeneity, isotropy, and independence assumptions, the corresponding Hermitian increments have Gaussian Unitary Ensemble statistics. This yields a random-matrix Schrödinger dynamics whose transition probabilities depend only on Fubini-Study distance. Through the embedding of localized states, the classical Gaussian transition law becomes the Born rule in projective state space. Measurement outcomes are identified with first arrival at detector-defined finite-resolution equivalence classes, while each realized state-space evolution remains linear and unitary. The framework is applied to classical emergence, macroscopic stabilization, double-slit experiments, composite systems, and entangled states. A spatially local Caldeira-Leggett-type system-environment model is also shown, under explicit thermal, symmetry, Ohmic, and coarse-graining assumptions, to generate the Brownian family required by the stochastic lift. The remaining open problem is to establish the range and universality of this mechanism for realistic many-body measuring environments.

At 0.99, the tolerated splitting-ratio deviation shrinks from 0.035 for visibility to 0.0035 for min-entropy, and the simulated yields are 68% (T) and 91% (Al1) for coupler visibility and 90% (T) and 66% (Al1) for Y-splitter min-entropy. Abstract: Although it has often been noted that both quantum mechanics and relativistic physics involve significant forms of relationality, the nature of the connection remains unclear. In addressing such a question it makes sense to start from the simplest possibility, so in this article I set out to consider the viability of the hypothesis that the two types of relationality are ultimately the same: that is, quantum states always characterize degrees of freedom which can be changed by a symmetry transformation, so they are relativized for exactly the same reason as partial observables in general relativity. I use the idea that we only measure partial observables to argue that this suggestion is compatible with all existing empirical evidence for quantum mechanics, and then I consider the consequences for ongoing debates about the nature of measurement in quantum mechanics.

Abstract: Experiments on a multi-time quantum process may reveal its responses only on selected, possibly overlapping, sets of times. We ask whether these partial descriptions admit a common underlying process, and formulate this process tensor marginal problem as a semidefinite feasibility test. Agreement on overlaps is necessary but insufficient: three locally consistent qubit marginals can have no common extension. Another family is incompatible as a time-ordered process even though a single quantum channel satisfies both its channel and temporal marginal constraints. This isolates temporal causality as the obstruction. We quantify incompatibility by the mixing needed to restore compatibility and derive finite tests using separate interventions at each time and final measurements. We further show that incompatibility is equivalent to an advantage over every compatible family in a multi-time process-discrimination task. Together, these results test whether process descriptions obtained in different experiments can arise from one causally ordered quantum process.

</details>

*Jixuan Ruan,Zhuo Cui,Zhengding Hu,Zhongkai Yu,Xiang Fang,Yue Guan,Jason Ludmir,Phillip E. Weinberg,Xiuzhe Luo,Shengtao Wang,Sonia Lopez Alarcon,Hezi Zhang,Yufei Ding*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子编译依赖人工设计的启发式方法，硬件演进时需昂贵地重新设计，成为进展瓶颈；而朴素单遍 LLM 方法难以应对量子编译的独特约束。

Method: 三级分解的智能体框架：(1) 将程序划分为顺序操作阶段；(2) 为放置、路由、优化分别部署专用智能体；(3) 利用解耦（分项）反馈实现精确纠错。此外，将 LLM 发现的新策略（convoy 式 shuttling）回注到既有 SOTA 编译器中。

Result: 相比 SOTA 基线 PowerMove，在真实工作负载上显著提升硬件运行时间与程序保真度，并能无缝适配新硬件能力；从回注策略中恢复了相当比例的 LLM 性能优势。

Conclusion: 三级分解的智能体化 LLM 设计可可靠处理复杂物理约束，既可直接作为编译器，也能发掘提升并发且不牺牲保真度的新策略；提供开放用户界面并公开代码。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer an adaptable alternative to traditional quantum compilation heuristics, which bottleneck progress by demanding costly manual redesigns whenever hardware evolves. However, naive single-pass LLM approaches fail under the unique challenges introduced by quantum compilation. We introduce Qubrio, a practical agentic framework that significantly reduces heuristic redesigns through a three-level decomposition. By (1) partitioning programs into sequential operation stages, (2) deploying specialized agents for placement, routing and optimization, and (3) using disaggregated feedback for precise error correction, our system reliably navigates complex physical constraints. Beyond serving as a direct compiler, our LLM framework also uncovers novel strategies that facilitate convoy-style shuttling, substantially enhancing hardware concurrency without sacrificing fidelity. We further integrate these strategies into the previous state-of-the-art (SOTA) compiler, recovering a significant fraction of the LLM's performance advantages. On realistic workloads, our compiler achieves significant improvements in hardware runtime and program fidelity over the SOTA baseline PowerMove, seamlessly adapting to new hardware capabilities. Qubrio features an open user interface for practical use, and its code is publicly available at https://github.com/crosaa/Qubrio.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 扩展维格纳朋友定理存在基于参考系依赖性的漏洞，该漏洞具有自然性与普适性，为量子力学与局域友善性的调和提供了新解释路径，也提示此类"不可能定理"的解读需更审慎地处理测量结果的意义与参考系问题。

Abstract: I argue that the Extended Wigner's Friend theorems have a `loophole' that has previously not been appreciated, coming from the fact that we typically need a reference system to give meaning to a measurement outcome. I show how this loophole can be used in the context of the Local Friendliness theorem to preserve the predictions of quantum mechanics without denying Local Friendliness, and I respond to some objections. I explain how the loophole arises naturally from consideration of relevant symmetry transformations, and then I appeal to the partial/complete observables formalism to argue that the same loophole will work for any type of Wigner's Friend experiment, even if it is performed with a variable that does not obviously look like it requires relativization to a reference system. Finally I compare the loophole to other possible responses to the Local Friendliness theorem, and I show that the same loophole will work for other Extended Wigner's Friend theorems too.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Finite-time driving in quantum thermodynamic cycles inevitably generates coherences in the instantaneous energy basis, leading to quantum friction and degrading the operational performance. In this work, we demonstrate that the non-adiabatic inner friction produced during unitary driving of a two-level system can be faithfully quantified by combining two distinct resource-theoretic notions: quantum texture (rugosity) and imaginarity. We show that neither rugosity nor imaginarity alone is sufficient to track the total non-adiabaticity, and we introduce a combined witness, which is strictly non-negative and vanishes if and only if the driving is quasi-static. Applying this framework to a finite-time quantum Otto refrigerator, we establish an exact closed-form relation connecting the coefficient of performance directly to the quantum texture and imaginarity, evaluated at the end of the unitary compression and expansion strokes. Furthermore, we show that local minima of the non-adiabatic witness accurately identify the operating regimes that maximize cooling performance. Our findings provide a geometric and resource-theoretic foundation for inner friction, offering practical diagnostic tools for optimizing finite-time quantum thermal machines.

Abstract: In this note, I describe a quantum algorithm that prepares an arbitrary state $|ψ\rangle$ to inverse exponential precision in polynomial time by making queries to an $\mathsf{NP}$ oracle, assuming one can directly query entries of the state vector corresponding to $|ψ\rangle$. I also discuss some implications of this result for the $\mathsf{QMA}$ search-to-decision problem and unitary synthesis.

Abstract: Quantum channel relative entropy can be nonadditive, making its regularization essential for asymptotic analysis. We study when the regularized value is attained at finite blocklength and how efficiently it can be approximated. For the first question, we establish a sharp dichotomy: the regularized value is either attained already at the single-copy level, implying additivity at every blocklength, or is never attained at any finite blocklength. Notably, a simple two-copy additivity test distinguishes between these alternatives for qubit channels and for channel pairs in arbitrary dimensions admitting an interior optimizer. We further show that both the interiority condition and finite nonattainment are generic, holding for almost every channel pair. For the second question, we establish explicit convergence rates for approximating the regularized channel relative entropy. For fixed channel dimensions, $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3/2})$ channel copies suffice to approximate the regularized value to additive accuracy $\varepsilon$. Alternatively, a semidefinite hierarchy achieves the same accuracy at level $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-2})$, yielding a certified polynomial-time approximation. Together, these results reveal the intriguing structure and generic behavior of regularized channel relative entropy, sharpening our understanding of both its analytical and computational aspects.

</details>

Abstract: A programmable quantum processor implements a target unitary using information stored in a classical or quantum program register. For arbitrary unitaries in $\mathrm{SU}(d)$, quantum programs attain the optimal leading-order memory cost, whereas deterministic classical rounding requires twice as much memory for the same diamond-distance error. We show that this gap disappears when classical randomization is allowed. We introduce barycentric programming, which stores the label of a unitary randomly selected from a finite set, with probabilities chosen so that first-order approximation errors cancel in the averaged unitary channel. Consequently, the diamond-distance error scales quadratically with the discretization spacing. For any $ν$-parameter family of unitaries forming a compact, smooth manifold without boundary, this method achieves error $\varepsilon$ using only $(ν/2)\log_2(1/\varepsilon)+O(1)$ classical bits. We also prove a matching lower bound for arbitrary quantum programs, determining the optimal leading-order program size for general unitary families. Thus, classical randomization achieves the optimal scaling $(ν/2+o(1))\log_2(1/\varepsilon)$ without requiring coherent quantum memory.

</details>

Abstract: Zero-noise extrapolation estimates ideal quantum observables by amplifying noise and extrapolating the measured response. Its accuracy depends on whether the extrapolation model captures the physical effect of amplification, including correlations between repeated operations. Here we derive the response of complete multiqubit gate folding in the simultaneous presence of time-local Markovian noise and stationary finite-band 1/f noise. An ordered circuit kernel couples the observable to noise memory; exact block counting shows how folding samples correlations at increasing time separations. The resulting response contains an r^2 ln r coordinate, with a coefficient fixed by the noise spectrum and the integrated forward-inverse control response. Ordinary polynomial Richardson extrapolation leaves a calculable contribution from this coordinate; the three-node rule (1,3,5) retains 2.73 theta. We construct response-matched extrapolation, including a log-domain implementation, that cancels this contribution at the retained order. Explicit bounds separate the remaining control-shape, finite-band, higher-order, and native-offset errors. The theory applies to arbitrary finite multiqubit circuits within the stated control and noise model, and distinguishes removal of memory-induced bias from an unconditional guarantee of the ideal intercept. Single- and ten-qubit numerical examples illustrate the predicted correction and its response dependence.

We show that the excess is largely an artefact of freezing the field. The P1 centres outside each cluster flip-flop in pairs that conserve their total $S_z$, generated by kinetic Monte Carlo at golden-rule rates from the dipolar Hamiltonian without adjustable parameters, and each cluster is propagated in the resulting time-dependent field. This dynamical mean field interpolates between the static-mean-field and mean-field-free limits. For a $^{14}$N P1 bath it reduces $T_2\cdot$[N] at 30 mT from 264 $\pm$ 16 to 188 $\pm$ 12 $μ$s ppm and gives 167 $\pm$ 11 $μ$s ppm at 10 mT, 1.7 and 0.4 standard deviations from experiment, with no dark spins. Letting each centre jump independently gives 147 $\pm$ 9 $μ$s ppm at 30 mT; the correlation of the flip-flop partners lengthens $T_2$ by 20-30 percent at high field. The Overhauser field is neither frozen nor motionally narrowed: 68-83 percent of its autocorrelation survives 200 $μ$s. At 2.5 mT, where the P1 electron and nucleus hybridise, $T_2$ remains below experiment for any choice of rates, so part of the field dependence remains ($T_2$ rises by a factor 1.69 $\pm$ 0.07 instead of 2.15 $\pm$ 0.08 from 2.5 to 30 mT).

Abstract: Homodyne breeding is a promising optical route for generating large cat states and Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill (GKP) grid states, using readily available squeezed-cat states as inputs. We analyze the performance of protocols, using squeezed single-photon seeds, $\hat{S}(r)\ket{1}$, as squeezed-cat resources and propagating them through the same cat-to-GKP breeding pipeline. We also investigate the effects of practical imperfections, including finite homodyne acceptance windows and photon loss. Abstract: Quantum state engineering frequently employs non-Gaussian operations, particularly photon addition and subtraction, to tailor and improve the nonclassical characteristics of quantum states. We investigate how photon addition to a two-mode squeezed Fock state (TMSFS) influences its performance in the Braunstein-Kimble quantum teleportation protocol. We obtain the Wigner characteristic function of the photon-added TMSFS (PA-TMSFS) and evaluate the corresponding teleportation fidelities for both Gaussian and non-Gaussian input states.
  We further compare the teleportation performance of PA-TMSFS with that of its photon-subtracted counterpart by accessing their fidelity enhancements over the parent TMSFS and considering the probabilistic nature of both the operations.

In particular, the symmetric $(1,1)$ photon subtraction yields the optimal trade-off between teleportation fidelity and success probability among the configurations investigated. These findings highlight that photon subtraction retains its advantage
  even when the underlying entangled resource is already non-Gaussian.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Fringes recovered by conditioning on quantum-eraser outcomes do not, by themselves, establish entanglement. We determine the minimum entanglement compatible with recorded eraser data when the preparation may be mixed, distinguishing these data from independently determined optimal which-path distinguishabilities and additional joint measurement outcomes. For noisy Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) preparations, explicit compatible states establish sharp entanglement-depth thresholds and show how parity information strengthens certification. A matching converse establishes the exact threshold for two complete Pauli-outcome distributions. For an ideal three-qubit eraser, we classify the additional marker measurement directions that certify genuine multipartite entanglement within a fixed eraser configuration and determine the separate minima of compatible concurrence and squared GHZ fidelity for specified tilted axes. For noisy three-qubit data, an exact semidefinite characterization and constructive converse determine biseparable compatibility at arbitrary fixed local measurement axes, with rational certificates bounding one example. These results establish what entanglement is required by the measurement record, rather than inferred from assumptions about the source.

*Jiayu He,Aviral Kumar Pandey,Krishna Mohan Mishra,Devendra Kumar Mishra,Matteo G. A. Paris*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究无损耗 n 端口环形腔中 n 个内部相位的同步局部估计，在全相干输入空间与腔工作点上优化，给出全局最优的充要条件，并证明平衡零差测量可达量子 Fisher 信息界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数估计并非单参数估计的简单叠加：不同参数的最优测量可能不对易，量子 Cramér-Rao 界（QCRB）不一定可达。环形腔因单个相干输入可重复与每个内部相位作用，是多参数估计问题的天然平台，需要确定其可同时最优估计多相位的条件与极限精度。

Method: 对无损耗 n 端口环形腔施加固定能量的多模相干态探针，在完整相干输入空间与腔工作点（内部相位与输入相位）上联合优化：以对称对数导数（SLD）代价函数与模型"sloppiness"为优化目标，分析量子 Fisher 信息矩阵（QFIM）、Uhlmann 曲率与相位不兼容性。

Result: 得到全局最优的充要条件：输入能量在各端口间均分，且各端口的内部相位与输入相位满足简单的相位匹配条件；除共同光学相位外最优解唯一。该点同时最小化 SLD 代价与模型 sloppiness；此时 QFIM 满秩且谱有闭式解，Uhlmann 曲率消失，无需支付不兼容性代价。精度保持相干态标度与相位数目依赖，但腔反馈使前置因子小于 n 次独立单程实验。此外，在每个输出端口做平衡零差测量、并将本振相位锁定到制备的输入相位时，其 Fisher 信息等于最优量子 Fisher 信息，故该界实际可达。

Conclusion: 环形腔可作为最优多相位传感平台，其最优经典（相干态）探针的精度达到 QCRB 且可由零差测量实现，为评估非经典探针带来的进一步精度提升提供了终极基准。

Abstract: Estimating several phases simultaneously is more than estimating them one at a time: the optimal measurements for different parameters may not commute, and in a multiparameter problem the quantum Cramér-Rao bound is not automatically achievable. Ring cavities are a natural setting for this question, since a single coherent input can be made to interact repeatedly with every internal phase before it is read out. We study the simultaneous local estimation of the $n$ internal phases of a lossless $n$-port ring cavity probed by a fixed-energy multimode coherent state, optimizing over the full coherent-input space and over the operating point of the cavity. We identify a necessary and sufficient condition for the global optimum: the input energy should be distributed equally among the ports, and the internal phases and input phases satisfy a simple phase-matching condition for every port. The optimum is unique up to a common optical phase and minimizes both the symmetric logarithmic derivative (SLD) cost and the sloppiness of the model. At this point the quantum Fisher information matrix is full rank and its spectrum is obtained in closed form, the Uhlmann curvature vanishes, and no incompatibility penalty has to be paid. The precision retains the coherent-state scaling and the dependence on the number of phases, but the cavity feedback reduces the prefactor relative to $n$ independent single-pass experiments. Finally, we show that this bound may be achieved in practice: the Fisher information of a balanced homodyne measurement at each output port, with local-oscillator phases locked to the prepared input phases is equal to the optimal quantum Fisher information. Our results establish the ring cavity as a platform for optimal multiphase sensing and provide the ultimate benchmark against which nonclassical probes may be evaluated for further precision enhancement.

</details>


### [82] [unitaryQIP = unitaryPSPACE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02819)
*Entong He,Fengning Ou,Mingnan Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明酉合成复杂度类相等：unitaryQIP = unitaryPSPACE，从而给出 QIP = PSPACE 与 stateQIP = statePSPACE 的酉版本类比，并导出常数轮并行化与关于 EFI 对存在性的复杂性障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 酉合成复杂度研究将目标酉算子作用于未知量子态的计算代价，把复杂性理论从经典输入/输出的问题框架推广到酉算子合成。Rosenthal–Yuen（ITCS 2022）引入了 unitaryQIP 与 unitaryPSPACE 两个类，但二者的关系（对应于经典 QIP=PSPACE 与 stateQIP=statePSPACE 的酉类比是否成立）此前未解决。

Method: 包含 unitaryQIP ⊆ unitaryPSPACE：由可靠性条件导出"接受刚性引理"（acceptance rigidity lemma），证明 state synthesis 中针对固定输入构造的多项式空间 prover 在所有输入上都实现目标酉算子，从而得到协议的多项式空间模拟。反向包含 unitaryPSPACE ⊆ unitaryQIP：基于 OpenAI 近期公布的酉合成问题解（对应 Aaronson–Kuperberg, Theory Comput. 2007 的问题），证明 unitaryPSPACE 族可在带 PSPACE 预言机的多项式时间内合成；再用 statePSPACE ⊆ stateQIP 协议制备辅助态来实现这些预言机查询。

Result: 证明 unitaryQIP = unitaryPSPACE；进一步表明当验证者获得多项式多份输入副本时，unitaryPSPACE 协议可并行化为常数轮；并得到一个关于证明 EFI 对存在性的复杂性理论障碍。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了酉合成复杂度的 QIP=PSPACE 型等式，将态合成中的多项式空间 prover 构造通过刚性引理提升到酉层面，并借助预言机合成与 stateQIP 协议完成双向模拟；副产品包括常数轮并行酉合成协议与对 EFI 对存在性证明的复杂性障碍。

Abstract: Unitary synthesis complexity studies the computational cost of applying a target unitary to an unknown quantum state, extending complexity theory beyond its traditional focus on problems with classical inputs and outputs. Rosenthal and Yuen (ITCS 2022) introduced two complexity classes for unitary synthesis: $\mathsf{unitaryQIP}$ and $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ for the unitary families implementable via interactive proofs and in polynomial space, respectively. In this work, we prove the equality $\mathsf{unitaryQIP}=\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ and thereby establish a unitary-complexity analogue of $\mathsf{QIP} = \mathsf{PSPACE}$ (Jain, Ji, Upadhyay, and Watrous, J. ACM 2011) and $\mathsf{stateQIP}=\mathsf{statePSPACE}$ (Rosenthal and Yuen, ITCS 2022; Metger and Yuen, FOCS 2023).
  To prove $\mathsf{unitaryQIP} \subseteq \mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$, we use an acceptance rigidity lemma derived from the soundness condition to show that the polynomial-space prover constructed for a fixed input in state synthesis (Metger and Yuen, FOCS 2023) implements the target unitary on every input, leading to a polynomial-space simulation of the protocol. For the reverse inclusion, we build on OpenAI's recently announced solution to the unitary synthesis problem (Aaronson and Kuperberg, Theory Comput. 2007), and show that $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ families can be synthesized in polynomial time with a $\mathsf{PSPACE}$ oracle. We then implement the oracle queries using auxiliary states prepared via the $\mathsf{statePSPACE} \subseteq \mathsf{stateQIP}$ protocol (Rosenthal and Yuen, ITCS 2022).
  Beyond the equality, we show that our protocol for $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ can be parallelized to constant rounds when the verifier receives polynomially many copies of the input, and we also obtain a complexity-theoretic barrier to proving the existence of $\mathsf{EFI}$ pairs.

</details>


### [83] [Reimagining computational quantum research with AI scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02833)
*Yuxuan Du,Hanqing Zhao,Xinbiao Wang,Yiming Lu,Kaining Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文揭示AI科学家难以复现已发表结果的根本障碍——论文表达与自主智能体需求之间的错配，并提出量子领域定制、可自我改进的Residual Agents（RA）方法，通过沉淀经外部验证的工作流修复经验显著提升复现性能，并构建基于MCP的可扩展验证平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI科学家正跨领域扩展自动化研究，但能否将科学文献记录转化为自主发现的基础仍不明确。作者旨在识别这一转变的核心障碍，并展示在计算量子科学中克服该障碍的路径。

Method: 构建OmniQ——一个经专家审核的虚拟研究环境，将已发表研究转化为可执行复现任务；覆盖59篇论文（经典模拟、量子算法、量子性质表征），系统评估5个LLM驱动的智能体系统与6种第三方智能体框架。基于人类专家干预可将失败转为成功复现的观察，提出Residual Agents（RA）：将外部验证的工作流修复蒸馏为可复用的研究经验，在固定基座模型与有限自适应预算下实现自我改进。最终将复现结果整合为基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的可扩展平台。

Result: 当前AI科学家常无法复现已发表结果；人类专家干预可将失败转化为成功复现。RA在固定基座模型、有限适应预算下显著提升性能，例如将DeepSeek V4.1 Flash的表现从68%提升至85%，以约三分之一成本匹配使用Opus 5的Claude Code（尽管基座模型更弱）。

Conclusion: 论文将AI科学家复现失败归结为论文所传达信息与自主智能体所需信息之间的根本错配，且该缺失信息可被识别并转化为可复用研究经验。RA验证了经验蒸馏式自我改进的有效性；基于MCP的开放平台使已验证研究工作流可直接被AI科学家调用，并支持持续纳入新论文。

Abstract: AI scientists are expanding automated research across scientific domains, yet their ability to turn the scientific record into a foundation for autonomous discovery remains elusive. Here we uncover a fundamental barrier to this transition and demonstrate a route to overcome it in computational quantum science, where advances shape both what we can learn from complex quantum systems and what quantum computing can ultimately deliver. To identify this barrier, we introduce OmniQ, an expert-audited virtual research environment that converts published studies into executable reproduction tasks. Across 59 papers spanning classical simulation, quantum algorithms and quantum-property characterization, we systematically evaluate five agentic systems powered by large language models and six third-party agent harnesses. Our findings reveal that current AI scientists often fail to reproduce published results, exposing a fundamental mismatch between what scientific papers communicate and what autonomous agents require. By contrast, human expert intervention can turn these failures into successful reproductions, showing that the missing information can be identified and converted into reusable research experience. Guided by this observation, we develop Residual Agents (RA), a quantum-tailored self-improving AI scientist that distils externally verified workflow repairs into such experience. RA substantially improves research performance under fixed base models and limited adaptation budgets. For example, RA raises DeepSeek V4.1 Flash performance from 68% to 85%, matching Claude Code with Opus 5 at a third of its cost despite using a weaker base model. We integrate all reproduced results into an extensible platform built on the model context protocol, making verified research workflows directly accessible to AI scientists and allowing new scientific papers to be continuously incorporated.

</details>

Abstract: The response of the Jaynes--Cummings (JC) model to strong driving has been examined previously, but the origin of intriguing features near emitter--cavity resonance remains poorly understood because of the large background in conventional transmission spectroscopy. Here we introduce a spectroscopic approach for circuit quantum electrodynamics that combines strong cavity driving with quantum-nondemolition qubit readout. Separating the drive and readout substantially enhances the measurement sensitivity as compared with conventional cavity-transmission spectroscopy. With this technique, we identify the near-resonant features as multiphoton transitions between emitter--photon dressed states of the same parity, and resolve transitions between dressed states of opposite parity. Away from resonance, we also observe selective enhancement and suppression of multiphoton transitions, together with transitions involving the second excited state of the anharmonic qubit. Our approach opens a route to resolving the rich transition structure of the JC ladder and probing strongly driven light--matter interactions.

</details>


### [85] [Full-Rank Constructions of Explicit Lossless Expanders and Good Small-Set qLTCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02963)
*Yali Aharon-Holzman,Michael Ilan,Assaf Reiner,Amnon Ta-Shma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构造满秩的双侧无损扩张图，据此得到显式渐近好量子LDPC码，并给出低温下寿命为 exp(Ω(N)) 的量子存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有无损扩张图与平衡积（balanced product）框架可生成量子LDPC码，但缺乏输入图邻接矩阵满秩的保证，导致校验矩阵无法满行秩、难以获得小集可靠度（confinement）与好的量子存储寿命。

Method: 在Hsieh–Lubotzky–Mohanty–Reiner–Zhang型无损扩张图上引入有限2-群自由作用，使邻接矩阵的单射性/满射性可在顶点轨道对应的固定尺寸矩阵上判定；在单一类型中间顶点处添加稀疏因子强制满秩（扩张仅有可控损失）。通过将Lubotzky–Phillips–Sarnak Ramanujan图生成元模2的幂（而非奇素数）约化，得到有限2-群上的Ramanujan Cayley图以提供该2-群作用；再将两个此类扩张图代入Lin–Hsieh平衡积，满秩性被校验矩阵继承。

Result: 得到X、Z校验矩阵均具满行秩、并在线性范围内满足小集可靠度的显式渐近好量子LDPC码族；结合Hong–Guo–Lucas分析，在其框架与动力学假设下，量子存储的自由能密度在每个正温度处解析，且在足够低的固定正温度下保证寿命为 exp(Ω(N))，N为码长。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了Hong–Guo–Lucas所寻求的码族，将低温下保证的寿命从拉伸指数级提升至指数级，并给出模2幂的Ramanujan Cayley图构造这一可能独立有价值的工具。

Abstract: We construct explicit two-sided lossless expanders whose binary adjacency matrices have full rank, and use them to obtain explicit asymptotically good quantum LDPC codes whose specified X- and Z-check matrices have full row rank and satisfy small-set soundness, or confinement, on a linear range.
  Our lossless expanders are a variant of those of Hsieh, Lubotzky, Mohanty, Reiner, and Zhang, with a free action of a finite 2-group. Under such an action, injectivity and surjectivity of the adjacency matrix are detected on a fixed-size matrix indexed by vertex orbits. Adding a sparse factor to the gadget at the middle vertices of a single type then enforces full rank, with a controlled loss in expansion. To obtain the 2-group action, we observe that reducing the generators of the Lubotzky-Phillips-Sarnak Ramanujan graphs modulo powers of 2, instead of modulo an odd prime, gives Ramanujan Cayley graphs on finite 2-groups. This construction may be of independent interest.
  Plugging two such expanders into the balanced product of Lin and Hsieh gives the quantum codes, since full rank of the input graphs is inherited by the check matrices.
  Our construction supplies the code family sought by Hong, Guo, and Lucas. Applying their analysis, within their framework and under their dynamical assumptions, gives quantum memories whose free-energy density is analytic at every positive temperature and whose guaranteed lifetime is $exp(Ω(N))$ at sufficiently low fixed positive temperature, where N is the blocklength. This improves the previously guaranteed stretched-exponential timescale.

</details>
