<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速率量子纠错码（如qLDPC码）实现的主要挑战之一是所需的远程相互作用。自旋量子比特平台可通过将量子比特穿梭至相邻位置来提供这种连通性，但码的性能取决于穿梭策略，因此需系统评估不同穿梭方案与硬件网格拓扑的影响。

Method: 面向未来自旋量子比特器件，构建方形网格与带对角线方形网格两种穿梭网格，对[[144,12,12]]双变量自行车（Gross）码开展存储（memory）实验；比较三种穿梭策略、结构化与随机初始布局，并覆盖从最优到最差情形的多种穿梭噪声模型。

Result: 最优穿梭策略是最小化最长移动距离而非总移动距离；初始布局仅产生微弱影响；在网格中加入对角线可使逻辑错误率降低最多两个数量级。

Conclusion: 穿梭策略应以最小化最长路径为设计准则，且网格拓扑（增加对角线连通）对逻辑错误率影响显著，这对未来自旋量子比特硬件设计具有启示意义。

Abstract: One of the major challenges of implementing high-rate quantum error correction codes, like qLDPC codes, is realizing the required long-range interactions. Spin qubit platforms can provide this connectivity by shuttling qubits next to each other whenever they need to interact. When doing this, the performance of the code depends on the shuttling strategy. We study memory experiments of the [[144,12,12]] bivariate bicycle (Gross) code on two shuttling grids for future spin qubit devices: a square grid and a square grid with diagonals. We compare three shuttling strategies, structured and random initial layouts, and a range of shuttling noise models spanning best- and worst-case scenarios. The best strategy minimizes the longest distance travelled rather than the total distance. The initial layout has only a weak effect, whereas adding diagonals to the grid reduces the logical error rate by up to two orders of magnitude which may have implications for future hardware design.

</details>


### [87] [eigenkit: A Provider-Neutral Quantum Computing Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02979)
*Laia Domingo,Daniil Tarpanov,Arsenii Senokosov,Asel Sagingalieva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: eigenkit 是一个厂商中立的量子计算库，通过统一电路表示与提供方接口将算法定义与后端执行分离，支持 12 个模拟器/硬件后端、后端无关梯度计算及 NumPy/PyTorch/scikit-learn 集成；在 IBM Heron 与 IQM Emerald 上的实验表明后端可移植性不仅便利，还影响计算效率与执行质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件生态中编程模型、功能支持与后端能力差异大，导致同一量子算法难以在不同执行环境间迁移和系统性比较。

Method: 设计 provider-neutral 库 eigenkit：以公共电路表示与 provider 接口解耦算法定义与后端执行；支持 12 个执行 provider（模拟器与真机）；提供后端无关的梯度计算；通过 NumPy、PyTorch、scikit-learn 接口融入科学计算与机器学习流程；分别在模拟器和量子硬件上评估抽象层的实用性。

Result: 模拟器性能随电路规模、结构、批处理和应用负载显著变化，不存在普遍最优的单一后端；在 IBM Heron 与 IQM Emerald 上，某处理器对给定算法的适配性不仅取决于硬件约束，还取决于算法拓扑与处理器物理连接性的匹配程度。

Conclusion: 后端可移植性不只是软件设计上的便利，对计算效率和量子执行质量都具有实质意义。

Abstract: Quantum software ecosystems provide increasingly capable tools for simulation and hardware execution, but differences in programming models, supported features, and backend capabilities can make it difficult to move and systematically compare the same quantum algorithm across execution environments. We introduce eigenkit, a provider-neutral quantum computing library that separates algorithm definition from backend execution through a common circuit representation and provider interface. The library currently supports 12 execution providers spanning simulators and quantum hardware, provides backend-independent gradient computation, and integrates with standard scientific-computing and machine-learning workflows through NumPy, PyTorch, and scikit-learn interfaces. We evaluate the practical relevance of this abstraction across both simulation and quantum hardware. Simulator performance varies substantially with circuit size, structure, batching, and application workload, showing that no single backend is consistently preferable. On quantum hardware, experiments on IBM Heron and IQM Emerald show that which processor is better suited to a given algorithm depends not only on hardware constraints, but also on how well the topology of the quantum algorithm matches the physical connectivity of the processor. These results demonstrate that backend portability is not only a software-design convenience, but can be important for both computational efficiency and quantum execution quality.

</details>


### [88] [Needle-beam generation from SPDC photons using inverse-designed flat optics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02993)
*Sunny,Jyotshna Bora,Ayush Tiwari,C. S. Yadav,Rajesh Menon,Amit D. Lad,Vishwa Pal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用紧凑型逆向设计多级衍射透镜（MDL）从自发参量下转换（SPDC）光中产生针状光束，并验证其非经典性与扩展焦深特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统折射透镜在SPDC量子光源照明下存在明显衍射展宽，缺乏可工程化调控扩展焦深（针状光束）的紧凑平面光学方案，以满足结构化量子光子学需求。

Method: 采用逆向设计的多级衍射透镜（MDL）；在MDL前以三探测器预示式Hanbury Brown–Twiss测量独立验证SPDC光源非经典性；用增强型CCD进行空间测量，比较共线SPDC照明下MDL与同标称焦距平凸透镜的轴向焦深；并考察非共线SPDC照明下的焦场分布。

Result: 零延迟二阶关联值约0.14（g²(0)≈0.14），证实单光子性质；共线照明下MDL在约11 mm轴向距离内保持紧聚焦分布，显著降低衍射展宽；非共线照明下出现两个分离的扩展轴向约束区域，表明焦场分布依赖入射空间场。

Conclusion: 建立了用紧凑平面光学（MDL）工程化调控扩展焦深SPDC光子场的方法，在结构化量子光子学中具应用潜力。

Abstract: We experimentally demonstrate the generation of needle-like beams from spontaneous parametric downconversion (SPDC) light using a compact inverse-designed multilevel diffractive lens (MDL). The nonclassical nature of the SPDC source is independently verified through a three-detector heralded Hanbury Brown-Twiss measurement performed before the MDL, yielding a zero-delay second-order correlation value of approximately 0.14. Spatial measurements using an intensified charge-coupled device reveal that the MDL maintains a tightly confined focal distribution over an axial distance of approximately 11 mm under collinear SPDC illumination, substantially reducing diffraction-induced broadening compared with a conventional plano-convex lens of the same nominal focal length. Under non-collinear SPDC illumination, two distinct regions of extended axial confinement are observed, demonstrating the dependence of the engineered focal distribution on the incident spatial field. These results establish a compact flat-optical approach for engineering extended-focus SPDC photon fields, with potential applications in structured quantum photonics.

</details>


### [89] [Nonreciprocal generation of Bell states via cooperative dissipation in a qubit-magnon system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03011)
*Fei Gao,Xiang-Jing Geng,Wei Feng,Lijiong Shen,Guo-Qiang Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出在qubit-magnon系统中利用协同耗散实现Bell态的非互易产生：正、负偏置磁场下暗态对应不同的Bell态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性量子技术需要对量子态进行定向产生与操控，而非互易纠缠态产生是其关键需求。

Method: 构建由超导transmon qubit与置于偏置磁场中的YIG球组成的qubit-magnon系统，二者共同耦合到同一传输线波导以引入协同耗散；在单激发子空间利用协同耗散形成的暗本征态，在适当参数下该暗态化为特定Bell态，借助驱动脉冲将系统制备于该暗态；偏置场反向时暗态对应另一Bell态。

Result: +B0偏置场下产生一种Bell态，−B0下产生另一种Bell态，从而实现Bell态的非互易产生；并定义基于保真度的非互易率来量化该非互易性。

Conclusion: 协同耗散可作为非互易纠缠态产生的通用资源，为手性量子技术提供新途径。

Abstract: Directionally generating and controlling quantum states is critical in chiral quantum technologies. Here, we propose a scheme for the nonreciprocal generation of Bell states via cooperative dissipation in a qubit-magnon system, consisting of a superconducting transmon qubit and a yttrium iron garnet sphere subject to a bias magnetic field. The cooperative dissipation between them is induced by coupling both to a common transmission-line waveguide. Due to this cooperative dissipation, the system possesses a dark eigenstate within the single-excitation subspace. Under appropriate parameter conditions, the dark eigenstate reduces to a specific Bell state. With the assistance of a drive pulse, the system can be prepared in this Bell state (i.e., the dark eigenstate). In particular, we find that the dark eigenstate corresponds to different Bell states under positive ($+\mathbf{B}_0$) and negative ($-\mathbf{B}_0$) bias fields. Consequently, nonreciprocal Bell-state generation is achieved: $+\mathbf{B}_0$ yields one Bell state, while $-\mathbf{B}_0$ yields the other. Moreover, we define a fidelity-based nonreciprocal rate to quantify this nonreciprocity. Our work establishes cooperative dissipation as a versatile resource for nonreciprocal entangled-state generation, opening a promising route toward chiral quantum technologies.

</details>


### [90] [Second-order mean force Gibbs state of fermionic open systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03089)
*David J. Strachan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在耦合强度二阶近似下，推导了任意化学势热库中一般费米子系统的巨正则平均力吉布斯（MFG）态，并引入"模式约化态"以保证费米子MFG态良定义；修正项分为粒子与空穴通道，二者经KMS条件关联。结果应用于共振能级模型、共享热库的双模系统及单杂质Anderson模型，并与数值精确结果（含相互作用模型的实时动力学）吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热力学假设弱耦合下系统弛豫到（巨正则）吉布斯态，但耦合非微弱时平衡态应为MFG态。然而现有MFG态的解析结果几乎仅限于玻色子热库，费米子情形缺乏一般解析理论，且费米子MFG态的定义本身存在技术困难。

Method: 对一般费米子系统，将其与任意化学势热库耦合，在耦合强度二阶展开下推导巨正则MFG态；引入"模式约化态"（mode reduced state）概念，使费米子MFG态在模式层面良定义；将修正项分解为粒子通道与空穴通道，分别以热库空态与占据态加权，并利用Kubo-Martin-Schwinger（KMS）条件建立两通道关系。

Result: 得到显式的二阶修正解析表达式；应用于共振能级模型、耦合共享热库的双模系统及单杂质Anderson模型。双模情形中，热库可在无直接相互作用的模式间诱导相干性，这是裸吉布斯态无法描述的。结果与数值精确计算（包括相互作用模型的实时动力学）一致，静态预测与动力学弛豫行为相符。

Conclusion: 本文提供了费米子体系在非弱耦合下平衡态的一般二阶解析理论，统一了粒子/空穴通道图像，并证明MFG态能刻画热库诱导的相干效应，静态预测与动力学弛豫一致，为强耦合费米子热力学奠定基础。

Abstract: Thermodynamics typically assumes that a system in contact with a thermal reservoir relaxes to the Gibbs state, or to the grand canonical Gibbs state when it also exchanges particles. However, this only holds when the coupling is very weak. Beyond this, the expected equilibrium state is the mean force Gibbs (MFG) state, obtained by tracing the reservoir out of the thermal state of the combined system. Despite its relevance, general analytic results for the MFG state have so far been obtained almost exclusively for bosonic reservoirs. Here we derive the grand canonical MFG state to second order in the coupling for a generic fermionic system coupled to a thermal reservoir at arbitrary chemical potential, using the concept of the mode reduced state in order for the fermionic MFG state to be well defined. The correction to the bare Gibbs state separates into particle and hole channels weighted by the empty and occupied reservoir states, related through the Kubo-Martin-Schwinger condition. We apply the result to the resonant level model, two modes coupled to a shared reservoir, and the single impurity Anderson model. For the two modes, the reservoir mediates coherence between modes that do not interact directly, something the bare Gibbs state cannot capture. We verify our results against numerically exact calculations, including real-time dynamics for the interacting model, and find agreement between the static prediction and the dynamical relaxation.

</details>


### [91] [On physical origin of Berry connection of free relativistic electrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03099)
*Chun-Fang Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 自由相对论电子的自旋与轨道角动量算符长期无共识；本文指出所谓"经由Berry联络的自旋-轨道耦合"并不存在，Berry联络描述的是量子参考点相对于实验室参考系的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对自由相对论电子自旋/轨道角动量算符选取缺乏共识的问题，澄清流行的"Berry联络导致自旋-轨道耦合"这一看法是否成立。

Method: 以Dirac方程正能解对应电子态为前提，主张只有投影到正能子空间后的算符才代表电子的物理量；将投影算符映射到不可约表示后进行分析。

Result: Berry联络并非自旋-轨道耦合的体现，而是刻画某个量子参考点相对于实验室参考系的位置，电子相对于该量子参考点的坐标才是正则的；该量子参考点绕实验室原点的轨道角动量由Berry联络表达，并与电子绕该参考点的正则轨道角动量相加，得到电子绕实验室原点的轨道角动量。

Conclusion: 不存在经由Berry联络的自旋-轨道耦合；Berry联络的物理含义是量子参考点位置的编码，从而为自由相对论电子角动量算符的物理解释提供了自洽框架。

Abstract: No consensus has been reached on the appropriate operators for the spin and orbital angular momentum of free relativistic electrons. But there is a tendency to believe the existence of a spin-orbit coupling through the Berry connection. Here we show that this is not the case. Because the positive-energy solution of the Dirac equation is associated with the state of the electron, it is reasonable to postulate that only when projected onto the positive-energy subspace can observable operators on the Hilbert space of the Dirac equation represent physical quantities of the electron. On this basis, we find, after mapping the projected operators onto an irreducible representation, that the Berry connection describes the position of a quantum reference point with respect to the laboratory reference frame, relative to which the position of the electron is canonical. The orbital angular momentum of the quantum reference point about the origin of the laboratory reference frame is expressed in terms of the Berry connection. It combines with the canonical orbital angular momentum of the electron about the quantum reference point to give the orbital angular momentum of the electron about the origin of the laboratory reference frame.

</details>


### [92] [Belief Propagation-Informed Tesseract Decoding of Quantum LDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03128)
*Sana Javed,Francisco Garcia-Herrero,Bane Vasić,Mark F. Flanagan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出BP引导的Tesseract解码器改进，用阻尼和积BP跳过或引导A*搜索，在保持接近Tesseract逻辑错误率的同时，将搜索节点扩展数在低物理错误率下降低两个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于搜索的解码器（如Tesseract）通过A*搜索最小权重错误事件集以获得高精度，但其搜索开销随物理错误率和码规模快速增长，需要降低搜索代价同时保持低逻辑错误率。

Method: 对量子LDPC码，在Tesseract搜索前对被触发的探测器应用阻尼和积BP：若BP硬判决触发的探测器与原始触发集一致则直接接受并跳过搜索；否则用带信任系数的BP边缘概率估计修正搜索优先级，路径代价仍用信道先验权重以保持解码目标不变。另支持BP初始化：以选定BP判决为起点进行残差搜索，其带符号代价目标可剔除错误初始判决，并以节点探索上限约束，超限则回退到原始触发集上的BP引导搜索。

Result: 在电路级噪声下的[[72,12,6]]、[[90,8,10]]和[[144,12,12]]双变量自行车码上评估；采用BP初始化与全局信任系数时，低物理错误率下Tesseract搜索节点扩展数最多降低两个数量级以上，逻辑错误率仍接近Tesseract。

Conclusion: BP引导可在不改变解码目标、不显著损失精度的前提下大幅削减搜索型解码器的计算开销，适用于量子LDPC码的实用化实时解码。

Abstract: In quantum error correction, the decoder must estimate the errors from the measurement data accurately and fast enough to avoid a backlog. Search-based decoders such as Tesseract achieve high accuracy by finding a minimum-weight set of error events with an A* search, but their search effort grows rapidly with the physical error rate and the code size. To reduce this search effort while maintaining a logical error rate close to that of Tesseract, we propose a belief propagation (BP)-guided modification of the Tesseract decoder for quantum low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes. Damped sum-product BP is applied to the triggered detectors before the Tesseract search. If the detectors triggered by the BP hard decision match the triggered detectors, this hard decision is accepted directly, and the Tesseract search is skipped. Otherwise, the BP marginal probability estimates, weighted by a trust coefficient, modify the search priority, while the path cost keeps the channel-prior weights so that the decoding objective is unchanged. The framework also supports BP-based initialization, in which selected BP decisions form the starting point of a residual search whose signed-cost objective allows incorrect initial decisions to be removed. A node-exploration limit bounds this residual search; if the limit is reached, the decoder falls back to the BP-guided search on the original triggered detector set. The method is evaluated on the [[72,12,6]], [[90,8,10]], and [[144,12,12]] bivariate bicycle codes under circuit-level noise. With BP-based initialization and a global trust coefficient, the number of Tesseract search nodes expanded is reduced by up to more than two orders of magnitude at low physical error rates, while the logical error rate remains close to that of Tesseract.

</details>


### [93] [Correlated Catalysis Removes the Strong Converse for Entanglement Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03148)
*Piotr Masajada,Aby Philip,Alexander Streltsov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明关联催化（correlated catalysis）可对所有可蒸馏的二体态消除纠缠蒸馏的强逆定理：在任意目标速率 R>E_D 下，最优渐近保真度为 E_D/R，且该优势在催化剂每个有限块长精确返还、输出与催化剂关联渐近消失的条件下依然成立，并刻画了维持该非零保真度所需的催化剂尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠蒸馏的强逆定理表明，当速率超过可蒸馏纠缠 E_D 时，与目标态的保真度渐近趋于零，即超过阈值的传输完全不可靠。作者希望探究：通过引入催化剂（辅助纠缠态）这一额外资源，能否在不超过最大可靠速率的前提下打破这种"全有或全无"的强逆行为。

Method: 在 LOCC（本地操作与经典通信）框架下引入关联催化协议，研究目标速率 R>E_D 时的渐近最优保真度；分析催化剂在有限块长被精确返还、输出态与催化剂的关联渐近趋于零的严格约束条件下的性能；并对维持非零保真度所需的催化剂规模进行定量刻画。

Result: 对所有可蒸馏二体态，关联催化均消除了强逆定理；在任意 R>E_D 时最优渐近保真度恰为 E_D/R，即保真度随速率增大而平滑衰减而非骤然归零。该优势在催化剂精确返还及关联渐近消失的条件下仍保持。

Conclusion: 催化不能提高最大可靠蒸馏速率（阈值仍为 E_D），但可将原本的强逆"断崖"转化为非零保真度的连续退化，实现每一有限速率下的非零保真度；论文进一步给出维持该优势所需催化剂尺寸的刻画，深化了对催化资源在纠缠蒸馏中作用的理解。

Abstract: Entanglement distillation converts noisy shared quantum states into maximally entangled pairs using local operations and classical communication. The distillable entanglement $E_D$ determines the maximum asymptotic rate at which this process can be performed reliably. For several classes of quantum states, distillation above this rate obeys a strong converse: the fidelity with the target state vanishes asymptotically. Here we show that correlated catalysis removes the strong converse for every distillable bipartite state. Specifically, at any target rate $R>E_D$, the optimal asymptotic fidelity is given by $E_D/R$. Thus, although catalysis does not increase the maximum reliable distillation rate, it allows nonvanishing fidelity at every finite rate. Remarkably, this advantage persists even when the catalyst is returned exactly at every finite blocklength and the correlations between the output and catalyst vanish asymptotically. We further characterize the catalyst size required to sustain nonvanishing fidelity above the reliable distillation rate.

</details>


### [94] [Taming a macroscopic free mass through continuous quantum measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03159)
*Umang Mishra,Teodor Strömberg,Gabriela Valencia-Guzman,Felipe Gómez-Lozada,Marta Marchese,Onur Hosten*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实现了处于自由粒子区域、环境隔离极佳的1毫克悬浮转子，阻尼率低至7 nHz，通过连续量子测量将其运动局域化，获得熵温度低至140 nK的条件态，比此前台式光力实验低两个数量级，并用于限制量子力学修正与基准化引力测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由粒子是量子力学中最基本的动力学系统，但固体机械振子虽已实现日益增大的量子控制，自由粒子对应的控制仍难以实现；耗散与环境噪声阻碍了在自由粒子固有的低频域实现控制，而该低频域对量子传感与基础物理检验日益重要。

Method: 制备一个悬浮的1毫克转子并使其工作于自由粒子区域，实现优异的环境隔离；结合低噪声测量架构，直接分辨对应阻尼率仅7 nHz的运动的熱涨落；对转子进行连续量子测量以局域化其原本无约束的运动，得到条件态。

Result: 直接分辨出对应7 nHz阻尼率的热涨落；获得熵温度低至140 nK的条件态，比此前台式光力实验低两个数量级；对量子力学修正给出新约束，并在量子—经典界面引力测试中，将推断的纠缠—退相干速率比基准化到接近当前最优水平的两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 该工作开辟了自由粒子区域的量子控制新工况，证明在极低耗散下可对宏观悬浮转子实施连续量子测量与条件态制备，并为量子力学基础检验与量子—经典界面引力实验提供了显著提升的基准。

Abstract: The free particle is the most elementary dynamical system in quantum mechanics. Despite remarkable progress in quantum control of increasingly massive solid-state mechanical oscillators, the analogous control of the free-particle counterpart has remained elusive. Dissipation and environmental noise have prevented such control in the inherently low-frequency domain of free-particle dynamics---a regime of growing importance for quantum sensing and tests of fundamental physics. Here we realize a suspended 1-milligram rotor operating in the free-particle regime with exceptional environmental isolation. Combining this isolation with a low-noise measurement architecture, we directly resolve thermal fluctuations in the motion associated with a damping rate of only 7~nHz. Through continuous quantum measurement of the rotor, we localize its otherwise unconfined motion, obtaining conditional states with entropic temperatures as low as 140~nK as a quantitative measure of control in the free-particle regime. This is two orders of magnitude below temperatures previously achieved in tabletop optomechanical experiments. We demonstrate immediate utilities of the new operating regime, setting new constraints on modifications of quantum mechanics and benchmarking inferred entanglement-to-decoherence rate ratios nearly two orders of magnitude beyond the state of the art for tests of gravity at the quantum-classical interface.

</details>


### [95] [Spacetime Emergence in the Memory of a Learning Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03596)
*Philip A. LeMaitre,T. Rick Perche*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文表明时空的因果结构可以在一个执行量子信息任务的学习智能体的内部记忆中出现：模型型强化学习智能体在没有被告知度规或因果结构的情况下，通过最大化局域量子探针与闵可夫斯基时空量子场之间的量子互信息，构建出内部预测模型，其规划器自发形成特征性的零结构，从而高精度重建闵可夫斯基度规的因果结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望探讨时空几何概念能否从"操作性的量子经验"中涌现，而非作为先验输入被给定；即检验时空度规与因果结构是否可由智能体在量子信息任务中的成功预测与行动中导出。

Method: 采用基于模型的强化学习（model-based RL）智能体，任务目标是最大化局域量子探针与闵可夫斯基时空中量子场相互作用的量子互信息；训练中不向智能体提供任何关于时空度规或因果结构的信息，训练后分析其内部预测模型与规划器所隐含的结构。

Result: 智能体在学会任务的过程中构建了内部预测模型，其规划器发展出时空特征性的零（null）结构，据此可高精度重建闵可夫斯基度规的因果结构。

Conclusion: 时空几何可从智能体的成功预测与行动中获得，这为"时空概念源自操作性量子经验"提供了初步证据。

Abstract: We show that the causal structure of spacetime can emerge within the memory of a learning agent performing a quantum information task. We train a model-based reinforcement-learning agent to maximize the quantum mutual information between localized quantum probes interacting with a quantum field in Minkowski spacetime, without directly providing it with information regarding the spacetime metric or causal structure. In learning to accomplish this task, the reinforcement-learning agent constructs an internal predictive model whose planner develops the characteristic null structure of spacetime, allowing us to reconstruct the causal structure of the Minkowski metric with high accuracy. Within this task, spacetime geometry is obtained from an agent's successful predictions and actions, providing a first step toward showing that spacetime concepts emerge from operational quantum experience.

</details>


### [96] [Lee-Yang theorems for $q$-deformed oscillators and doubly-scaled SYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03624)
*Arpon Basu,Siddhant Midha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明 q-变形振子真空配分函数 L_q(z) 满足 Lee-Yang 性质（零点全在虚轴上，q∈[-1,1)），从而证实 Okuyama'23 的猜想，并借助与双标度区 SYK 模型的联系，给出退火 SYK 的 Fisher 零点位于虚轴、淬火 SYK 存在宽度恒定的无零点带的高概率结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Okuyama'23 针对 q-变形振子的真空配分函数提出零点分布的猜想；同时 SYK 模型配分函数零点的位置，对理解其热力学极限下的相结构以及淬火–退火差异具有关键意义，但此前仅有大 k 复制计算，缺乏严格结果。

Method: 将 φ_q(z)=L_q(iz) 表示为"q-变形"匹配多项式的局部一致极限，证明这些多项式实根（从而 φ_q 实根并属 Laguerre-Pólya 类）；利用 Touchard–Riordan 公式导出联系 q-导数与 z-导数的偏微分方程，通过分析根的连续运动证明实根性；再用 q-变形振子与双标度 SYK 哈密顿量的联系，把结论迁移到 SYK 配分函数。

Result: L_q 的全部零点落在虚轴线上（所有 q∈[-1,1)），猜想得证；q-变形多项式实根性推出 q-变形匹配矩 M_ℓ(q)=Σ_{π∈P₂(2ℓ)} q^{cr(π)} 的新对数凹关系；双标度热力学极限下退火 SYK 配分函数具 Lee-Yang 性质（Fisher 零点全在虚轴）；淬火 SYK 配分函数以高概率存在常数宽度的无零点带。

Conclusion: 该工作以组合多项式的实根性为核心工具，严格证明了 q-变形振子及双标度 SYK 的 Lee-Yang 性质，为 Zlokapa–Kiani'26 的大 k 复制计算提供了非微扰的严格补充，并附带得到匹配矩的对数凹性。

Abstract: We show that the vacuum partition function of the $q$-deformed oscillator
  \[
  L_q(z):= \langle0|e^{zT_q}|0\rangle, \qquad T_q := \mathsf{a}_q + \mathsf{a}_q^*
  \] satisfies the Lee-Yang property, i.e., all zeros of $L_q$ lie on the imaginary line for all $q\in[-1, 1)$, proving the conjectures of Okuyama'23. Using the connection between $q$-deformed oscillators and the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) Hamiltonian in the doubly-scaled regime, we show that the annealed SYK partition function satisfies the Lee-Yang property in the thermodynamic limit, that is, all of its Fisher zeros are on the imaginary axis. As a result, we show that the quenched SYK partition function admits a zero-free strip of constant width with high probability, rigorously complementing the large$-k$ replica calculations of Zlokapa and Kiani'26.
  We show the imaginary-rootedness of $L_q$ by expressing $\varphi_q(z):= L_q(\mathbf{i} z)$ as a locally uniform limit of ``$q$-deformed'' matching polynomials and then showing that those $q$-deformed polynomials are real-rooted. This establishes that $\varphi_q$ is real-rooted, and in fact belongs to the \emph{Laguerre-Pólya} class. The real-rootedness of the $q$-deformed polynomials yields novel log-concavity relations on the \emph{$q$-deformed matching moments}
  \[M_\ell(q):= \sum_{π\in\mathcal{P}_2(2\ell)}q^{\operatorname{cr}(π)}.\]
  We show the real-rootedness of the $q$-deformed polynomials by using the Touchard--Riordan formula to derive a partial differential equation relating their $q$-derivatives to their $z$-derivatives, and then analyzing the resulting motion of their roots.

</details>


### [97] [Quantum channel tomography: optimal bounds in the near-boundary regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03639)
*Kean Chen,Aadil Oufkir*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文解决了量子信道层析在近边界区（1<τ<1+o(1)）的查询复杂度空白，给出diamond范数与Choi迹范数误差下的紧界，从而完全确定量子信道层析的查询复杂度，并揭示两种定性不同的Heisenberg-to-classical转变形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作[CGO+26]指出扩张率τ=rd2/d1≥1控制查询复杂度的Heisenberg-to-classical转变，边界区（τ=1）与非边界区（τ≥1+Ω(1)）的最优界已确定，但近边界区（1<τ<1+o(1)）的精确行为尚未解决，本文旨在填补这一空缺。

Method: 针对输入维度d1、输出维度d2、Kraus秩至多r的未知信道，在diamond范数误差ε与Choi迹范数误差ε两种度量下分别构造下界论证（并匹配已有上界），以刻画近边界区查询复杂度。

Result: diamond范数下查询复杂度为Ω(rd1d2/ε²)，与[MB26, CGO+26]的上界匹配；Choi迹范数下为Θ(d1²/ε+min{(τ−1)d1², (τ−1)²d1²d2²}/ε²)。

Conclusion: 量子信道层析的查询复杂度被完全确定；diamond范数层析中Heisenberg标度一旦τ离开边界区即突然消失，而Choi迹范数层析则随τ从边界区向非边界区移动呈现从Heisenberg到经典的渐进转变，说明二者是性质不同的两类转变。

Abstract: Quantum channel tomography (also known as quantum process tomography) is a fundamental task in quantum information and quantum computing, aiming to learn the full description of an unknown quantum channel from black-box queries. For channels with input dimension $d_1$, output dimension $d_2$, and Kraus rank at most $r$, [CGO+26] identifies the dilation rate $τ=rd_2/d_1\geq 1$ as a key parameter governing a Heisenberg-to-classical transition in query complexity. While the optimal bounds in the boundary regime ($τ=1$) and the away-from-boundary regime ($τ\geq 1+Ω(1)$) are now known, the precise behavior in the near-boundary regime ($1<τ<1+o(1)$) has remained unresolved.
  In this paper, we close this gap, thereby fully settling the query complexity of quantum channel tomography. Specifically, we show that the query complexity is
  - $Ω(rd_1d_2/\varepsilon^2)$ for diamond norm error $\varepsilon$, matching the upper bound by [MB26, CGO+26],
  - $Θ(d_1^2/\varepsilon+\min\{(τ-1)d_1^2,(τ-1)^2d_1^2d_2^2\}/\varepsilon^2)$ for Choi trace norm error $\varepsilon$.
  Our results reveal two qualitatively different forms of the Heisenberg-to-classical transition. For diamond norm tomography, the Heisenberg scaling disappears suddenly as soon as $τ$ leaves the boundary regime. In contrast, Choi trace norm tomography exhibits a gradual transition from Heisenberg to classical scaling as $τ$ moves from the boundary regime to the away-from-boundary regime.

</details>

### [98] [The Agentic AI Kerr Lab: LLM Agents for Kerr Microscopy Experiments with Magnetic Skyrmions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00299)
*Kilian Leutner,Yuean Zhou,Aditya Kumar,Thomas B. Winkler,Robert Frömter,Hans Fangohr,Mathias Kläui*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出基于大语言模型的智能体系统AgentKerr，自动在克尔显微镜上完成磁斯格明子的成像—分析—激励闭环，由CNN将图像转为可观测物理量供智能体决策，并以确定性动作策略保障硬件操作安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统磁光克尔成像研究依赖"成像—分析—激励"的串行循环，每一步都需实验人员判断，效率低、无法自动化；而直接让语言模型做图像模式识别又不可靠、难以保证硬件安全。

Method: 构建Agentic AI Kerr Lab：用训练于克尔图像的卷积神经网络把实时图像转换为斯格明子的位置、尺寸、形状等可观测量；语言模型智能体基于这些量自主决策与控制；所有硬件动作经独立于智能体的确定性动作策略校验，确保安全；智能体在无给定流程条件下自行探索实验策略并编写分析脚本。

Result: 在无人给出实验流程的情况下，智能体自主确定如何产生、调节与移动斯格明子，并自行撰写分析代码，实现高效自动化的实时成像实验闭环。

Conclusion: 该架构可推广至其他自旋结构，指向一种新范式：研究者提出问题，智能体自主设计并执行实验以给出答案。

Abstract: Magnetic skyrmions are topological spin textures that can be observed directly by magnetic microscopy and driven by fields and currents, which makes them a model system for fundamental physics and candidates for spintronic devices. Magneto-optical Kerr microscopy is a method to image them in real time; however, most experiments use an inefficient cycle of consecutive imaging, analysis and excitation in which every step requires the judgement of the experimentalist operating the setup. Here we present the Agentic AI Kerr Lab (AgentKerr), in which an agent based on a large language model operates this cycle automatically and efficiently on a Kerr microscope. Rather than relying on pattern detection by a language model, a convolutional neural network trained on Kerr images turns live images into skyrmion observables such as position, size and shape, on which the agent bases its decisions. The architecture lets agents operate the instruments safely, as every hardware action is checked deterministically against an action policy, independently of the agent that issues it. With no procedure given, the agent determines by itself how to create, tune and move skyrmions, and writes its own analyses. The approach can be transferred to other spin textures and points towards magnetic imaging experiments in which the researcher poses the question and the agent finds the way to realize the experiments that yield the answer.

</details>


### [99] [Quantum Dipolar Magnon Drag in Conducting Magnetic Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00320)
*Ling Gan,Shufeng Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文预测两个电绝缘隔离的金属铁磁体之间可由量子偶极磁振子耦合介导产生电拖曳效应，并在被动层中输出可测电压。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拖曳机制（如声子拖曳、库仑拖曳）难以在同一体系中同时实现共振动量转移与磁学可调性，作者希望寻找一种可由磁化构型调控、具有量子特征的拖曳通道。

Method: 理论预测：主动层中的电荷流激发非平衡磁振子流，其动量通过层间偶极相互作用穿过绝缘间隔层转移至被动层；被动层中的磁振子流经电子-磁振子散射转化为可测电压。

Result: 发现共振量子动量转移与通过控制磁化构型实现的磁学可调性两项特征，可用于将量子偶极拖曳与常规拖曳机制区分开。

Conclusion: 量子偶极磁振子拖曳构成一种可磁调控、具共振特征的新型拖曳机制，为铁磁异质结构中的非局域输运与磁振子学提供可检验的实验判据。

Abstract: We predict electrical drag between two electrically isolated metallic ferromagnets mediated by quantum dipolar magnon coupling. A charge current in one layer generates a nonequilibrium magnon current whose momentum is transferred across an insulating spacer through interlayer dipolar interactions. The resulting magnon current in the passive layer produces a measurable voltage through electron-magnon scattering. We show the combination of resonant quantum momentum transfer and magnetic tunability through the control of magnetization configurations distinguish quantum dipolar drag from conventional drag mechanism.

</details>


### [100] [Helical Magnetic Nature of $β$-Mn Structured CoZn Thin-Films Determined by a Bayesian Approach to Modeling Polarized Neutron Reflectivity Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00390)
*M. Dearg,A. J. Caruana,C. J. Kinane,N. Satchell,G. Burnell,S. Langridge,C. H. Marrows*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作用极化中子反射(PNR)结合贝叶斯模型选择方法，证明100 nm厚(约半个螺旋周期)的β-Mn结构CoZn溅射薄膜中仍保留螺旋磁序，并绘制了其磁场-温度相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性磁体可承载斯格明子，是低功耗自旋电子学存储的候选材料，但实际应用要求室温工作和薄膜形态；因此需要确认薄膜中手性磁序(螺旋态)是否存在，并建立其磁场-温度相图。而区分螺旋态与"界面磁性减弱的均匀态"极为困难。

Method: 对居里温度约130 °C的β-Mn结构CoZn溅射薄膜开展极化中子反射测量；开发并采用贝叶斯方法，通过模型选择对PNR数据进行分析，进而映射薄膜的磁场-温度相图。

Result: PNR数据分析表明，螺旋磁序在仅100 nm厚(约为一个螺旋周期的一半)的CoZn薄膜中依然存在；并据此给出了该薄膜的磁场-温度相图。

Conclusion: 手性螺旋磁序可在接近室温居里温度、亚螺旋周期厚度的CoZn薄膜中保持，为基于斯格明子的低功耗存储应用提供了材料层面的依据；同时表明贝叶斯模型选择是解析PNR数据、判别微弱手性磁信号的有效手段。

Abstract: Chiral magnetic materials can host skyrmions and hence are of interest for numerous spintronic applications including low-power storage. To reach this goal, room temperature operation and a thin film form are needed. We have studied the chiral magnetism in a $β$-Mn structured CoZn sputtered film, with a Curie temperature of $\approx 130$ °C, by means of polarized neutron reflectometry (PNR) in order to reveal its magnetic field-temperature phase diagram. In bulk this material has a helimagnetic ground state. Here we use PNR data to show that the helimagnetism survives in a CoZn film only 100 nm thick, roughly half a helical period. Given the subtlety of differentiating the helical state from a uniform state with reduced interfacial magnetism, we have developed and employed a Bayesian approach that uses model selection to analyze our PNR data and to map out the field-temperature phase diagram of our thin film.

</details>


### [101] [Validation of the five-electron pO qubit in Si using full configuration interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00443)
*David W. Kanaar,John H. Caporaletti,J. P. Kestner,Christopher R. Anderson,Mark F. Gyure*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用FCI计算评估库仑相互作用对Si量子点中五电子pO电荷量子比特可行性的影响，发现尽管本征态偏离壳层填充图像，但当谷劈裂E_VS>50 μeV时，量子比特态间零偶极矩与非零四极矩等核心特性依然保持，且零偶极矩由五电子哈密顿量的对称性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: pO量子比特（五个电子局域于栅极定义的Si量子点）因两个量子比特态无偶极矩、仅通过四极矩耦合电场，从而抑制了传统电荷量子比特的主要退相干通道，同时保留全电学操控能力。但原始方案基于无库仑相互作用的壳层填充图像，需检验库仑相互作用对其可行性的影响。

Method: 采用全组态相互作用（FCI）方法进行计算，考察库仑相互作用对pO量子比特本征态轨道占据、简并结构及量子比特相关矩阵元的影响，并借助五电子哈密顿量的对称性分析偶极矩消失的成因。

Result: 相互作用本征态占据壳层填充图像之外的轨道壳层，激发程度由谷劈裂、轨道能与库仑能标度之间的竞争决定；基态形成两类不同的简并双重态：在谷劈裂E_VS≈50 μeV的交叉点以上，双重态为低能谷中|⟨L_z⟩|=1的轨道角动量激发对，以下则为⟨L_z⟩=0的单谷激发对。尽管显著偏离壳层填充图像，当E_VS>50 μeV时，量子比特态间偶极矩消失与非零四极矩张量等本质特征仍得以保持；零偶极矩由五电子哈密顿量的对称性质严格保证。控制轨道占据的相同能标也决定四极矩张量元与第一激发态能隙，二者原则上可通过器件工程调控。

Conclusion: 库仑相互作用虽使pO量子比特的本征态明显偏离无相互作用的壳层填充图像并改变基态简并结构，但在足够大的谷劈裂（>≈50 μeV）下，其零偶极矩与四极矩耦合等关键优势得以保持，且相关参数可由能标竞争与器件工程调节，支持该量子比特方案的可行性。

Abstract: The $pO$ qubit is a recently proposed charge qubit consisting of five electrons confined in a gate-defined Si quantum dot. Because its two qubit states have no dipole moment, it couples to electric fields only through the quadrupole moment, suppressing the dominant decoherence channel of conventional charge qubits while retaining all-electrical control. The original proposal was formulated in a shell-filling picture without Coulomb interactions. In this work we perform full configuration interaction (FCI) calculations to assess the impact of the Coulomb interaction on the viability of the $pO$ qubit. We find that the interacting eigenstates occupy orbital shells beyond the shell-filling picture, with the degree of excitation governed by the competition between valley splitting, orbital energy, and Coulomb energy scales. Additionally, we find that the ground states form degenerate doublets, but of two distinct kinds. Above a crossover near a valley splitting of $E_{\rm{VS}}\approx50~μ$eV the doublet is a pair of orbital angular momentum excitations with $|\langle L_z\rangle|=1$ in the lower valley, while below the crossover the doublet is a pair of single valley excitations with $\langle L_z\rangle=0$. Despite this substantial departure from the shell-filling picture, for $E_{\rm{VS}} > 50~μ$eV the essential features, vanishing dipole moments and nonzero quadrupole tensor between qubit states are preserved. We show that the vanishing dipole is in fact guaranteed by the symmetry properties of the five-electron Hamiltonian. The same energy scales that govern the orbital occupation also set the quadrupole tensor elements and the first excited state energy gap, both of which are in principle tunable through device engineering.

</details>


### [102] [Physics-informed X-ray tomography of magnetization and material parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00528)
*Alexander Setescak,Polly Mitchell,Luke J. R. Higgins,Burkhard Kaulich,Luke Turnbull,Rikako Yamamoto,Marina Raboni Ferreira,Daniel A. Mayoh,Geetha Balakrishnan,Anuj Chavan,Dieter Suess,Amalio Fernández-Pacheco,Manuel Guizar-Sicairos,Claire Donnelly,Claas Abert*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出基于微磁能量约束的重建方法，可从少量（甚至单轴）X射线矢量层析投影中恢复三维磁化分布，并反演材料中交换作用与Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）之比值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线矢量层析可成像纳米尺度三维自旋织构，但磁衬度弱、可视角范围有限，导致磁化部分分量欠定；同时希望从成像数据中直接获取决定织构稳定性的微观相互作用信息。

Method: 在重建中引入微磁能量泛函作为正则化/物理约束，对欠采样投影数据进行三维磁化重建；以手性磁体Co8Zn9Mn3为对象，通过测试不同交换-DMI比值下重建结果对实测投影的再现程度，反演该比值。

Result: 仅用双轴投影的很小一部分，或仅用单一倾转轴数据，即可解析出三维螺旋织构；反演得到的交换/DMI比值与由螺旋周期推导的文献值相差约10%。

Conclusion: 物理约束的重建可显著缩短矢量层析所需测量时间，并将层析成像拓展为定量确定材料基本磁相互作用的手段。

Abstract: Three-dimensional spin textures are stabilized by microscopic interactions within their host materials. X-ray vector tomography can image these nanoscale structures, but weak magnetic contrast and a limited range of viewing angles often leave parts of the magnetization poorly determined. Here we show that a reconstruction constrained by the micromagnetic energy recovers the three-dimensional magnetization from such data and gains sensitivity to the underlying material interactions. Applied to the chiral magnet Co$_8$Zn$_9$Mn$_3$, the method resolves the three-dimensional helical texture even from a small fraction of the dual-axis projections typically required for a successful tomographic reconstruction, or from a single tilt axis alone. By testing which ratio of exchange to Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction in the reconstruction best reproduces the measured projections, we obtain a value for the sample that differs by about 10 % from the literature value derived from the helical winding period. Taken together, our findings suggest a way to reduce the time needed for vector tomography and to extend it to determining fundamental magnetic interactions in a material.

</details>


### [103] [Quantized valley source in bilayer graphene via adiabatic quantum pumping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00733)
*Kai-Tong Wang,Lei Zhang,Bin Wang,Yunjin Yu,Fuming Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出基于双层石墨烯中绝热量子泵浦的量子化谷源，利用螺旋边缘态产生纯谷电流，在特定量子点几何下谷对数目呈量子化平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索电学可控的量子化谷源方案，利用谷自由度作为信息载体，需要在同时存在量子自旋霍尔与量子谷霍尔效应的体系中实现纯谷电流的可控、量子化发射。

Method: 在双层石墨烯纳米带旁耦合两个量子点，周期性调制量子点栅压进行绝热量子泵浦；依托螺旋边缘态产生纯谷电流。通过静态栅压、量子点-纳米带耦合强度调控泵浦行为，并考察安德森无序及锯齿型/扶手椅型量子点边界的影响。

Result: 取决于量子点几何构型，发射的谷对数目或形成量子化平台，或随泵浦幅度连续变化；平台结构可由静态栅压与量子点-纳米带耦合调节；量子化谷对在量子点内安德森无序下保持鲁棒，且对锯齿型与扶手椅型边界均成立。

Conclusion: 绝热量子泵浦为电学可控的量子化谷源提供了一条可行途径。

Abstract: We propose a quantized valley source based on adiabatic quantum pumping in bilayer graphene in the presence of both quantum spin Hall and quantum valley Hall effects. By periodically modulating the gate voltages of two quantum dots coupled to a bilayer graphene nanoribbon, the helical edge states enable the generation of a pure valley current. Depending on the geometry of quantum dots, the number of emitted valley pairs either forms quantized plateaus or varies continuously with the pumping amplitude. The quantized plateau structure can be tuned by the static gate voltage and by the coupling between the quantum dots and the ribbon. Quantized valley pairs are robust against Anderson disorder applied in the quantum dots and persist for both zigzag and armchair quantum-dot boundaries. These results demonstrate that adiabatic quantum pumping provides a route toward electrically controllable quantized valley sources.

</details>


### [104] [High-order concentric hopfions in a helimagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00858)
*Konstantin L. Metlov,Maksim M. Gordei*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文构建并求解了一类新型“同心”磁霍普夫子（concentric magnetic hopfions）的解析模型，给出其尺寸与平衡能估计，刻画其在螺旋磁体相图中的稳定区，并确定高霍普夫指数同心霍普夫子可在均匀磁化背景上平衡存在所需的材料条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁霍普夫子是磁化矢量场中的三维拓扑孤子（“结”），是新兴三维自旋电子学的关键要素；而关于新型同心磁霍普夫子的简单解析描述尚缺，亟需理论模型以指导材料设计。

Method: 采用解析建模方法：构造同心磁霍普夫子的简化解析模型并求解，据此估算其尺寸与平衡能量，并在螺旋磁体（helimagnet）的磁相图上标定其稳定区域。

Result: 获得了同心霍普夫子的尺寸与平衡能估计值，绘制出其在螺旋磁体磁相图中的稳定区间，并给出实现均匀磁化背景上高霍普夫指数平衡同心霍普夫子所需的材料参数要求。

Conclusion: 同心磁霍普夫子可作为一类可解析描述的三维拓扑孤子存在，其稳定存在依赖于材料参数所满足的特定条件；该模型为三维自旋电子学中拓扑结构的理论预测与材料筛选提供了简洁依据。

Abstract: Magnetic hopfions are three-dimensional topological solitons: ``knots'' in the vector field of magnetization of magnetically ordered materials, which are a key element of the three-dimensional spintronics emerging today. In this work, a simple analytical model for a new type of magnetic hopfions --- ``concentric'' ones --- is constructed and solved. Their size and equilibrium energy are estimated, their stability region on the magnetic phase diagram of a helimagnet is mapped out, and the requirements for materials admitting equilibrium concentric hopfions with high Hopf index on a uniformly magnetized background are determined.

</details>


### [105] [Excited-state-induced nonlinearity and polarity reversals in the thermocurrent through a quantum dot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01006)
*Parvathy Gireesan,Giorgio Biasiol,Madhu Thalakulam*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 单GaAs/AlGaAs量子点在源极白噪声加热下，热电流随源温升高出现附加极值并发展为极性反转；两级Pauli主方程模型表明这是激发态作为第二输运通道与基态竞争、并在慢弛豫与强激发态耦合共同作用下产生的，从而确立热电流线型为探测激发态隧穿与弛豫的手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子点热电器件在大热偏置下热电输运呈现附加极值与热电流极性反转等复杂行为，这些不对称性与非线性直接影响整流与功率转换，但其起源尚未被系统研究。

Method: 对源极经白噪声激发加热的单GaAs/AlGaAs量子点进行测量与建模；采用保留两引线完整费米分布、两级隧穿速率不同并含级间弛豫的Pauli主方程，描述两级点的顺序隧穿。

Result: 激发态充当与基态贡献竞争的第二输运通道；强激发态耦合叠加慢弛豫产生热电流极性反转，二者缺一时激发态仅给出附加极值与跨电荷简并点的幅度不对称。实验上源温升高时热电流先出现附加极值并发展为极性反转；利用脉冲栅与偏压谱独立获得的激发态能级间距与基态隧穿速率，模型将观测极值位置与幅度复现至约三倍因子以内，表明器件处于慢弛豫与强激发态耦合区。

Conclusion: 热电流线型可作为量子点中激发态隧穿与弛豫的探针；常被视为次要修正的激发态，在远离平衡时可主导量子点热电输运。

Abstract: Quantum dots are electrically tuneable thermal machines capable of both energy harvesting and cooling at the nanoscale in the cryogenic regime. At large thermal biases, their thermoelectric transport shows a complex behaviour, including additional extrema and polarity reversals in the thermocurrent. Though these asymmetries and nonlinearities directly affect rectification and power conversion, their origin has not been studied systematically. We address this through measurements and modelling of a single GaAs/AlGaAs quantum dot whose source is heated by white-noise excitation. Using Pauli master equation for sequential tunnelling through a two-level dot, retaining the full Fermi distributions of both leads, distinct tunnel rates for the two levels, and relaxation between them, we show that the excited state acts as a second transport channel which competes with the ground-state contribution. Strong excited-state coupling combined with slow relaxation produces polarity reversals in the thermocurrent; without both, the excited state yields only additional extrema and an amplitude asymmetry across the charge degeneracy point. Experimentally, as the source temperature increases, an additional extremum emerges in the thermocurrent which develops into a polarity reversal. Using the excited-state level spacing and ground-state tunnel rate obtained independently from pulsed-gate and bias spectroscopies, the model reproduces the positions of the observed extrema and their amplitudes to within a factor of about three, placing the device in the regime of slow relaxation and strong excited-state coupling. Our results establish thermocurrent line-shape as a probe of excited-state tunnelling and relaxation in quantum dots. Excited states, often treated as a minor correction, can dominate quantum-dot thermoelectric transport far from equilibrium.

</details>


### [106] [Phase-Tunable Liouvillian Topology: Dual Winding Structures and Skin Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01110)
*Daksh K. Sambhare,Koustav Roy,Saurabh Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 即使在位点间粒子交换严格平衡的条件下也可出现方向性边缘局域（Liouvillian 趋肤效应），由单一可调相位 α 决定方向、双重绕转结构决定类型：最近邻跃迁算符导致单极趋肤效应，次近邻跃迁算符导致双极趋肤效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为 Liouvillian 趋肤效应源自耗散速率偏向单向流动；本文旨在表明，方向性边缘局域并不需要非平衡耗散，平衡的保数目热库（以本征态选择著称）中的相位参数即可驱动稳态电流并产生边缘局域。

Method: 以密度矩阵的左（ket）与右（bra）矢量指标分别独立扭转，研究谱绕转结构及其由反幺正镜面对称性决定的关系；比较最近邻与次近邻跃迁算符对该对称性的破缺情况；并在二聚化链中分析稳态零模与弛豫时间。

Result: 最近邻跃迁算符破缺镜面对称性，两种绕转重合为共同谱绕转 W₀(α)=±1，产生密度堆积于特定边缘的单极趋肤效应；次近邻跃迁保持对称性，两种绕转相反，ket 与 bra 被推向相反边缘，形成相干性的双极趋肤效应，而稳态密度无边缘局域。二聚化链中相位调控的密度趋肤效应使稳态零模在趋肤边缘具有与尺寸无关的权重，并在拓扑转变处伴随扭结；弛豫时间随系统尺寸线性增长，但在使跃迁算符变为厄米的特定 α 处，具趋肤特征的边缘局域本征模与扩散型弛豫共存。

Conclusion: Liouvillian 趋肤效应可在无净耗散偏置的平衡热库中由相位 α 与跃迁程长调控，谱绕转的对称关系决定单极/双极局域类型；相位依赖的弛豫时间与拓扑扭结提示可调控的非厄米稳态输运与拓扑关联。

Abstract: Liouvillian skin effects are usually associated with dissipation whose rates favor unidirectional flow. In this work, we show that directional edge localization can emerge even when population exchange between sites is exactly balanced, with a single tunable phase parameter $α$ deciding their direction and a dual winding structure deciding their nature. Although this number-conserving bath is known for eigenstate selection, we demonstrate here that its phase controls a steady current, which Hamiltonian hopping converts to directional edge localization. Further, we study the spectral winding structure by independently twisting the left (ket) and right (bra) vector indices of the density matrix, revealing two distinct windings whose relationship is dictated by an antiunitary mirror symmetry. Nearest-neighbor jump operators break this symmetry: both windings coincide, giving rise to a common spectral winding $W_0(α)=\pm1$, producing a unipolar skin effect in which the density accumulates at a specific edge. However, next nearest neighbor jump preserve it. The two windings are forced to be opposite, and the ket and bra are pushed to opposite edges, giving a bipolar skin effect of coherences present in eigenmodes, without edge localization of the density in the steady state. Remarkably, in a dimerized chain the phase-controlled density skin effect gives the steady-state zero modes a size-independent weight at the skin edge, accompanied by a kink at the topological transition. In addition, the relaxation time grows linearly with the system size, however at specific phases $α$ where the jump operators are rendered Hermitian, edge-localized eigenmodes with skin character coexist with diffusive relaxation.

</details>


### [107] [Visualizing Anisotropic Charge Transport in CrSBr using Bias-Dependent Kelvin Probe Force Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01217)
*Satyam Sahu,Arijit Kayal,Bing Wu,Aljoscha Söll,Zdeněk Sofer,Matěj Velický,Otakar Frank*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用偏压依赖的KPFM直接成像CrSBr在不同偏压构型下的各向异性电势分布，并结合各向异性输运模拟统一解释电荷输运与偏压诱导的电势再分布，揭示沿低电导a轴施加偏压时输运场的边界相关再分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性半导体因方向依赖的输运特性为下一代电子学提供新机遇，但传统电学测量仅给出空间平均特性，无法分辨局域的各向异性输运行为，缺乏对低对称量子材料中局域输运现象的直接观测手段。

Method: 采用偏压依赖的Kelvin探针力显微镜（KPFM）对CrSBr在不同偏压构型下的响应进行空间分辨成像；同时构建各向异性输运模拟，重现CrSBr中的电荷输运与电场分布，并与实验映射结果对照。

Result: KPFM图显示高度明显的、依赖晶向的电势分布；实验与模拟高度吻合，给出各向异性电荷输运与偏压诱导电势再分布的统一图像；沿a轴（低电导方向）加偏压时观察到输运场与晶界相关的再分布。

Conclusion: 偏压依赖KPFM可作为揭示低对称量子材料中局域输运现象的有力平台，并提示晶界在低电导方向电荷输运中可能起作用。

Abstract: Anisotropic semiconductors offer unique opportunities for next-generation electronics due to their direction-dependent transport. However, conventional electrical measurements provide only spatially averaged characteristics. Here, we employ bias-dependent Kelvin probe force microscopy (KPFM) to directly visualize the response of CrSBr under different electrical bias configurations. Spatially resolved KPFM maps reveal highly distinct, crystallographic-direction-dependent potential profiles. To elucidate this behavior, we employ anisotropic transport simulations that capture the charge transport and electric-field distributions in CrSBr. The close agreement between experiment and simulation provides a unified picture of anisotropic charge transport and bias-induced potential redistribution. Our experiments further reveal a boundary-associated redistribution of the transport field when bias is applied along the $a$-axis, suggesting a possible role of crystal boundaries in charge transport along the low-conductivity direction. This work establishes bias-dependent KPFM as a powerful platform for uncovering localized transport phenomena in low-symmetry quantum materials.

</details>


### [108] [Artificial spin ice on the CaVO lattice: a tricritical bridge from square to kagome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01397)
*F. Museur,G. M. Macauley,S. Nilsson,V. Scagnoli,P. M. Derlet,L. J. Heyderman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究CaVO晶格人工自旋冰，通过调节正方形/八边形键长比r实现从类方冰到类kagome的连续相行为，并给出多临界区、磁测协议与可调二聚体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有ASIs热力学研究多集中于方形与kagome晶格及其变体，缺乏仅靠几何调控（而非极端实验条件）获得丰富相图的手段；本文希望在同一晶格常数下通过几何参数微调实现相互作用强度的强调节。

Method: 在由正方形与八边形构成的CaVO晶格上构建人工自旋冰；固定晶格常数，改变正方形与八边形边长比$r$以在几何近不变条件下强调节相互作用；结合磁力显微镜(MFM)成像与Monte Carlo模拟；并用八边形环向磁矩的Blume-Capel模型刻画多临界区；据此设计对比磁测协议，并构建可调二聚体模型分析电荷有序区。

Result: 观察到从类方冰到类kagome行为的连续过渡：小$r$时系统只有单一有序转变；大$r$时经历从关联顺磁体→电荷有序→长程有序的多步转变；两区间在多临界区交汇，可由Blume-Capel模型准确描述。所设计的磁测协议能在热激活样品中明确区分相互作用效应与单个纳磁体效应；电荷有序区映射为可调二聚体模型，其两条有序二聚体相之间存在Coulomb相与pinch points。

Conclusion: CaVO人工自旋冰仅通过几何调控即可获得丰富相图，无需极端实验条件；为ASIs热力学研究提供了新的可调平台与可分离相互作用/单粒子效应的实验方法。

Abstract: Artificial spin ices (ASIs) are arrays of interacting nanomagnets in which phase transitions can be imaged and controlled. Studies of the thermodynamic have focused mainly on the square and kagome lattices, and on their variants. Here we study artificial spin ice on the CaVO lattice, formed of squares and octagons. At fixed lattice constant, the ratio $r$ of the square and octagon link lengths tunes the interactions strongly while changing the geometry only slightly. Magnetic force microscopy and Monte Carlo simulations show a continuous bridge from square-ice-like to kagome-like behavior. At small $r$ the system has a single ordering transition, whereas at large $r$ it passes from a correlated paramagnet to charge order and then long-range order. The two regimes meet in a multicritical region, accurately modeled by a Blume-Capel model of the octagon toroidal moments. From this picture, we design a comparative magnetometry protocol that unambiguously separates interaction effects from single-nanomagnet effects in thermally active samples. Finally, the charge-ordered region maps onto a tunable dimer model, with a Coulomb phase and pinch points along the line between two ordered dimer phases. The CaVO ASI thus provides access to a rich phase diagram only through geometric tuning, without requiring extreme experimental conditions.

</details>


### [109] [Beyond-RTA magnetoconductivity of multifold semimetals under generic orientations of electromagnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01398)
*Ipsita Mandal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文将线性化半经典玻尔兹曼方程（SBE）推广到任意取向电磁场下多重简并半金属的精确求解框架，摆脱弛豫时间近似，将Berry曲率、轨道磁矩、洛伦兹力算符与散射项同等处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对多重简并半金属磁电电导的处理依赖弛豫时间近似、不完整的试探ansatz以及对洛伦兹力算符的不完整处理，难以准确刻画拓扑性质、倾斜与带间/带内散射的贡献，故需一个超越这些近似的精确可解框架。

Method: 求解线性化半经典玻尔兹曼方程的精确解；散射振幅与动量无关，通过带内、带间与带间谷（intranode、interband、internode）过程耦合费米面各片；散射核为有限秩，SBE约化为少数矩的线性方程组，并由电子数守恒约束封闭。应用于含二次修正的三重简并点、Weyl点与Rarita-Schwinger-Weyl点共四类体系（含倾斜三重简并点），给出零场及磁场领头阶的解析结果与全磁场依赖的数值解。

Result: 获得零场和磁场领头阶的解析电导结果，以及覆盖全磁场范围的数值解；定量阐明拓扑性质、洛伦兹力算符、倾斜以及带内/带间谷散射在塑造磁电电导中的作用。

Conclusion: 该框架修正了此前处理中试探ansatz不完整、洛伦兹力算符处理不完整等严重缺陷，为多重简并半金属磁电输运提供了严格且可解析处理的统一途径。

Abstract: We present an exact solution of the linearised semiclassical Boltzmann equation (SBE) beyond the relaxation-time approximation for multifold semimetals under arbitrarily oriented electric and magnetic fields. The framework treats the Berry curvature, orbital magnetic moment, Lorentz-force operator, and scattering terms on equal footing. Momentum-independent scattering-amplitudes couple the Fermi-surface sheets through intranode, interband, and internode processes. The scattering kernel has a finite rank and the SBE reduces to a linear system for a few moments, which is closed by the electron-number constraint. We apply this approach to four systems involving quadratic-corrected triple-point, Weyl, and Rarita-Schwinger-Weyl nodes, including tilted triple-point nodes. We obtain analytical results at zero field and to leading orders in the magnetic field, alongside numerical solutions for the full field dependence. Our results elucidate the roles of the topological properties, Lorentz-force operator, tilt, and intra- and internode scatterings in shaping the magnetoelectric conductivity. It cures severe shortcomings of previous treatments like incomplete ansätze and incomplete treatment of the Lorentz-force operator.

</details>


### [110] [Plasmon modes in three-dimensional multifold semimetals. II. Rarita-Schwinger-Weyl and Kramers-Weyl nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01401)
*Ipsita Mandal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在随机相位近似下系统研究Rarita-Schwinger-Weyl与Kramers-Weyl半金属中多同心费米面的集体电荷激发，包括密度响应、等离激元、Landau阻尼与Friedel振荡，并与伴随工作比较以厘清能带色散与本征态的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维multifold半金属在正化学势下存在多个同心费米面，其集体电荷动力学如何在能带色散与本征态共同作用下形成，尚缺乏系统认识；作者希望厘清色散关系和本征态各自所起的独特作用。

Method: 在随机相位近似（RPA）框架内计算密度响应函数、Drude权重、介电函数、等离激元色散与其Landau阻尼，以及Friedel振荡；同时处理小动量转移与任意动量转移，并针对赝自旋-3/2 Rarita-Schwinger-Weyl与Kramers-Weyl半金属进行对比，另与其伴随工作（赝自旋-1三重简并点、Hopf与Weyl半金属）横向比较。

Result: 获得了上述两类体系的完整电荷响应与介电描述，给出等离激元的色散、阻尼行为与Friedel振荡特征，并显示多同心费米面及本征态结构会显著改变集体激发谱；跨体系比较表明能带色散与本征态对集体动力学贡献不同。

Conclusion: 集体电荷激发可用来区分具有多重费米面的multifold节点，并揭示单粒子色散之外的、编码于本征态中的信息。

Abstract: We investigate collective charge excitations in three-dimensional multifold semimetals in which multiple bands cross the Fermi level at a positive chemical potential, focusing on pseudospin-3/2 Rarita-Schwinger-Weyl semimetals and on Kramers-Weyl semimetals. Both systems host multiple concentric Fermi surfaces at positive chemical potential. Within the random-phase approximation, we analyse the density response, Drude weights, dielectric function, plasmon modes and their Landau damping, and Friedel oscillations, considering both small and arbitrary momentum transfers. In our companion work, arXiv:2610.18629, we investigated pseudospin-1 triple-point semimetals, with and without a quadratic correction to their dispersion, as well as Hopf and Weyl semimetals. Comparing these systems allows us to elucidate the distinct roles of band dispersions and eigenstates in shaping collective charge dynamics. Our results demonstrate that collective charge excitations can distinguish multifold nodes with multiple Fermi surfaces and reveal information encoded in their eigenstates beyond that contained in the single-particle dispersion.

</details>


### [111] [Superradiance fingerprints of moiré exciton-biexciton condensates: path-entangled biphoton vs. supercurrent-enabled one-photon channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01515)
*Haochen Wang,Shengxian Li,Hongyu Lu,Mingxing Chen,Wang Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文揭示了扭转MoTe₂双层中杂化莫尔激子凝聚体的协同辐射特征：基态激子在单个莫尔势阱中因层杂化选择定则表现为携带轨道角动量的长寿命量子发射体；凝聚后出现激子型与双激子型BEC共存，分别通过超电流驱动的超辐射和路径纠缠双光子超辐射发光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂化莫尔激子兼具可电调单光子发射阵列与强偶极-偶极相互作用，可构建Bose-Hubbard量子模拟器以研究BEC等关联物态；但其凝聚体的协同光学响应（辐射机制、发光特性）尚缺乏系统刻画，尤其层杂化选择定则与多体凝聚如何共同决定发射行为不清楚。

Method: 以扭转MoTe₂双层为平台，基于层杂化选择定则分析单个莫尔势阱中基态激子的轨道角动量光子发射性质与辐射寿命；结合子晶格间偶极吸引与子晶格内排斥的相互作用模型，研究激子与双激子BEC的共存条件及权重；进而推导两类凝聚体的辐射通道，包括双激子（分子型）BEC的路径纠缠双光子超辐射与激子（原子型）BEC在静止时暗、载流时超辐射的行为。

Result: 单阱基态激子为发射轨道角动量光子的量子发射体，辐射寿命异常长，仅当形成凝聚后才显著发光；子晶格间吸引与子晶格内排斥导致激子BEC与双激子BEC以可比权重共存；双激子BEC经路径纠缠双光子超辐射衰变，激子BEC静止时暗、携带超电流时呈超辐射。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了莫尔激子凝聚体中原子型与分子型BEC共存的独特协同辐射图景，既提供了一种新型纠缠光源，也为以莫尔激子探索原子-分子凝聚体的"冷化学"提供了独特指纹。

Abstract: Hybrid moiré excitons form a dense array of electrically tunable single-photon emitters, entailing cooperative optical responses with intriguing consequences. With the strong dipole-dipole interaction, they also realize Bose-Hubbard quantum simulators of emergent correlated matter such as Bose-Einstein condensates (BEC). Here we reveal the characteristic cooperative radiation from condensates of hybrid moiré excitons in twisted MoTe$_2$ bilayers. In each individual moiré trap, the layer hybridization selection rule dictates that the ground-state exciton is a quantum emitter of orbital-angular-momentum photons, with an exceptionally long radiative lifetime. Ground-state emission becomes pronounced only upon forming condensates, where inter-sublattice dipole attraction and intra-sublattice repulsion together lead to the coexistence of exciton and biexciton BEC with comparable weights. The biexciton (molecular-type) BEC decays through superradiance of path-entangled biphotons, whereas the exciton (atomic-type) BEC is dark at rest and becomes superradiant when it carries a supercurrent. Our findings not only uncover a novel source of entangled light but also establish unique fingerprints for exploring the cold chemistry of atomic-molecular condensates with moiré excitons.

</details>


### [112] [Multipolar Anisotropy of Magnonic Orbital Pumping in $d$-Wave Altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01848)
*Myeonil Choi,Guidobeth Sáez,Daegeun Jo,Yuriy Mokrousov,Hyun-Woo Lee,Kyoung-Whan Kim,Dongwook Go*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在d波交错磁体（altermagnet）中，反铁磁磁振子可驱动轨道泵浦，其轨道响应对Néel矢量方向呈显著的d波角度依赖，各向异性远大于自旋泵浦，并可作为一种直接动力学特征用于探测d波交错磁序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋泵浦已在交错磁体中被广泛研究，而磁振子驱动的轨道泵浦及其各向异性尚不清楚；作者希望揭示交错磁体中轨道响应与局域晶体场诱导的电四极矩的直接耦合，从而寻找区别于自旋泵浦的、可探测d波交错磁序的动力学特征。

Method: 基于交错磁体中d波反铁磁磁振子的理论分析，结合对称性分析，计算轨道泵浦对Néel矢量取向的角度依赖；并将轨道响应与由交错局域晶体场产生的电四极矩（多极矩）耦合联系起来，分别讨论热激发磁振子（两种相反手性）与相干磁振子的情形。

Result: 轨道泵浦呈现d波角度依赖，且各向异性一般远大于自旋泵浦；该差异源于轨道响应与电四极矩的直接耦合。对称性分析表明，对两种相反手性的反铁磁磁振子，该各向异性并不相互抵消，因此即使仅靠热激发磁振子也可观测到该效应；进一步分析了相干磁振子情形下的手性依赖。

Conclusion: 轨道泵浦的多极各向异性可作为d波交错磁序的直接动力学特征，凸显交错磁体作为磁振子驱动轨道电子学平台以及研究磁振子与电多极矩相互作用的潜力。

Abstract: We show that antiferromagnetic magnons in $d$-wave altermagnets lead to orbital pumping with distinctive $d$-wave angular dependence on the direction of the Néel vector. We find that the anisotropy is generally much larger than that of spin pumping. We elucidate that this difference originates from the direct coupling of the orbital response to the electrical quadrupole moments induced by staggered local crystal field in an altermagnet. By symmetry analysis, we show that the anisotropy does not cancel for the two opposite-handedness antiferromagnetic magnons, suggesting that our prediction can be observed even with thermally excited magnons. We further analyze the handedness dependence for the situation with coherent magnons. These results establish multipolar anisotropy of orbital pumping as a direct dynamical signature of $d$-wave altermagnetic order and highlight the potential of altermagnets as a platform for magnon-driven orbitronics as well as for the interplay between magnons and electrical multipoles.

</details>


### [113] [Fractional quantum Hall effect at ν=1/2 in wide GaAs quantum wells: Subband structure, exact diagonalization, and the magnitude of the gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01885)
*Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: The authors theoretically analyze the ν=1/2 fractional quantum Hall state in a wide GaAs quantum well, showing that an active second subband is essential and that the ground state lies on a continuous path between the Moore-Read Pfaffian and Halperin 331 states, with results favoring a Pfaffian-like state.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Recent experiments report an unusually large activation gap at ν=1/2 in a wide GaAs quantum well, a sharp density-dependent maximum, and a possible Pfaffian-to-331 transition. The origin and nature of this state remain unresolved, motivating a detailed theoretical reanalysis.

Method: Self-consistent subband calculations, exact diagonalization, and reanalysis of published transport data; benchmark comparison with the ν=1/3 Laughlin state in the same well.

Result: The well hosts two relevant subbands. With the second subband frozen out there is most likely no incompressible state at ν=1/2; a strict bilayer at charge-density maxima does not yield 331. With an active second subband, the ground state is a paired state of electrons in both subbands, lying on a continuous path between Pfaffian and 331 with a neutral gap much larger than the single-subband Pfaffian. The path position depends on electrostatic energies only partly captured at accessible sizes, and standard overlaps fail to identify the state. Transport data do not support the reported gap magnitude; the extracted gap is smaller and comparable to the calculated two-subband neutral gap.

Conclusion: The results are more consistent with a Pfaffian-like state than with 331 but are not definitive. An active second subband is essential, and the Laughlin ν=1/3 benchmark provides an experimental test of the electrostatic treatment.

Abstract: Recent experiments on a wide GaAs quantum well report a fractional quantum Hall state at filling factor ν=1/2 with an unusually large activation gap, a sharp maximum of the gap as a function of density, and a possible transition from the non-Abelian Moore-Read Pfaffian to the Abelian Halperin 331 state. We analyze this system theoretically, with self-consistent subband calculations, exact diagonalization, and a reanalysis of the published transport data. We find that the well has two relevant subbands, that with the second subband frozen out the well most likely has no incompressible state at ν=1/2, that a strict bilayer at the maxima of the charge density does not produce 331, and that an active second subband is essential: the ground state is a paired state of electrons in both subbands, on a continuous path between the Pfaffian and 331, with a neutral gap much larger than that of a one-subband Pfaffian. The position of the state on this path is controlled by electrostatic energies that the accessible system sizes contain only in part, and the standard overlaps do not identify the state at these sizes. Our results are more consistent with a Pfaffian-like state than with 331, but they are not definitive. The transport data do not support the reported magnitude of the gap; the gap we extract is smaller and comparable to the calculated two-subband neutral gap. A benchmark calculation for the Laughlin state at ν=1/3 in the same well provides an experimental test of the electrostatic treatment.

</details>


### [114] [Trapping photo-carriers in undoped gate-defined quantum dots using an on-chip microwave resonator for charge readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02050)
*Pierre Lefloïc,Zhiren Wang,Yasmine Faraj,Harshana Runjeet,Michel Pioro-Ladrière,Alicia Kam,Louis Gaudreau,Mathieu Juan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出利用光生电荷在积累栅下形成量子点并结合超导共面波导谐振器读出，仅用四个栅极即实现双量子点器件，为量子点阵列可扩展性提供新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 栅极定义量子点系统的规模化受制于控制栅数量快速增长，需要减少栅极数目并简化制造工艺。

Method: 在无掺杂GaAs衬底上照射带隙以上激光，产生并分离电子-空穴对，使电荷被困于积累栅下方形成量子点；采用电容耦合的超导共面波导谐振器进行电荷读出。

Result: 实现了一个可工作的多电荷双量子点器件，仅含两个柱塞栅、一个隧穿耦合栅和一个传感栅；无需工程化势阱、源漏偏压、欧姆接触或原位掺杂。

Conclusion: 该混合器件示证了以最少栅极和简化工艺构建量子点阵列的新可扩展路径，并可推广至多种直接带隙半导体量子点体系。

Abstract: Scaling up gate-defined quantum dot systems is hindered by the rapid growth in the number of control gates. To tackle this challenge, we propose a different approach where the quantum dots are created from optically generated charges trapped beneath accumulation gates. By shining an above-the-gap laser light onto an undoped GaAs substrate, we demonstrate that it is possible to create and separate electron-hole pairs to form quantum dots. For the charge readout, we use a superconducting coplanar waveguide resonator capacitively coupled to the quantum dots. With these elements, we demonstrate a working many-charge double quantum dot device comprised of only two plunger gates, one tunnel coupling gate and one sensing gate. With our approach, we show that populating quantum dots does not require engineered reservoirs, source/drain bias, ohmic contacts, or in-situ doping. As a result, the number of gates can be kept minimal and the fabrication process can be simplified. Moreover, this approach can be applied to a wide range of direct-bandgap semiconductor quantum dot systems. This hybrid device demonstration is a first step towards a different scalability approach for quantum dot arrays.

</details>


### [115] [Topological Acoustic Metamaterials with Synthetic Magnetism: Observation of Hofstadter Spectrum and Topological Edge States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02262)
*Elena Rybalova,Jackson Saunders,Curtis Rasmussen,Nicholas H. Patino,Emil Prodan,Massimo Ruzzene,Camelia Prodan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作将此前提出的理论方案落地为一个集成平台，输入理论模型即可输出动力学等价的全被动声学超材料，并在θ=2π/3的Hofstadter模型上实验制备了双扇区Chern数C_s=±1的自旋陈绝缘体，观测到预测的螺旋边缘态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构材料虽已用于实现拓扑动力学，但用纯无源（被动）元件实现规范场与保护拓扑的对称性，仍是核心的实际挑战。

Method: 把先前的理论策略转化为一个自动化/集成化设计平台：以理论模型为输入，直接返回与之动力学等价的无源声学超材料；在Hofstadter模型（θ=2π/3）上开展实验验证。

Result: 成功制备了自旋陈绝缘体，其中两个扇区分别携带Chern数C_s=±1，并实验观测到理论预言的螺旋边缘态。

Conclusion: 纯无源声学架构平台可高保真地映射并实现拓扑规范场模型，为无源拓扑声学器件的按需设计与实验验证提供了通用路径。

Abstract: Architected materials are increasingly used to implement topological dynamics, yet realizing the gauge fields and symmetries that protect the topology with purely passive elements remains the central practical challenge. Here we turn a previously proposed theoretical strategy into an integrated platform that accepts a theoretical model as input and returns a passive acoustic metamaterial with identical dynamics. We demonstrate it experimentally on the Hofstadter model at $θ= 2π/3$, fabricating a spin-Chern insulator whose two sectors carry Chern numbers $C_s = \pm 1$, and observe the predicted helical edge states.

</details>


### [116] [Amplification of valley polarization in an hBN-separated MoSe2-WSe2 heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02351)
*Fateme Mahdikhany,Jacob Cutshall,Daniel Shanks,Garett Reichenbach,Davoud Adinehloo,Michael R. Koehler,David G. Mandrus,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Vasili Perebeinos,Brian J. LeRoy,John R. Schaibley*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了MoSe2/双层hBN/WSe2异质结中的低温谷极化动力学，发现双层hBN间隔层使层间激子寿命超过2 μs，并产生反常的谷极化放大效应；理论模型表明该放大源于长激子寿命与激子-激子相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属硫族化物异质结中±K谷的长寿命层间激子可承载长寿命谷极化，但如何获得足够长的激子寿命并实现可控的谷极化调控（尤其非线性放大）仍待探索。

Method: 构建MoSe2-双层hBN-WSe2异质结，以双层hBN作为间隔层；在低温下测量谷极化动力学（时间分辨极化测量），并建立理论模型分析极化放大机制的来源。

Result: 层间激子寿命超过2 μs；观察到反常的谷极化放大效应；理论模型将该放大归因于长激子寿命与激子-激子相互作用的共同作用。

Conclusion: 长寿命层间激子可用于实现非线性谷电子学晶体管与放大器，为非线性谷电子学器件奠定基础。

Abstract: Transition metal dichalcogenide heterostructures host long-lived interlayer excitons in the +K and -K valleys that can encode long-lived valley polarization. We investigated the low-temperature valley polarization dynamics in an MoSe2-bilayer hBN-WSe2 heterostructure. The bilayer hBN spacer results in an interlayer exciton lifetime exceeding 2 μs and an anomalous valley polarization amplification effect. Our theoretical model shows that this amplification results from the long exciton lifetime and exciton-exciton interactions. This work sets the stage for long-lived interlayer excitons to be used in nonlinear valleytronic transistors and amplifiers.

</details>


### [117] [Full edge control of bulk energy gap in armchair graphene nanoribbons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02367)
*Lin Wang,Thomas Luu,Ulf-G. Meißner*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在$N_w=3p+2$家族锯齿型（armchair）石墨烯纳米带中，体态能隙可完全由边界处的跃迁调控，源于体内沿带与跨带长程跃迁对能隙贡献的精确抵消，且在Hubbard相互作用下与任意宽度均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，由扩展体态形成的体能隙主要由内部体区决定，边界仅有微弱贡献。作者希望检验并挑战这一直觉，探索边界自由度是否可主导体能隙。

Method: 以armchair石墨烯纳米带为对象，聚焦$N_w=3p+2$（$p\in\mathbb{Z}_{\ge0}$）家族，通过解析分析长程跃迁对能隙的贡献，揭示体内沿带与跨带跃迁贡献的精确抵消机制，并进一步在引入Hubbard相互作用的情形下验证结论，同时考察其与带宽度的关系。

Result: 发现该家族纳米带的体能隙可仅通过边界处跃迁完全控制；这一"全边界控制"源于体内长程跃迁贡献的精确抵消；该效应在Hubbard相互作用存在时依然保持，且不依赖于带宽。

Conclusion: 边界动力学可作为调控体电子性质的有力手段，实现保持宽度不变的一维能带结构与拓扑相工程，在量子比特、人工量子自旋链、拓扑量子点等量子器件中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: In solid-state physics, the ability to open and tune an electronic energy gap provides a direct route to controlling charge transport and accessing distinct electronic phases. In solids with physical boundaries, a bulk energy gap formed exclusively by extended bulk states is conventionally expected to be governed primarily by the interior bulk region, with only a minor contribution from the boundaries. Here we show that, in stark contrast to this conventional expectation, the bulk gap of armchair graphene nanoribbons in the $N_w=3p+2$ family with $p\in\mathbb{Z}\ge 0$ can be fully controlled through the hoppings solely at the boundaries. This full boundary control originates from an exact cancellation between the gap contributions of long-range hoppings along and across the ribbon in the bulk region. It persists in the presence of Hubbard interactions and holds irrespective of ribbon width in this family. Our findings establish boundary dynamics as an unexpectedly powerful means of manipulating bulk electronic properties, enabling width-preserving engineering of one-dimensional (1D) band structures and topological phases, with potential applications in qubits, artificial quantum spin chains, topological quantum dots and other quantum devices.

</details>


### [118] [Gate-tunable superconductor-semiconductor coupling and its impact on zero-energy modes in PbTe-Pb nanowires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02411)
*Ruidong Li,Shuai Yang,Wenyu Song,Zeyu Yan,Yuhao Wang,Zehao Yu,Li Chen,Zixun Xia,Zonglin Li,Shan Zhang,Jiaye Xu,Lining Yang,Weizhao Wang,Xiao Feng,Tiantian Wang,Yunyi Zang,Lin Li,Runan Shang,Dong E. Liu,Qi-Kun Xue,Ke He,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在PbTe-Pb杂化纳米线中通过栅压调控超导-半导体耦合强度，建立耦合强度与稳健零偏压峰（ZBP）之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导-半导体耦合在杂化纳米线中对Majorana器件至关重要，需要理解可调耦合如何影响诱导能隙与零偏压峰行为。

Method: 制备PbTe-Pb杂化纳米线器件，利用栅压将系统从强耦合区调至弱耦合区，并在不同磁场范围内测量诱导能隙与零偏压峰的稳健性。

Result: 强耦合区呈现干净的超导能隙，在不同磁场区间分别观测到稳健与非稳健ZBP；增大栅压进入弱耦合区后能隙变窄变软，稳健ZBP消失而非稳健ZBP仍存在。

Conclusion: 可调耦合与稳健ZBP存在直接关联，为Majorana零模与拓扑量子比特的器件工程设计提供指导。

Abstract: Superconductor-semiconductor coupling in hybrid nanowires plays a pivotal role in Majorana devices. Here, we demonstrate this tunable coupling in PbTe-Pb nanowires and its impact on the induced gap and zero-bias peaks (ZBPs). In the strong coupling regime, the device reveals a clean superconducting gap, where robust and non-robust ZBPs are observed in distinct magnetic field ranges. Conversely, increasing the gate voltage drives the system toward the weak coupling regime, where the gap becomes narrow and soft. Consequently, robust ZBPs disappear while non-robust ones persist. Our results establish a direct link between tunable coupling and robust ZBPs, providing guidelines on engineering Majorana zero modes and topological qubits.

</details>


### [119] [Experimental realization of loss-induced quadrupole topology from a gapped trivial insulator in RLC circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02435)
*Wenxue He,Chen Wang,Fan Yang,Hechen Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过在方形-八边形RLC电路中引入图形化接地电阻，实验实现了损耗诱导的四极子拓扑相变，并用带谱、双正交嵌套Wilson圈与阻抗谱验证了实线隙闭合重开及角局域响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米拓扑相变为通过耗散调控拓扑提供了途径，但其实验信号取决于损耗如何改变系统响应；本文旨在实验上实现并观测由损耗诱导的四极子拓扑，将耗散驱动拓扑与可观测边界响应联系起来。

Method: 构建方形-八边形RLC电路，其理想厄米极限为有能隙的平庸绝缘体；采用图形化接地电阻引入在位损耗，同时保持保守耦合不变；通过带谱和双正交嵌套Wilson圈判定拓扑相变，并用阻抗谱与空间映射测量角局域响应，比较两种有限损耗构型。

Result: 带谱与双正交嵌套Wilson圈识别出伴随实线隙闭合与重开的损耗驱动拓扑相变；阻抗谱与空间映射显示出与预测四极子相一致的角局域响应；在相同预测相内，较强图形化损耗给出更显著的角对比度。

Conclusion: 损耗可在RLC电路中诱导并调控四极子拓扑，其边界响应可通过电学测量直接观测，为非厄米耗散驱动拓扑的实验研究提供了平台与判据。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian topological transitions offer a route to controlling topology through dissipation, but their experimental signatures depend on how loss modifies the system's response. Here, we experimentally realize loss-induced quadrupole topology in a square-octagon RLC circuit whose ideal Hermitian limit is a gapped trivial insulator. Patterned grounding resistors introduce on-site loss while the conservative couplings remain fixed. Band spectra and biorthogonal nested Wilson loops identify a loss-driven topological transition accompanied by closure and reopening of the real line gap. Impedance spectroscopy and spatial maps reveal corner-localized responses consistent with the predicted quadrupole phase. Comparing two finite-loss configurations, we observe enhanced corner contrast at stronger patterned loss within the same predicted phase. These results connect dissipation-driven topology to observable boundary responses in RLC circuits.

</details>


### [120] [Electrically tunable moiré hybridization in scalable twist-with-a-spacer structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02471)
*Ian Babich,Daria Belotcerkovtceva,Benjamin Fuller,Ilya Begichev,Nansi Zhou,Alex Boschi,Vaidotas Miseikis,Gianluigi Baiardi,Andrei Kudriashov,Konstantin S. Novoselov,Stiven Forti,Antonio Rossi,Fabio Beltram,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Sergio Pezzini,Camilla Coletti,Vladimir Falko,Alexey I. Berdyugin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过在扭转石墨烯层间插入原子级薄间隔层（如错位单层石墨烯），可在降低层间耦合的同时保持莫尔周期，实现莫尔微带与Brown-Zak振荡，并可用CVD规模化生长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔超晶格源于层间杂化与长波长周期的共同作用，二者均由直接接触层的扭转角决定，难以解耦；同时原子弛豫在极小扭转角下破坏超晶格均匀性，且手工堆叠难以规模化。

Method: 提出"带间隔层的扭转"（twist-with-a-spacer）结构：在扭转石墨烯层之间插入原子级薄间隔层（错位单层石墨烯），以弱化层间耦合；用较小位移场调节莫尔杂化而不改变莫尔周期；并采用可规模化CVD直接生长该结构，与手工组装器件对比输运测量。

Result: 插入间隔层后莫尔微带与显著的Brown-Zak振荡依然存在；位移场可独立调控杂化强度，从而分离其在微带形成中的作用；间隔层抑制原子弛豫，在低至约0.1°扭转角下仍获得高度均匀的刚性超晶格；CVD生长器件复现与手工堆叠器件相同的莫尔物理现象。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了莫尔能带结构形成的物理机制（区分杂化与周期性的贡献），并证明twist-with-a-spacer结构可经CVD规模化制备，为可扩展莫尔体系提供路径。

Abstract: Moiré superlattices emerge from the interplay between interlayer hybridization and long-wavelength periodicity, both set by the twist angle between layers in direct contact. Here we show that moiré minibands and pronounced Brown-Zak oscillations persist even when twisted graphene layers are separated by an atomically thin spacer, such as a misaligned graphene monolayer. This twist-with-a-spacer architecture reduces interlayer coupling, such that moiré hybridization can be tuned with a modest displacement field without changing moiré periodicity, allowing us to disentangle the roles of those parameters in miniband formation. Importantly, the spacer suppresses atomic relaxation, enabling highly uniform, rigid superlattices at twist angles down to ~0.1 degree. This robustness allows twist-with-a-spacer structures to be grown directly by scalable chemical vapour deposition (CVD): devices fabricated from CVD-grown structures exhibit the same moiré physics phenomena as those assembled manually. Our work provides insights into the physics of moiré bandstructure formation and opens a route towards scalable moiré systems.

</details>


### [121] [Magnetically and electrically tunable nonlinear Hall effect in Tt-symmetric antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02567)
*Yueyue Ning,Yongliang Song,Yanrui Ding,Zhichao Zhou,Xiao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出在铁电Tt对称反铁磁体中实现电学与磁学可调的非线性霍尔输运：铁电极化反转使贝里曲率偶极(BCD)变号，奈尔矢量转动调制其大小，并在VOCl2单层中第一性原理验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tt对称反铁磁体可具多铁性，铁电序与磁序共存形成多个序参量构型，但如何用电学方式读出这些构型尚待解决。

Method: 结合紧束缚模型与第一性原理计算，研究铁电Tt对称反铁磁体中的非线性霍尔输运，考察铁电翻转与奈尔矢量旋转对BCD符号与幅度的影响，并以VOCl2单层做验证。

Result: 模型计算显示铁电翻转可反转BCD符号，奈尔矢量旋转调制BCD大小，使铁电序与磁序均体现在非线性霍尔信号中；第一性原理计算证实VOCl2单层具备该行为，并预测出可观的BCD及潜在可测的霍尔电压。

Conclusion: 在单一材料中实现非线性霍尔效应的电学与磁学双重调控，为反铁磁自旋电子学中的多态信息编码与读出提供可能。

Abstract: Tt-symmetric antiferromagnets can exhibit multiferroicity with coexisting ferroelectricity and magnetic order, and thus host multiple order-parameter configurations. However, how to electrically read out these configurations is an issue meriting further investigation. Here, by combining tight-binding and first-principles calculations, we propose electrically and magnetically tunable nonlinear Hall transport in ferroelectric Tt-symmetric antiferromagnets. Model calculations show that ferroelectric switching can reverse the sign of the Berry curvature dipole (BCD), whereas the Neel vector rotation modulates its magnitude, allowing both ferroelectric and magnetic order to be reflected in the nonlinear Hall signal. First-principles calculations of the VOCl2 monolayer confirm these behaviors and predict a sizable BCD that gives rise to a potentially detectable Hall voltage. Our findings open up opportunities to integrate electrical and magnetic modulations of the nonlinear Hall effect within a single material, with potential applications in multi-state information encoding and readout in antiferromagnetic spintronics.

</details>


### [122] [Phonon Angular Momentum Multipoles and Nonlinear Thermal Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02619)
*Xun-Jiang Luo,Mingliang Tian*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 对全部122个磁点群系统分类 phonon angular momentum (PAM) 织构，得到 s/p/d/f/g 五重分类，并揭示温度梯度诱导 PAM 密度与 Berry 曲率多极子的对应关系，提出悬臂力矩测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性声子携带角动量并耦合晶格动力学与磁、输运现象，但磁体中动量空间 PAM 织构缺少系统分类，其热响应的实验特征也不明晰。

Method: 采用两种等价方法对全部122个磁点群进行分类：直接求解多极展开系数的线性对称约束，以及由群表示基函数构造允许织构；并分析温度梯度下非平衡 PAM 密度的展开与选择定则，提出悬臂力矩角谐波测量。

Result: 两种方法给出相同的五重分类（s 单极、p 偶极、d 四极、f 八极、g 十六极，按首个非零阶）；∇T 的第 n 阶项包含能量加权 PAM 2^n-极张量与运动学余项，从而将响应阶数与分波通道以精确选择定则联系；且与 Berry 曲率多极子及其非线性霍尔响应一一对应。

Conclusion: PAM 织构具有与 Berry 曲率多极子同构的对称分类，悬臂力矩测量中允许的角谐波由 MPG 固定（纯 n 阶谐波周期 2π/n），可作为非磁与磁材料中 PAM 分波内容的直接探针。

Abstract: Chiral phonons carry angular momentum and couple lattice dynamics to magnetic and transport phenomena. In magnets, however, a systematic classification of momentum-space phonon angular momentum (PAM) textures and experimental signatures of the resulting thermal responses remain elusive. Here we classify the PAM textures for all 122 magnetic point groups (MPGs) using two equivalent approaches: directly solving the linear symmetry constraints on the multipole expansion coefficients and constructing the allowed textures from group-representation basis functions. Both methods yield the same fivefold classification according to the leading nonzero order: $s$-wave (monopole), $p$-wave (dipole), $d$-wave (quadrupole), $f$-wave (octupole), and $g$-wave (hexadecapole). We further show that a temperature gradient generates a nonequilibrium PAM density whose $n$th-order term in $\nabla T$ contains an energy-weighted PAM $2^n$-pole tensor and a kinematic remainder, thereby linking the response order and partial-wave channel through exact selection rules. Because the Berry curvature obeys the same symmetry constraints, this taxonomy is in one-to-one correspondence with Berry-curvature multipoles and their nonlinear Hall responses. We propose a cantilever torque measurement whose allowed angular harmonics are fixed by the MPG, with period $2π/n$ for a pure $n$th harmonic, providing a direct probe of the partial-wave content of PAM in both nonmagnetic and magnetic materials.

</details>


### [123] [Strain-tunable electronic transport in disordered borophene nanoribbons with line defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02646)
*Pei-Jia Hu,Jia-Le Yu,Tie-Feng Fang,Ai-Min Guo,Qing-Feng Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文理论上研究了面内机械应变对含随机线缺陷的无序硼烯纳米带（BNR）电子输运性质的调控，揭示了共振峰对应变响应的高度各向异性与鲁棒性，并提出"缺陷-应变工程"策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序BNR中线缺陷随机分布会本征地产生共振隧穿，可作为输运调控平台；但缺乏对面内机械应变如何调制其电子输运的系统认识，需要理论阐明以实现可控的输运调制与器件应用。

Method: 采用理论计算方法，构建与金属电极耦合的不同宽度无序BNR模型，施加单轴与双轴面内机械应变，系统分析随机线缺陷诱导的共振峰在能量、宽度与幅度上的变化规律。

Result: （1）不同宽度BNR的共振峰对电极参数变化保持鲁棒；（2）单轴应变显著调制共振峰的能量、宽度与幅度，可选择性抑制共振峰并诱导新峰；（3）纵向与横向应变引起相反的能量移动，表现出显著各向异性；（4）不同宽度中重复出现的相同共振峰对应变响应一致；（5）双轴应变可实现多能级的高效电导开关，带宽越宽调制频率越高。

Conclusion: 研究确立了"缺陷-应变工程"作为一种调控硼烯及其他无能隙材料电子输运的有效策略，为基于共振隧穿的应变可调纳米电子器件提供理论依据。

Abstract: Resonant tunneling intrinsically exists in disordered borophene nanoribbons (BNRs) with randomly distributed line defects (LDs), providing a versatile platform for transport modulation. Here, we theoretically investigate the effects of in-plane mechanical strain on the electronic transport properties of disordered BNRs coupled to metallic electrodes. Our results demonstrate that the resonant peaks in disordered BNRs of different widths remain robust against variations in electrode parameters and can be efficiently modulated via mechanical strain. Specifically, uniaxial strain significantly modulates the energies, widths, and amplitudes of these resonant peaks, and can even suppress the peaks selectively and induce new ones. In particular, the response of the resonant energies to the applied strain exhibits remarkable anisotropy, where strains along the longitudinal and transverse directions induce opposite energy shifts. Moreover, identical resonant peaks recurring in different nanoribbon widths respond uniformly to applied strain. Biaxial strain enables highly efficient conductance switching across multiple distinct levels, with the modulation frequency increasing for wider nanoribbons. Our findings establish a promising defect-strain-engineering strategy for manipulating electronic transport in borophene and other gapless materials.

</details>


### [124] [Coulomb Blockade in a One-Dimensional Array of Strong Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02798)
*Bayan Karimi,Ze-Yan Chen,Jukka P. Pekola,T. Yamamoto,M. Houzet,L. I. Glazman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 测量长约瑟夫森结阵列的I-V特性，未观察到临界电流而出现阈值电压和有限激活能，与库仑阻塞一致；但能量尺度远低于几何电容预期，归因于E_J≫E_C时的强量子电荷涨落及准粒子诱导的电荷-e孤子，定量上可解释阈值但低估了阈下电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长约瑟夫森结阵列在电压偏置下的输运行为（是否存在临界电流、库仑阻塞效应及其能量尺度）尚需实验澄清；同时单岛几何电容所预期的充电能是否适用值得检验。

Method: 对电压偏置的长约瑟夫森结阵列测量电流-电压特性，提取阈值电压与激活能；将数据与考虑E_J≫E_C下强量子电荷涨落的理论比较，并引入由引线进入阵列的准粒子所对应的电荷-e孤子进行定量建模。

Result: I-V曲线无临界电流特征，呈现阈值电压与有限激活能，符合库仑阻塞；但实测能量尺度显著低于单岛几何电容给出的值。数据与理论对比显示阈值随E_J/E_C呈指数抑制。计入电荷-e孤子后可与理论定量吻合，但该模型低估了阈值以下的电流。

Conclusion: 长阵列中观测到的阈值行为由E_J≫E_C下的强电荷量子涨落主导，阈值呈对E_J/E_C的指数抑制，并可由准粒子相关的电荷-e孤子定量描述；然而阈下电流仍需额外机制解释。

Abstract: We measure current across a voltage-biased, long array of Josephson junctions.
