Current-voltage characteristics do not reveal any critical current and instead display the presence of threshold voltage and finite activation energy, consistent with the Coulomb blockade. However, the respective energy scale is well below the value prescribed by a single-island geometric capacitances. We explain it by strong quantum fluctuations of charge associated with a large value of Josephson energy, $E_J\gg E_C$, where $E_C$ is the charging energy associated with the inter-island capacitance. Comparison between the data and theory points to an exponential (in parameter $E_J/E_C$) suppression of the threshold. We achieve a quantitative agreement with theory by accounting for charge-$e$ solitons associated with the quasiparticles entering the array from its leads. This scenario however underestimates the current below threshold in comparison with the experiment.

</details>


### [125] [Observing oscillations using the non-magnetic and magnetic spin Hall effect in a non-collinear antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02804)
*Monalisa Cavalcante,José Laurentino,Carlos Eduardo,Kevin Mesel,José Holanda*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在非共线反铁磁IrMn3中，非磁性自旋霍尔效应（NMSHE）与磁性自旋霍尔效应（MSHE）随温度变化下三角磁矩的演化而呈现振荡，且振荡受IrMn3与相邻层（Py或Pt）界面磁性强烈影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非共线反铁磁体中温度驱动的磁矩演化如何调控自旋霍尔效应，并厘清界面磁性对NMSHE与MSHE振荡行为的作用。

Method: 以非共线IrMn3为研究对象，分别与铁磁体坡莫合金（Py）和顺磁体铂（Pt）构成相邻层界面；在不同实验条件下进行磁电阻测量以验证效应。

Result: NMSHE与MSHE均随温度诱导的三角磁序演化发生振荡，振荡幅度/行为显著依赖IrMn3/相邻层界面磁性，磁电阻测量结果支持该现象。

Conclusion: 该现象具有开创性，界面磁性是调控非共线反铁磁体中自旋霍尔效应振荡的关键因素。

Abstract: In this study, it was observed that, in non-collinear IrMn3, both the non-magnetic spin Hall effect (NMSHE) and the magnetic spin Hall effect (MSHE) exhibit oscillations as the triangularly ordered magnetic moments of IrMn3 evolve with temperature. These oscillations are strongly influenced by the magnetic properties at the interface between IrMn3 and the adjacent material. Permalloy (Py), a ferromagnet, and platinum (Pt), a paramagnet, were employed as adjacent layers. Such experimental analyses demonstrate the pioneering nature of this phenomenon, which is validated through magnetoresistance measurements performed under different experimental conditions.

</details>


### [126] [Zero-field plasmon splitter: Controlled interaction between quantum anomalous Hall edge states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02879)
*Dominik Hoborka,Torsten Röper,Fabian Hassler,Alexey A. Taskin,Yoichi Ando,Erwann Bocquillon*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过悬浮金属岛工程化两个空间分离、反向传播的QAH边缘通道间的库仑相互作用，观测到边缘等离激元的频率依赖再分配与杂化，并用长程相互作用模型定量再现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAH效应可提供无外磁场的手性边缘态，但其边缘通道轨迹难以像传统量子霍尔体系那样通过静电方式调控，缺乏有效的耦合与调控手段。

Method: 利用悬浮金属岛在两个空间分离、反向传播的QAH边缘通道之间引入库仑耦合；采用微波测量探测注入边缘等离激元在两个输出通道间的频率依赖分布；构建长程相互作用模型进行拟合分析。

Result: 微波测量显示注入的边缘等离激元在两输出通道间发生频率依赖的再分配，表明相互作用诱导的集体模式杂化；长程相互作用模型定量再现响应，并揭示耦合区等离激元速度极低，主要源于静电屏蔽效应。

Conclusion: 工程化库仑耦合可作为调控QAH边缘态集体激发的有效途径，并为磁拓扑绝缘体中的手性等离激元电路与电子量子光学提供基础构件。

Abstract: The quantum anomalous Hall (QAH) effect provides chiral edge states without the need for an external magnetic field, but unlike in conventional quantum Hall systems, their trajectories cannot be readily controlled electrostatically. Here, we engineer Coulomb interactions between two spatially separated, counter-propagating QAH edge channels using a floating metallic island. Microwave measurements reveal frequency-dependent redistribution of injected edge plasmons between two output channels, demonstrating interaction-induced hybridization of the collective modes. A long-range interaction model quantitatively reproduces the response and reveals a very low plasmon velocity in the coupling region, strongly reduced by electrostatic screening. Our results establish engineered Coulomb coupling as a route to control collective excitations in QAH edge states and provide a building block for chiral plasmonic circuits and electron quantum optics in magnetic topological insulators.

</details>


### [127] [Frequency-domain spin-wave logic gate based on three-magnon confluence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02946)
*Vladimir D. Bessonov,Stephanie R. Lake,Bikash Das Mohapatra,Jannis Bensmann,Georg Schmidt,Rudolf Bratschitsch,Sergej O. Demokritov,Vladislav E. Demidov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在YIG纳米波导中利用共振非简并三磁子汇聚实现频域自旋波逻辑门，可直接执行AND逻辑与模拟乘法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于波的信息处理（自旋波/磁子）有望实现低功耗纳米尺度计算，其频域操作无需为输入输出信号设置独立传播通道，是高效小型化的关键优势；本文旨在验证频域自旋波逻辑的可行性。

Method: 在钇铁石榴石（YIG）纳米波导中，利用共振、非简并的三磁子汇聚（three-magnon confluence）过程：两个不同频率的输入自旋波在共振条件下产生和频输出自旋波，仅当两输入同时存在时才有输出。

Result: 实现频域自旋波AND逻辑门；输出强度与每个输入强度呈线性关系，即正比于两者乘积，从而可实现两路信号的模拟乘法。

Conclusion: 非线性磁子汇聚是一种可用于频域自旋波逻辑与模拟信号处理的通用机制。

Abstract: Spin-wave-based information processing provides a promising platform for low-power nanoscale computing by exploiting coherent magnon dynamics and intrinsic nonlinear interactions. A key advantage of wave-based processing for efficient miniaturization is the ability to perform operations in the frequency domain without requiring separate propagation paths for the input and output signals. Here, we demonstrate a frequency-domain spin-wave logic gate based on resonant, non-degenerate three-magnon confluence in an yttrium iron garnet nanowaveguide. Under resonant conditions, two input spin waves at different frequencies efficiently generate an output spin wave at the sum frequency, which occurs only when both input signals are present, thereby directly implementing an AND logic operation. We further show that the output intensity scales linearly with each input intensity and is therefore proportional to their product, enabling analog multiplication of two signals. These results demonstrate nonlinear magnon confluence as a versatile mechanism for frequency-domain spin-wave logic and analog signal processing.

</details>


### [128] [Exact fermionic matrix elements from the Bethe-ansatz solution of the Richardson-Gaudin model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03141)
*Peter D. Johannsen,Valerii K. Kozin,Jelena Klinovaja,Daniel Loss*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 基于Richardson–Gaudin模型的Bethe-ansatz解，提出介观超导系统的有效哈密顿量描述，给出用本征值变量表示的费米子创生/湮灭算符矩阵元的紧凑行列式公式，从而在模型本征基下直接表示单电子隧穿项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于本征值的框架仅能处理重叠积分（overlaps）与已知形状因子（form factors），无法在本征基中直接表示单电子隧穿等单粒子算符，限制了对介观超导器件（如超导Anderson杂质模型）的低能有效描述与可观测量的计算。

Method: 以Richardson–Gaudin可积模型的Bethe-ansatz解为基础构造有效哈密顿量；推导费米子创生与湮灭算符矩阵元的紧凑行列式公式，全部以本征值相关变量表达；将该公式用于超导Anderson杂质模型（量子点耦合超小超导颗粒），在正则系综下建立低能有效描述。

Result: 矩阵元行列式公式使单电子隧穿项可在Richardson–Gaudin本征基中直接写出，推广了本征值框架；在量子点–超小超导颗粒体系的正则系综描述中自然涵盖奇偶效应（even-odd effects）、亚能隙激发与量子点物理；并首次给出该类颗粒–点系统在固定粒子数下的光学电导率与可磁通调谐的能谱等此前未能计算的可观测量。

Conclusion: 该工作将本征值形式的可积方法从重叠积分与形状因子拓展到单粒子算符矩阵元，为介观超导系统提供统一、可在正则系综中直接使用的有效哈密顿量框架，并为超导-量子点杂化结构的谱学与输运性质计算开辟途径。

Abstract: We introduce an effective Hamiltonian description of mesoscopic superconducting systems based on the Bethe-ansatz solution of the Richardson--Gaudin model. Our central result is a compact determinant formula for the matrix elements of fermionic creation and annihilation operators, expressed entirely in terms of eigenvalue-based variables. This formula allows single-electron tunneling terms to be represented directly in the eigenbasis of the Richardson--Gaudin model, extending the eigenvalue-based framework beyond overlaps and previously known form factors. As a benchmark, we apply the method to a superconducting Anderson impurity model comprising a quantum dot coupled to an ultrasmall superconducting grain. The resulting low-energy description, formulated naturally in the canonical ensemble, captures even-odd effects, subgap excitations, and quantum-dot physics. The same framework gives access to observables not previously computed for such grain-dot systems in the canonical ensemble, such as the fixed-particle-number optical conductivity and flux-tunable spectra.

</details>


### [129] [Magnetic field as a tuning knob for edge mode interaction in integer quantum Hall interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03284)
*Ning Ma,Tevž Lotrič,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Steven H. Simon,Mitali Banerjee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用高可调双层石墨烯量子霍尔干涉仪，测量h/e周期与倍频(h/2e)区域随磁场的分布，并观测到倍频振荡与内侧手性模h/e周期叠加共存，支持边模间强电容耦合导致频率加倍的图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子霍尔干涉仪可探测边缘手性模性质及其相互作用；强模间相互作用会使干涉周期由h/e变为h/2e，但其微观机制尚不清楚，需要可调体系系统研究。

Method: 采用高度可调的双层石墨烯器件构建量子霍尔干涉仪，作为磁场函数测量h/e周期与倍频（半量子磁通）区域的分布，并将器件推至倍频振荡与来自更远离边缘的内侧手性模的h/e周期振荡叠加共存的区域。

Result: 观测到h/e周期与倍频两种区域随磁场的变化；在特定区域实现倍频振荡与叠加的h/e周期图样共存。

Conclusion: 该共存区域可由边模之间强电容耦合导致频率加倍的物理图像自然解释，为倍数机制提供了微观洞察。

Abstract: The quantum Hall interferometer has been shown to be a powerful tool for probing both the nature of the chiral modes present at the edges of these systems and the interactions between them. Strong inter-edge mode interactions can lead the system into a regime where the period of the interferometer signal corresponds to an insertion of a half-quantum flux h/2e, rather than the standard flux quantum h/e. Using a highly tunable bilayer graphene device, we are able to measure the prevalence of both the h/e-period and the doubled-frequency regimes as a function of magnetic field, providing insight into the microscopic nature of the doubling mechanism. Furthermore, we are able to push our device to a regime where double-frequency oscillations coexist with a superimposed period-h/e pattern associated with the inner chiral modes farther from the edge. We argue that this regime has a natural interpretation in a picture of the frequency doubling being caused by strong capacitive couplings between the edge modes.

</details>


### [130] [Electron--phonon cooling noise in monolayer and bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03316)
*Muhammad Waqas Haseeb,Edvin G. Idrisov*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究单层与Bernal双层石墨烯中电子-声子热流涨落，构造对称热流算符并在电子与声子温度不等时计算非平衡有限频率流-流关联函数，给出Bloch–Grüneisen与均分区间解析结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限频率下非平衡热流噪声缺乏直接计算的谱密度框架，动态涨落-耗散关系在电子声子温度不等时失效，需建立可直接计算的对称热流算符与非平衡关联函数方法。

Method: 构造对称热流算符，在不等电子/声子温度下求非平衡流-流关联子（有限频率）；热流顶点含探针能量加二倍声子能量；直接计算非平衡谱密度；在Bloch–Grüneisen与均分区间给出完整解析式及低频极限，并与单层石墨烯完整掺杂带内on-shell计算对比。

Result: 解析与数值结果衔接两个温度区间，显示非平衡下有限频率噪声的变化；代表性参数下修正在上GHz至亚THz频段达百分量级；平衡时满足零频热FDT并重现已知冷却功率与热导定律。

Conclusion: 所构造的对称热流算符与非平衡关联函数方案适用于石墨烯电子-声子热输运噪声，可捕捉非平衡有限频率修正，并在平衡极限下与FDT及既有热输运定律自洽。

Abstract: We study the electron--phonon heat-current fluctuations in monolayer and Bernal bilayer graphene coupled to longitudinal acoustic phonons. To do this, we construct a symmetric heat-current operator and evaluate the non-equilibrium current--current correlator at finite frequency for unequal electron and phonon temperatures. The heat-current vertices then involve the probe energy combined with twice the phonon energy, and the nonequilibrium spectral density must be calculated directly rather than inferred from a dynamic thermal fluctuation--dissipation relation. Further, we obtain full analytical expressions in the Bloch--Grüneisen (BG) and Equipartition (EP) regimes, derive their low-frequency limits, and compare them with a complete doped-intraband on-shell calculations for monolayer graphene. The numerical results connect the two temperature regimes and show how finite-frequency noise changes out of equilibrium. For representative parameters, the corrections become percent-level in the upper-gigahertz to sub-terahertz range. It is worth mentioning that in equilibrium, the results satisfy the zero-frequency thermal fluctuation--dissipation relation(FDT) and reproduce the well established cooling-power and thermal-conductance laws.

</details>


### [131] [Polariton tweezers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03506)
*Nika Gribova,Evgeny Sedov,Alexey Kavokin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出"动态极化激元镊子"概念：利用偶极（d-）激子-极化激元的长程静电场，在二维电子系统中对电子进行无创、确定性的俘获与输运，并通过泵浦频率失谐实现任意方向上的刚性传送。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态量子技术中，电荷载流子的非侵入式、确定性操控仍是一项基础性难题；现有手段往往依赖接触电极或直接动量传递，缺乏可重构、长程的光学操控平台。

Method: 以TE偏振、专门设计的非共线三光束连续波光泵驱动半导体微腔，合成具有六角对称性的宏观极化激元晶格；d-极化激元凝聚体的空间周期电场构成可重构的电子俘获势场。通过在泵浦间引入定向频率失谐打破系统时空对称性，将静态干涉图样转化为动态"传送带"，并借助精确的相位匹配机制实现无需净动能转移的晶格刚性平移。

Result: 实验演示了静态六角极化激元晶格对电子的静电俘获；频率失谐使晶格及被俘获电子沿任意面内方向整体刚性移动，实现长程输运，且该过程不依赖净动能动量转移，表现出稳健的光学可控性。

Conclusion: 动态极化激元镊子为驱动-耗散量子流体中带电粒子及拓扑缺陷的长程路由提供了无接触、可重构且精确相位匹配的光学操控平台，拓展了极化激元光力学的应用范围。

Abstract: The non-invasive, deterministic manipulation of charge carriers remains a fundamental challenge in solid-state quantum technologies. Here, we demonstrate the concept of dynamic polariton tweezers capable of trapping and transporting electrons across a two-dimensional electron system via the long-range electrostatic potential of dipolar (d-) exciton-polaritons. By driving a semiconductor microcavity with a specifically engineered, non-collinear three-beam optical pump with TE-polarization, we synthesize a macroscopic polariton lattice with hexagonal symmetry. The spatially periodic field of this d-polariton condensate acts as a reconfigurable trapping landscape for the electrons. We show that introducing targeted frequency detunings between the continuous-wave pumps strictly breaks the spatiotemporal symmetry of the system, converting the stationary macroscopic interference pattern into a dynamically moving conveyor belt. By precisely customizing these frequency detunings, the entire lattice - along with the electrostatically trapped electrons - can be rigidly driven in any arbitrary in-plane direction. This precise phase-matching mechanism bypasses the need for a net kinetic momentum transfer, establishing a robust, optically controlled platform for the long-range routing of charged particles and topological defects in driven-dissipative quantum fluids.

</details>


### [132] [The effects of an alternating bias on a single orbital spin impurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03507)
*Matyas Nachtigall,Jose Reina-Gálvez,Christoph Wolf,Nicolás Lorente*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 采用Redfield量子主方程框架，将射频偏压显式地通过电子库引入，揭示了ESR-STM中射频驱动自旋的微观机制：RF电压周期性地扫过静态输运图景，使DLT在电荷跃迁附近被强烈增强，而FLT源于虚电荷涨落、在库仑封锁深处仍保持有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ESR-STM能够以原子级空间分辨率相干操控与探测单个原子自旋，但射频偏压驱动自旋的微观机制仍存在争议，亟需澄清其物理来源。

Method: 构建Redfield量子主方程框架，将振荡偏压以电子库的形式显式纳入，而非采用等效的含时隧穿振幅；在半经典极限下分析RF电压对系统静态输运图景的周期性扫描。

Result: 输运区间由直流偏压与RF幅度共同决定；当RF轨迹接近或穿越电荷跃迁时，与自旋极化电荷转移相关的类阻尼力矩（DLT）显著增强；而交换场诱导的类场力矩（FLT）因源于虚电荷涨落，在库仑封锁区深处仍保持有限；RF轨迹还对强偏压依赖的静态量在有限电压范围内取平均，从而平滑了电荷阈值附近的Lamb位移等特征。

Conclusion: RF幅度不仅是ESR驱动强度的控制参数，同时也是调控输运区间以及FLT/DLT比值的控制参数。

Abstract: Electron spin resonance with the scanning tunneling microscope (ESR-STM) enables coherent manipulation and detection of individual atomic spins with atomic-scale spatial resolution, yet the microscopic mechanism by which the radiofrequency (RF) bias drives the spin remains under debate. We study this problem using a Redfield quantum-master-equation framework in which the oscillating bias enters explicitly through the electronic reservoirs rather than through an effective time-dependent tunneling amplitude. In the semiclassical limit, the RF voltage periodically sweeps the system through its static transport landscape, so that the transport regime depends jointly on the DC bias and RF amplitude. When the RF trajectory approaches or crosses a charge transition, the damping-like torque (DLT) associated with spin-polarized charge transfer is strongly enhanced. By contrast, the field-like torque (FLT) induced by the exchange field remains finite deeper in the Coulomb-blockade (CB) regime because it originates from virtual charge fluctuations. The RF trajectory also averages strongly bias-dependent static quantities over a finite voltage range, smoothing features such as the Lamb shift near the charge thresholds. These results establish the RF amplitude as a control parameter not only for the strength of the ESR drive, but also for the transport regime and the ratio between FLT and DLT.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [Animated Ultraviolet Holography via Programmable Nonlinear Phase Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00267)
*Ze Zheng,Olga Sergaeva,Davide Rocco,Luca Carletti,Can Tao,Yucheng Zhang,Guoquan Zhang,Costantino De Angelis,Lei Xu,Anatoly V. Zayats,Mohsen Rahmani*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 基于600 nm厚铌酸锂薄膜构建"虚拟超表面"，通过二次非线性实现空间相位跨频率转移，在紫外波段实现可编程、动态（10 Hz）全息与光束偏转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紫外波段材料吸收强，透镜、反射镜、滤波器及空间相位调制器等光学与光电器件功能受限，缺乏灵活、可重构的UV光场调控手段。

Method: 用空间光调制器将计算机生成相位分布编码到长波长泵浦光束上，经600 nm铌酸锂薄膜的二阶非线性（SHG）将编程相位传递至紫外场；无需纳米结构图案化或直接UV空间调制，并在全息设计中考入非线性相位关系。

Result: 实验重建6种UV全息图样，归一化互相关0.68–0.83；SLM动态刷新实现10 Hz动画UV全息；SHG发射可调谐350–390 nm；动态光束偏转约20 Hz。

Conclusion: 虚拟超表面可通过跨光频的空间编程实现软件定义的UV全息，为无需UV兼容光学与光电器件的可重构短波光束整形与结构照明提供新途径。

Abstract: Dynamic and programmable control of ultraviolet (UV) light remains challenging because of conventional high material absorption and the limited functionality of optics such as lenses, mirrors or filters, and optoelectronic devices, such as spatial phase modulators, over the UV range. Here, by employing the concept of virtual metasurfaces based on arrays of programmable optical patterns on a 600-nm-thick lithium niobate film as the nonlinear medium, we demonstrate nonlinear phase transfer, generating programmable holography in the UV range. This arrangement provides a unique platform for generating animated holograms. A spatial light modulator encodes a computer-generated phase distribution onto a longer-wavelength pump beam, while second-harmonic generation transfers the programmed spatial phase to the UV field through the quadratic nonlinear interaction. Importantly, the nonlinear film serves as a simple frequency-conversion interface, eliminating the need for nanostructure patterning or direct UV spatial modulation. By incorporating the nonlinear phase relationship into the hologram design, we experimentally reconstruct six distinct UV holographic patterns, with normalised cross-correlation values ranging from 0.68 to 0.83, demonstrating reliable UV holographic image reconstruction. As a result, the dynamic updating of the SLM enables animated UV holograms at 10 Hz, demonstrating reconfigurable UV holography using the same nonlinear film. The system also supports tunable SHG emission from 350 to 390 nm and dynamic beam steering at approximately 20 Hz. The results demonstrate the potential of virtual metasurfaces to realise software-defined UV holography by transferring spatial programming across optical frequencies. This opens new opportunities for reconfigurable short-wavelength UV beam shaping and structured illumination, without the need for UV-compatible optics or optoelectronics.

</details>


### [134] [Coherent transduction and dynamical back-action between light and millimeter waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00279)
*André Garcia Primo,Jiawen Liu,Amit Vankayala,Gabriel Jülg,Aleksei Gaier,Yazan Lampert,Ileana-Cristina Benea-Chelmus*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作实现了光学与毫米波谐振腔尺寸相当、模式体积最小的三共振电光换能器，在263 GHz处获得g0/2π≈60 kHz的真空电光耦合率，并演示了内效率约67%的相干转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电光转换可将毫米波信号参数化映射到低损耗、低噪声的光载波上，从而规避电子频率转换的损耗与噪声，且耦合率随频率增长。但要利用这一增长，需同时缩小毫米波与光学谐振腔并保持其品质因数与空间重叠，此前尚未实现。

Method: 构建三共振换能器：由耦合的铌酸锂Fabry-Pérot腔（自由光谱范围>650 GHz、品质因数达10^6）构成光子分子，并与263 GHz的四分之一波长金谐振腔集成；两腔尺寸相当、模式体积最小。

Result: 真空电光耦合率达g0/2π≈60 kHz，为既往毫米波换能器的6倍、微波频率下的60倍；可在有效占据数低于1时光学探测室温热毫米波光子，并用作转换效率的校准信号；相干转换合作度接近0.3，对应内效率约67%，并观察到展宽或窄化光学共振的电光动力学反作用。

Conclusion: 结果验证了电光耦合频率缩放的优越性与可行性，为弱信号传感及量子与经典网络建立了器件架构。

Abstract: Electro-optic transduction can bypass the loss and noise of electronic frequency conversion at millimeter-wave frequencies by parametrically converting signals onto low-loss, low-noise optical carriers, with coupling rates that grow with frequency. Exploiting this growth, however, requires shrinking both the millimeter-wave and optical resonators while preserving their quality factors and spatial overlap, which has not yet been achieved. Here, we demonstrate a triply resonant transducer whose optical and millimeter-wave resonators have comparable dimensions and minimal mode volumes. Coupled lithium niobate Fabry-Pérot cavities, with free spectral ranges above 650 GHz and quality factors up to $10^6$, form a photonic molecule integrated with a quarter-wave gold resonator at 263 GHz. The device reaches a vacuum electro-optic coupling rate $g_0/2π\approx 60$ kHz, six times larger than previous millimeter-wave transducers and sixty times larger than at microwave frequencies. The device optically detects room-temperature thermal millimeter-wave photons at effective occupancies below one, which also serve as a calibration tone for transduction efficiency. Coherent transduction reaches cooperativities approaching 0.3, corresponding to internal efficiencies around 67%, accompanied by electro-optic dynamical back-action that broadens or narrows the optical resonances. These results demonstrate the advantage and feasibility of the frequency scaling of electro-optic coupling, establishing an architecture for weak-signal sensing and quantum and classical networking.

</details>


### [135] [BEEF: A Hybrid Integrated Photonic Simulation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00393)
*Jacob M. Hiesener,Stephen E. Ralph*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: BEEF: 一个Python框架，将器件分段并用EME或FDTD分别求解后级联，实现保持第一性原理电磁建模的同时扩展仿真规模，并用FDTD校准EME传播常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单独使用EME或FDTD都难以处理既长又几何复杂的器件：FDTD对长器件计算不可行，EME对复杂几何适应性差。需要一种在保持第一性原理精度的同时突破单一方法规模限制的方案。

Method: 将器件划分为若干区段，按各区段几何特点分别用EME或FDTD独立Maxwell求解器仿真，再级联各区段响应；并利用FDTD实测的相位累积对EME传播常数进行校准，提供跨求解器界面的模式一致性验证诊断。

Result: 2x2 MMI分束器：混合结果与全器件FDTD在C波段内偏差分别为0.59%(bar)与0.76%(cross)；锥形变体为0.87%与2.01%。定向耦合器：有效折射率校准使切分位置依赖降低167倍。电路级级联复现实测Fabry-Perot谐振线型与自由光谱范围，并通过四次缓存仿真的后处理探索耦合长度与臂长设计空间。最后仿真了167.3 μm长4x4 MMI基90度光混频器（全器件FDTD不可行），缩短长度变体在透射率与相对相位上吻合良好。

Conclusion: BEEF证明混合EME-FDTD分区级联可在保持第一性原理精度的前提下，将仿真能力扩展到全器件FDTD不可行的长且复杂的集成光子器件，并支持高效的电路级设计空间探索。

Abstract: A Python framework is presented that retains first-principles electromagnetic modeling while extending simulation to devices whose length and geometric complexity place them beyond the practical reach of either method alone. The Bridged Engine for eigenmode expansion (EME) and finite-difference time-domain (FDTD) (BEEF) partitions a device into sections which are simulated using either EME or FDTD independently developed Maxwell solvers depending on what best suits each section's geometry, and cascades the resulting responses. BEEF calibrates the EME propagation constants against FDTD-measured phase accumulation, providing a mode identity verification diagnostic across solver interfaces. For a 2x2 multimode-interference splitter, the hybrid result agrees with full-device FDTD to within 0.59% (bar) and 0.76% (cross) across the C-band; a tapered variant agrees to within 0.87% and 2.01%, respectively. For a directional coupler, effective-index calibration reduces cut-position dependence 167-fold. A circuit-level cascade reproduces the measured Fabry-Perot resonance lineshape and free spectral range while exploring the coupler-length and arm-length design plane through post-processing of four cached simulations. Finally, we use BEEF to simulate a 167.3 um-long 4x4 MMI-based 90-degree optical hybrid that is computationally impractical for full-device FDTD, with a reduced-length variant confirming close agreement in transmission and relative phase.

</details>


### [136] [Nonperturbative theory of thin-vapor-layer linear optical properties: towards the optimal design of alkali-metal vapor nanocells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00400)
*Andrei V. Ermolaev,Tigran A. Vartanyan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出严格数值方法计算纳米厚碱金属蒸气层的非局域光学响应谱，适用于任意光学密度与厚度，预测了一阶微扰中缺失的密度依赖共振蓝移，并给出反射率导数极值厚度的近似解析式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米级蒸气池是微型频率标准、磁力计和原子传感器的理想介质，但中低原子密度下其线性光学性质理解不足；根源在于原子自由运动与壁碰撞导致的强非局域光学响应。已有计算仅限淬灭碰撞情形下的密度微扰论，限制实用范围。

Method: 建立严格的数值方法，在任意光学密度与层厚下精确处理非局域光学响应，计算薄蒸气层的反射与透射谱，突破一阶微扰框架。

Result: 预测共振的密度依赖蓝移，该效应在一阶微扰描述中不存在，其量级可与原子跃迁自然线宽相比，多数情形不可忽略；系统扫描不同层厚，揭示反射率及其对激光失谐导数的厚度依赖规律。

Conclusion: 导出反射率一阶导数取极大值时的厚度近似表达式，与严格数值模型高度吻合且可退化为一阶解析结果，为纳米厚光谱池设计提供实用工具并为理论发展奠定基础。

Abstract: Hot alkali-metal vapors confined inside nanometer-thick spectroscopic cells are highly suitable media for miniature and cheap frequency standards, magnetometers and atomic sensors. Yet, their linear optical properties in case of low to moderate atomic densities are not fully understood. The primary reason for that is the complex, strongly nonlocal optical response of such systems to the incident resonant radiation arising as the result of the free motion of atoms and their collisions with the walls. To date, calculations of reflection and transmission spectra of these cells has been restricted to perturbation theory in atomic density for the case of quenching atom-wall collisions, which substantially limited the scope of their practical applications. Here we introduce the rigorous numerical approach for computing thin-vapor-layer spectra with an accurate account for the nonlocal optical response at arbitrary optical density and layer thickness. Our model predicts a density-dependent blue shift of the resonance that is absent in the first-order perturbative description. We show that its values are comparable to the natural linewidth of an atomic transition and, therefore, too large to be neglected in most use cases. Inspired by recent experiments, we then explore a wide range of vapor layer thicknesses and study how the reflectivity and its derivative with respect to the laser detuning depend on the layer thickness. Finally, we derive an approximate expression for the thickness at which the first derivative of the reflectivity reaches its maximum that agrees closely with the rigorous numerical model and reduces to the first-order analytical result. These results provide a practical design tool for nanometer-thick spectroscopic cells in different applications as well as a starting point for further development of the theory.

</details>


### [137] [Experimental demonstration of response-enhanced fiber Sagnac gyroscopy via imaginary weak-value amplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00481)
*Jing-Hui Huang,Hong-Liang Cui,Tianying Chang,Jing Zhang,TianYe Huang,Xiang-Yun Hu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 实验演示100 m光纤Sagnac陀螺仪，利用虚弱值放大(IWVA)将Sagnac偏振相位经后选择与光谱分辨探测转为可测光谱响应，最大响应增强约13倍，Allan偏差由0.06降至0.0091 rad/s（约6.6倍改善）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升光纤Sagnac陀螺仪对旋转的光学读出响应与灵敏度，将弱值放大技术从原理验证推向实用的旋转精密传感。

Method: 构建100 m长光纤Sagnac陀螺仪；Sagnac引起的偏振相位经偏振后选择转换为光谱响应，采用光谱分辨探测；改变后选择角表征旋转速率范围内的响应；用FFT与Allan偏差表征噪声与稳定性。

Result: 响应随后选择角趋近正交而系统性增大，相对无后选择的固定参考配置最大实验增强约13倍；近正后选择下等效转速波动降低，最小Allan偏差由参考配置的0.06 rad/s降到0.0091 rad/s（约6.6倍降低）；SLD光源低频涨落限制长期稳定性。

Conclusion: IWVA为响应增强型光纤Sagnac陀螺与精密旋转传感提供了一种实用途径；后续需抑制宽带SLD光源的低频涨落以改善长期稳定性。

Abstract: We experimentally demonstrate a 100-m long fiber Sagnac gyroscope employing imaginary weak-value amplification (IWVA) to enhance the optical readout response to rotation. A Sagnac-induced polarization phase is converted into a measurable spectral response through polarization post-selection and spectrally resolved detection. By varying the post-selection angle, we characterize the gyroscope response over the experimentally accessible rotation-rate range. The response increases systematically toward orthogonal post-selection, yielding a maximum experimental enhancement of approximately 13 times relative to a fixed reference configuration without postselection. Fast Fourier transform and Allan-deviation measurements further characterize the noise and stability of the gyroscope. Near-orthogonal post-selection reduces the equivalent rotation-rate fluctuations, with the minimum Allan deviation decreasing from $0.06~\mathrm{rad/s}$ for the reference configuration to $0.0091~\mathrm{rad/s}$ for the configuration exhibiting the maximum response enhancement, corresponding to an approximately 6.6-fold reduction. Low-frequency fluctuations of the broadband superluminescent diode source limit the long-term stability. These results demonstrate that IWVA provides a practical approach for response-enhanced fiber-based Sagnac gyroscopy and precision rotation sensing.

</details>


### [138] [Non-Hermitian Theory of Surface Emitting Jackiw-Rebbi States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00674)
*Hangkai Fan,Mingzhao Song,Andrey Bogdanov*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文建立非厄米狄拉克形式描述介质超光栅中的表面发射Jackiw-Rebbi态，通过调节微扰局域振幅塑造复狄拉克质量分布，实现界面态品质因子与远场辐射图案的可控拓扑调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放光子体系中拓扑界面态兼具空间局域与时间（辐射品质因子）约束，但如何在保持拓扑特性的同时调控其品质因子与远场辐射尚缺乏理论框架。

Method: 构建非厄米狄拉克形式，将晶格微扰的局域振幅映射为复狄拉克质量剖面；在具有二阶布里渊区折叠的超光栅中设计空间渐变的超晶格微扰。

Result: 空间渐变超晶格微扰支持品质因子超过10^4的界面态，并实现厄米-高斯光束发射，同时保持拓扑性质不变。

Conclusion: 超晶格微扰可作为拓扑光束整形策略，为高Q拓扑表面发射器提供实现途径。

Abstract: Topological interface states in open photonic systems combine spatial localization at domain walls with temporal confinement characterized by a radiative quality factor. Here, we develop a non-Hermitian Dirac formalism for surface-emitting Jackiw-Rebbi states in dielectric metagratings. We show how varying the local amplitude of the lattice perturbation shapes the complex Dirac mass profile, enabling control over the quality factors and far-field radiation patterns of the interface states, while preserving their topological character. Applying this framework to a metagrating with second-order Brillouin-zone folding, we design a spatially graded superlattice perturbation that supports interface states with quality factors exceeding 10$^4$ and Hermite-Gaussian beam emission. These results establish superlattice perturbations as a strategy for topological beam shaping and offer a route toward high-Q topological surface emitters.

</details>


### [139] [Energy-Centroid Trembling near Exceptional Points in Photonic Time Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00743)
*Kyungmin Lee,Younsung Kim,Minwook Kyung,Seungmin Yeon,Bumki Min*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 在调制Lorentz回复系数的光子时间晶体中，调制重新分配极化势能、损耗耗散其动能；当二者在脉冲内错位时，能量质心逐周期"颤动"而坡印廷通量速度保持有界。近带边奇异点处模式几何放大错位，窄带脉冲的颤动速度振幅随动量失谐的负二分之一次方增长，损耗相位调制可单独改变颤动系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究光子时间晶体中隐含超出能带斜率与Floquet Petermann因子所决定范围的能量质心动力学，明确极化势能与动能（损耗）之间的非平衡交换如何影响脉冲传播特性。

Method: 构建调制Lorentz回复系数的时间晶体模型：将调制视为对极化势能的重新加权，损耗抽走动能；分析脉冲内二者的相位错位，在带边奇异点附近考察模式几何对错位的放大；对单通带Floquet分支上的窄带脉冲解析导出颤动速度与动量失谐的标度关系，并考察损耗与回复系数同相/反相调制的影响，最后用有损实例做数值验证。

Result: 近带边奇异点处颤动速度振幅随动量失谐近似按负二分之一次方增长（受有限带宽圆整）；同相或反相损耗调制可改变颤动系数而能带斜率与Petermann系数几乎不变。有损算例中，极化初始静止的向前脉冲在明显能量损失后于拍频复现附近出现延迟爆发，单色振幅在计算范围内随失谐按负二分之三次方变化。

Conclusion: 周期内能量交换与初态制备共同塑造质心运动，其效应超出能带斜率与Floquet Petermann因子的描述能力，二者需作为独立机制纳入时间晶体的脉冲动力学刻画。

Abstract: In a photonic time crystal with a modulated Lorentz restoring coefficient, modulation reweights the potential energy of the polarization and loss drains its kinetic energy; when these sectors are offset within a pulse, the energy centroid trembles in every cycle while the Poynting-flux velocity stays bounded. Near the gap-edge exceptional point the mode geometry amplifies these offsets, and for a narrow-band pulse on one passband Floquet branch the trembling-velocity amplitude generically grows as the inverse square root of the momentum detuning, rounded by finite bandwidth. Loss modulated in phase or in antiphase with the restoring coefficient can change the trembling coefficient while the band-slope and Petermann coefficients barely move. In the lossy example studied, a forward field pulse launched with the polarization at rest exhibits delayed bursts near the beat recurrence after substantial energy loss, with monochromatic amplitudes following the inverse three-halves power of the detuning over the computed range. Intracycle energy exchange and preparation thus shape the centroid motion beyond what the band slope and the Floquet Petermann factor determine.

</details>


### [140] [Semi-classical analysis of noise squeezing in Kerr microresonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00805)
*Sara Persia,Lev Fandeev,Fuchuan Lei,Bastian Ruhnke,Alexander E. Ulanov,Vaishali Adya,Victor Torres-Company,Tobias Herr*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文证明基于Lugiato-Lefever方程与Ikeda映射的半经典框架在正确引入真空涨落后，可准确预测Kerr微谐振器中三种压缩构型的正交噪声特性，为压缩光源设计提供实用数值工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kerr微谐振器是片上压缩光光源的重要平台，但设计此类光源需要能兼顾真实器件物理与噪声过程的数值工具；全量子模拟代价高，而已有的半经典框架（LLE、Ikeda映射）虽在经典光频梳开发中极为成功，其是否可用于压缩噪声预测尚不明确。

Method: 在半经典场方程中显式加入适当归一化的真空涨落，构造随机半经典模型，并针对三种典型压缩构型——双模正交压缩、单模正交压缩与强度差压缩——计算正交方差与噪声谱，与全量子理论结果进行对照。

Result: 在三种构型下，半经典模拟给出的正交方差与噪声谱均与量子理论预测高度吻合；压缩的关键实验特征可被理解为随机半经典场方程中对归一化真空涨落的相位敏感放大。

Conclusion: LLE与Ikeda映射类半经典框架可作为压缩光源设计的实用工具，且易于纳入额外噪声机制、非线性效应和复杂谐振器结构；同时该结果为区分哪些压缩特性可由半经典场演化获得、哪些必须依赖完整量子描述提供了判据。

Abstract: Kerr microresonators are integrated nonlinear devices widely used to generate classical optical frequency combs and nonclassical states of light. In particular, they provide a promising platform for squeezed light sources, which are important elements of emerging quantum sensing, communication, and computing technologies. Designing such sources requires numerical tools that efficiently incorporate realistic device physics and noise processes. Here, we show that established semi-classical frameworks based on the Lugiato-Lefever equation and the Ikeda map, instrumental in the development of classical light sources, can also predict the quadrature-noise properties of squeezed light when vacuum fluctuations are properly included. We consider three canonical configurations producing two-mode quadrature squeezing, single-mode quadrature squeezing, and intensity difference squeezing. In each case, the semi-classical simulations produce quadrature variances and noise spectra in close agreement with the quantum theoretical predictions. These frameworks therefore provide practical tools for designing squeezed light sources, with the capability to incorporate additional noise mechanisms, nonlinear effects, and complex resonator arrangements. More fundamentally, our results show that key experimental signatures of squeezing can be modeled as phase-sensitive amplification of properly normalized vacuum fluctuations within stochastic semi-classical field equations. This observation helps distinguish squeezing properties accessible through semi-classical field evolution from those intrinsically requiring a full quantum description.

</details>


### [141] [Unlocking Low-Frequency Laser Doppler Holography Spectra for Functional Retinal and Choroidal Microvascular Phenotyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01052)
*Zacharie Auray,Adrien Gordon,Gabriel Cellier,Yann Fischer,Michael Atlan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种基于心相位对齐的时变谱过量（SE）分布构建方法，用于在毛细血管等未分辨血管区域提取功能性血流波形，并以谱过量指数（SEI，SE的时间中位数）概括其强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光多普勒全息术以37 kHz帧率记录视网膜与脉络膜大血管的功能性血流波形时，通常滤除低频段；但在毛细血管相关区域，血管未被分辨且间歇性眼动可主导信号，需要一种能在该情形下刻画低频多普勒信号时间组织的方法。

Method: 选取完整心动周期，在未分辨血管区域施加连续的空间中位数与心搏中位数以抑制眼动；在每个相位上，以约±18.5 kHz高频尾端定义的参考面积对比约±2 kHz两个固定低频峰之间的积分多普勒谱面积，构造时变谱过量（SE）分布；将波形对照归一化心相位显示，并用SE的时间中位数定义谱过量指数（SEI），另给出谱池化后的替代评估方式。

Result: 一条代表性记录展示了该构建流程；所得波形在整个心动周期内保持变化，为探究其时间组织提供了基础。文中未给出定量性能指标，生理特异性和可重复性仍有待量化。

Conclusion: 该方法为未分辨毛细血管区域的功能性血流波动提供了一种心相位分辨的谱学描述框架，其中SEI作为强度概括指标；其生理特异性与重复性尚需后续量化验证。

Abstract: The low-frequency part of optical fluctuation spectra recorded by laser Doppler holography at a frame rate of 37~kHz is typically filtered out to reveal functional blood-flow waveforms in major retinal and choroidal vessels. We construct a time-varying spectral-excess (SE) distribution in capillary-associated territories by selecting complete cardiac cycles and applying successive spatial and beat medians in regions where vessels are unresolved and intermittent eye motion can dominate the signal. At each phase, spectral excess compares the integrated Doppler spectral area between two fixed low-frequency peaks near $\pm2$~kHz with a reference area defined by the high-frequency tail near $\pm18.5$~kHz. The resulting waveform, displayed against normalized cardiac phase, retains variation throughout the cardiac cycle and provides a basis for exploring its temporal organization. We summarize spectral-excess strength by the spectral excess index (SEI), defined as the temporal median of SE, and also describe an alternative evaluation after spectral pooling. A representative recording illustrates the construction. Physiological specificity and repeatability remain to be quantified.

</details>


### [142] [Quantitative Analytical Spheroid Model for Scattering-Type Scanning Near-Field Optical Spectroscopy of Layered Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01057)
*Kirill V. Voronin,Edoardo Vicentini,Lars Mester,Rainer Hillenbrand,Alexey Y. Nikitin*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文将此前针对均匀块体样品的s-SNOM准静电场长椭球精确解析解推广至多层样品，提出广义镜像电荷法与傅里叶积分法两种等价实现，兼具数值仿真的精度与解析模型的效率，并经PMMA层及SiC/InAs体系实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: s-SNOM理论建模通常依赖计算昂贵的数值仿真或现象学解析模型，限制了光谱的定量解释；已有的精确解析解仅适用于均匀块体，无法处理多层样品。

Method: 在准静电场长椭球模型框架下，用两种等价方法（广义镜像电荷法、傅里叶积分法）将精确解析解推广到多层样品，并与有限元仿真对比；实验上对不同厚度PMMA层进行验证，进一步应用于介电衬底上的强共振SiC层及光激发InAs衬底。

Result: 模型在数值精度范围内复现有限元仿真结果，同时保持解析模型的计算效率；单一组几何参数可同时重现多个解调谐波和所有测量厚度（有限偶极模型无法做到）；在现有解析方法失效的强共振SiC层与光激发InAs体系上仍适用。

Conclusion: 该工作为多层s-SNOM光谱的定量解释与局域光学性质提取提供了高效、精确的解析框架。

Abstract: Scattering-type scanning near-field optical microscopy (s-SNOM) is a versatile tool for probing optical properties at deeply subwavelength scales. Its theoretical modeling, however, typically relies on computationally intensive numerical simulations or phenomenological analytical models, limiting quantitative spectral interpretation. In our previous work, we introduced an exact analytical solution for the quasi-electrostatic prolate spheroid model of s-SNOM for homogeneous bulk samples, combining the accuracy of numerical simulations with the efficiency of analytical approaches. Here, we generalize this framework to multilayer samples using two equivalent methods: a generalized image charge method and a Fourier integral method. The resulting exact spheroid model reproduces finite-element simulations within numerical precision while retaining the computational efficiency of an analytical model. We validate the model experimentally for poly (methyl methacrylate) layers of different thicknesses, showing that a single set of geometrical parameters simultaneously reproduces multiple demodulation harmonics and all measured thicknesses, unlike the finite dipole model. We further demonstrate its applicability to strongly resonant SiC layers on dielectric and photoexcited InAs substrates, where existing analytical approaches fail. The model provides an efficient quantitative framework for interpreting multilayer s-SNOM spectra and extracting local optical properties.

</details>


### [143] [Chiral Unidirectional Resonances Enabled by Complete Coherent Radiation Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01080)
*Keren Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出统一框架，将偏振选择性与方向选择性结合，定义并实现手性单向共振（CUR）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光学共振多以品质因子刻画，而辐射的偏振选择性与方向选择性分属手性共振与单向导模共振两类互补研究，缺乏统一调控二者的理论框架

Method: 建立统一的理论框架，引入手性单向共振（CUR）；并将理论扩展至按指定辐射寿命设计任意相干辐射态的几何结构；同时推导寿命与辐射态保真度的标度律

Result: CUR可实现任意高的辐射品质因子与近 unity 的单通道选择性；可在有限动量区域内实现鲁棒的手性单向辐射；给出寿命与辐射态保真度的标度关系

Conclusion: 为单侧圆偏振光源与偏振匹配的光学耦合提供了理论基础与设计方法

Abstract: Optical resonances are commonly characterized by their quality factors, while their radiation exhibits richer properties associated with polarization and directional selectivity. Chiral and unidirectional guided resonances exploit complementary aspects of radiation, motivating a unified framework for controlling polarization and directionality together. Here we develop such a framework and introduce chiral unidirectional resonances (CURs) with arbitrarily high radiative quality factors and near-unity single-channel selectivity. We further extend this theory to guide the geometric design of arbitrary coherent emission states with prescribed radiative lifetimes. In addition, we establish scaling laws for the lifetime and radiation-state fidelity, and demonstrate robust chiral unidirectional emission over a finite momentum region. Our demonstrations provide a basis for single-sided circularly polarized light sources and polarization-matched optical coupling.

</details>


### [144] [Nonlinear Dynamics in Periodically Distributed Fibre Amplification Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01159)
*Sunday O. Atuba,Boris A. Malomed*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究周期性分布放大光纤中纵向变化非线性的效应，重点考察GVD恒定而非线性系数沿传输距离指数增长或衰减的情形，基于已知可积条件导出光场演化的精确解析解（周期波列及明/暗孤子长波极限），并验证其稳健性与结构稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际高效非线性器件（宽带光源、先进放大方案）需要精确管理GVD、非线性与增益三者的相互作用，因此有必要理解增益管理光纤中非线性的空间分布对脉冲动力学的调控作用。

Method: 采用已知的通用可积条件，在周期性分布增益与定制非线性廓线下推导光场的精确解析表达式；通过引入有限扰动并直接评估其对强度剖面影响来检验解的稳健性，并考察解对偏离可积条件的结构稳定性。

Result: 得到周期波列解及其明/暗孤子长波极限，揭示了在实际放大条件下对非线性效应的可控增强或抑制；有限扰动下解保持稳健，且对偏离可积条件具有结构稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究深化了对工程化增益管理光纤中非线性脉冲动力学的理解，并为利用非线性空间分布优化性能的新型光学系统设计提供指导。

Abstract: We investigate effects of longitudinally varying nonlinearity on periodically distributed amplification systems in optical fibres. Emphasis is made on configurations in which the nonlinear coefficient exponentially grows or decays along the propagation distance, while the group-velocity-dispersion (GVD) coefficient is held constant. Such systems are of practical relevance to the design of highly efficient nonlinear devices, including broadband light sources and advanced amplification schemes, where precise management of the interplay between the GVD, nonlinearity, and gain is essential. Adopting the known generic integrability condition, we derive exact analytical expressions for the evolution of the optical field in the presence of the tailored nonlinearity profiles and periodically distributed gain. The resulting periodic wavetrain solutions and their long-wave limit in the form of bright and dark solitons provide insight into the controlled enhancement or suppression of nonlinear effects under realistic amplification conditions. The robustness of these solutions is examined by introducing finite perturbations and directly evaluating their effects on the corresponding intensity profiles. The structural stability of solutions against deviations from the integrability condition is demonstrated too. Outcomes of this study contribute to a deeper understanding of the nonlinear pulse dynamics in engineered gain-managed fibres and offer guidance for the development of novel optical systems, where the spatial distribution of the nonlinearity is exploited to optimise the performance.

</details>


### [145] [Optical Magnetic-Field Gating of Photon Avalanching Quantum Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01179)
*Benoît Reynier,Filippo Calavaro,Natalie Fardian-Melamed,Eric Charron,Bleuenn Belz,Maria Sanz-Paz,Artiom Skripka,Sébastien Bidault,Emory M. Chan,P. James Schuck,Mathieu Mivelle*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 利用光驻波中电场与磁场的空间分离调控镧系掺杂纳米颗粒的种子激发概率，实现磁控光子雪崩阈值与非线性阶数的巨幅调节（s=15→62），并实现亚波长超分辨成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子雪崩的非线性阶数通常由材料组分决定，难以调谐；而自适应传感、成像与神经形态计算需要可主动编程的强非线性。作者注意到雪崩过程中种子激发（基态吸收，混合跃迁，含磁偶极成分）与放大循环（电偶极主导）对电磁场的响应不同，从而提出用光的磁场分量作为调控自由度。

Method: 以镧系掺杂纳米颗粒为体系，利用其激发态吸收与交叉弛豫构成的能量回环为电驱动、基态吸收为允许磁偶极相互作用的混合跃迁这一选择性量子跃迁规则；将发光体置于光驻波中，通过空间分割电磁场来调节局部磁场/电场比，从而选择性调控种子与放大过程的概率；并用紧聚焦柱矢量光束实现单一纳米团簇内电场与磁场的空间分离。

Result: 实现了对功率阈值与非线性阶数的巨幅动态调控，非线性阶数由s=15连续调至s=62；借助柱矢量光束对单纳米团簇非线性的局域剪裁，获得亚波长超分辨成像，成像性能显著优于高斯照明下的传统光子雪崩成像。

Conclusion: 将光的磁场分量确立为主动编程非线性光学动力学、亚衍射纳米显微以及纳米尺度临界量子动力学的实用调控手段。

Abstract: Highly nonlinear phenomena, where macroscopic changes are triggered by microscopic perturbations, are ubiquitous in complex systems ranging from epidemic spreading to neural networks. In optics, photon avalanching represents a prime manifestation of such highly nonlinear behavior, characterized by a steep nonlinear response (s) following an avalanche threshold. However, this nonlinearity is typically determined by material composition, making this behavior challenging to tune for adaptive sensing, imaging, and neuromorphic applications. Here, we demonstrate giant optical magnetic field gated threshold and nonlinearity in lanthanide doped nanoparticles. We exploit the distinct quantum selection rules of the avalanche process: while the energy looping cycles of excited state absorption and cross relaxation are primarily electrically driven, the seed excitation, ground state absorption, is a mixed transition permitting magnetic dipole interaction. By positioning the emitters within an optical standing wave to spatially partition the electromagnetic field, we tune the local magnetic to electric field balance to selectively regulate the seeding and amplification probabilities. This allows us to demonstrate an unprecedented dynamic range of control over the power threshold and nonlinearity, tuning the order from s = 15 to s = 62. Furthermore, we harness this spatial separation of electric and magnetic fields using tightly focused cylindrical vector beams to locally sculpt the nonlinearity of a single nanocluster, achieving sub wavelength super resolution imaging that substantially surpasses conventional photon avalanche imaging under Gaussian illumination. These findings establish the magnetic component of light as a practical knob to actively program nonlinear optical dynamics, sub diffraction nanoscopy, and critical quantum dynamics at the nanoscale.

</details>


### [146] [Relaxation characteristics and spectral compression in the system of coupled radiating oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01206)
*Henrik Parsamyan,Hasmik Shahinyan,Khachatur Nerkararyan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究两个相同耦合振子对超短脉冲的电磁响应，发现振子间辐射干涉使其偶极辐射时域动力学取决于偶极矩振幅间的非平凡关系，并可据此高效地将超短脉冲能量转换为准单色辐射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立振子的辐射行为已有明确描述，但多个耦合振子共同辐射时，辐射干涉如何改变系统的时域偶极辐射动力学尚不清楚；同时探索能否利用这种耦合辐射效应实现超短脉冲到准单色辐射的能量转换。

Method: 基于耦合模理论（coupled-mode theory）分析两个相同耦合振子的辐射干涉与偶极矩振幅关系；并用太赫兹频段共振、间距远小于波长的薄导电棒进行定性数值模拟，作为耦合辐射振子系统的物理实现。

Result: 系统偶极辐射的时域动力学与孤立振子显著不同，由两振子偶极矩振幅间的非平凡关系决定；在适当条件下，入射超短脉冲能量可被高效转换为准单色辐射；薄导电棒数值模拟结果支持理论预测。

Conclusion: 耦合振子间的辐射干涉可显著调控超短脉冲激励下的辐射动力学，并提供了超短脉冲向准单色辐射高效转换的机制，太赫兹亚波长导电棒对为该效应提供了可行的物理平台。

Abstract: The electromagnetic response of two identical coupled oscillators to an ultrashort pulse is investigated. Due to interference between the radiation emitted by the constituent oscillators, the temporal dynamics of the dipole radiation from the system are governed by a nontrivial relationship between their dipole moment amplitudes and differ markedly from those of an isolated oscillator. An analysis based on coupled-mode theory demonstrates that, under appropriate conditions, the energy of an incident ultrashort pulse can be converted into quasi-monochromatic radiation with high efficiency. The theoretical results are supported by qualitative numerical simulations of thin conductive rods resonant in the terahertz frequency range and separated by a deeply subwavelength distance, providing a physical realization of the coupled radiating-oscillator system.

</details>


### [147] [PETE: A Hybrid Photonic and Electronic Thermoelectric Device for Direct Heat-to-Electricity Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01644)
*Yasmine Ben Mechlia,Shuo Chen,Bo Zhao*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出PETE混合固态架构，用热电腿替代近场光子热机的被动间隔物，同时维持纳米间隙并将寄生传导热转化为电能，使功率密度与效率同步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子热机效率高，但远场功率密度受黑体极限限制；近场器件通过纳米真空隙中的光子隧穿突破该限制，然而维持间隙所需的机械间隔物引入寄生热传导，造成热量损失。

Method: 以热电器件（TEG）腿取代被动间隔物，构建PETE混合架构：TEG腿既机械支撑近场间隙，又将传导热转换为电能。选取ITO发射极、InAs光伏电池以及TaFeSb基与ZrNiSn基Half-Heusler热电材料进行器件评价，并考察热电几何与材料选择对性能的调控作用。

Result: 在热侧温度977 K下，实现18.6 W·cm⁻²的功率密度与19.76%的效率；功率密度约为单独近场热光伏器件或单独TEG的两倍，效率比单独TEG高44%；该性能可通过热电几何与材料选择调节，并在多种热电材料与宽工作温度范围内展现适应性。

Conclusion: PETE为近场光子热机中间隔物介导的热泄漏回收提供了通用策略，实现了功率密度与效率的协同提升。

Abstract: Photonic heat engines can achieve high conversion efficiencies, but their far-field power density is constrained by the blackbody limit. Near-field devices overcome this limitation through photon tunneling across a nanoscale vacuum gap; however, the mechanical spacers required to maintain this gap introduce parasitic heat conduction. We propose PETE (Photonic and Electronic Thermoelectric), a hybrid solid-state architecture in which thermoelectric generator (TEG) legs replace passive spacers. The legs simultaneously maintain the near-field gap and convert conducted heat into electricity. We evaluate PETE using an indium tin oxide emitter, an indium arsenide photovoltaic cell, and TaFeSb- and ZrNiSn-based Half-Heusler thermoelectric materials. At a hot-side temperature of 977K, the device achieves a power density of 18.6 W$\cdot$cm$^{-2}$ at 19.76% efficiency. This power density is approximately twice that of either the standalone near-field thermophotovoltaic device or the standalone TEG, while the efficiency is 44% higher than that of the standalone TEG. The performance can be tuned through thermoelectric geometry and material selection, and its adaptability is demonstrated for a broad range of thermoelectric materials and operating temperatures. PETE provides a general strategy for recovering spacer-mediated heat leakage in near-field photonic heat engines.

</details>


### [148] [Learned optical compression for single-pixel quantitative phase imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01675)
*Ary Portes,Yuntian Wang,Che-Yung Shen,Shiqi Chen,Aydogan Ozcan*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了面向单像素定量相位成像（QPI）的"学习型光学压缩"框架，联合优化衍射编码器与数字神经解码器，在可见光波段以约40倍维度压缩（仅用重建像素数2.55%的强度测量）实现高保真定量相位重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统QPI依赖空间分辨的强度测量（面阵探测器），在高成本或不可得的高质量探测器阵列波段受限，且测量数据量大。作者希望将相位信息压缩直接嵌入单像素强度测量中，实现低数据率、测量高效的相位成像，并向探测器昂贵的波段扩展。

Method: 联合优化衍射编码器与数字神经解码器：单个可重构相位调制平面依次显示与输入物体无关的学习型编码图案，每个图案产生一个标量强度测量，形成紧凑测量向量；通过端到端学习使编码图案将重建相关相位信息集中到少量测量中。

Result: 可见光波段实验系统在约40倍维度压缩（测量数为重建像素的2.55%）下重建未知物体定量相位；对手写数字与类光栅相位物体的盲测平均皮尔逊相关系数分别为0.945与0.848；相同测量预算下重建保真度显著优于其他编码策略；固定光学编码器后仅用解码器神经适配即可迁移到未见数据分布。

Conclusion: 学习型光学压缩是一种测量高效且可复用的QPI框架，将相位信息压缩融入单像素强度测量并保持对新数据的适应性，为低数据率相位成像与传感、以及在高质量探测器阵列昂贵或不可得的光谱区域开展QPI提供了途径。

Abstract: Quantitative phase imaging (QPI) reveals optical path-length variations in transparent objects but typically relies on spatially resolved intensity measurements. Here we numerically and experimentally demonstrate learned optical compression for single-pixel QPI, jointly optimizing a diffractive encoder and a digital neural decoder to concentrate reconstruction-relevant phase information into a compact measurement vector. A single reconfigurable phase-modulation plane sequentially displays the learned encoding patterns, which are independent of the input object, each yielding one scalar intensity measurement. Operating at a visible wavelength, the experimental system reconstructs quantitative phase images of unknown objects with ~40-fold dimensional compression, using only 2.55% as many intensity measurements as reconstructed pixels. Experimental blind testing on handwritten digits and grating-like phase objects achieves mean Pearson correlation coefficients of 0.945 and 0.848, respectively. Benchmarking under the same measurement budget demonstrates substantially higher reconstruction fidelity than other encoding strategies, highlighting the advantages of learned optical compression. With the optical encoder fixed, decoder-only neural adaptation enables experimental transfer to previously unseen data distributions, extending the utility of the learned measurement representation. These results establish learned optical compression as a measurement-efficient and reusable framework for QPI, integrating phase-information compression into single-pixel intensity measurements while retaining adaptability to new data. This approach paves the way for low-data-rate phase imaging and sensing, with the potential to extend QPI to spectral regions where high-quality detector arrays are costly or unavailable.

</details>


### [149] [Propagation invariant one-dimensional flat-top vector beam with conserved topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01883)
*Cathy Maako,Yuancong Cao,Pedro Ornelas,Mingjian Cheng,Andrew Forbes,Angela Dudley,Isaac Nape*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过数字控制的正交偏振一阶花瓣模与高斯模叠加，生成并操控传播不变的一维平顶矢量光束，分析其空间与偏振拓扑特征并评估其在激光切割与光捕获中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索拓扑结构光在传播不变强度分布之外，是否还具有对传播动力学不变的空间与偏振拓扑特性，并将其与激光加工/光操控等实际应用关联。

Method: 采用数字控制的正交偏振一阶花瓣模与高斯模叠加，生成一维平顶矢量光束；详细解析其空间与偏振分布及拓扑特征；利用空间分量间的差分Gouy相位分析庞加莱球上偏振分布所描绘大圆的旋转；并针对激光切割与光捕获进行性能评估。

Result: 该矢量光束保持传播不变的强度分布，且空间与偏振拓扑特征亦对传播动力学不变；差分Gouy相位导致庞加莱球上偏振轨迹大圆发生旋转；在激光切割与光捕获应用中展现出相关性。

Conclusion: 拓扑结构光在传播不变强度之外还提供传播不变的空间/偏振拓扑自由度，差分Gouy相位可作为偏振态演化的调控手段，对激光切割与光捕获等应用具有价值。

Abstract: We report the generation and control of a propagation invariant one-dimensional flat-top vector beam through the digitally controlled superposition of a first-order petal- and Gaussian- mode each marked with orthogonal polarizations. We present a detailed analysis of the spatial and polarization profiles and unveil their intricate topological features, showing that they have properties that are invariant to the propagation dynamics, in addition to their preserved intensity. The differential Gouy phase between the spatial components is shown to rotate the great circle traced by the polarisation distribution on the Poincaré sphere. Finally, we assess the beams for laser cutting and optical trapping, highlighting the relevance of topological structured light to these applications.

</details>


### [150] [Experimental demonstration of phase-conjugated twin-wave coherent reception over 100 km with injection locking assisted phase stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01946)
*Zhenlin Zhao,Xingwen Yi,Dawei Wang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 实验演示注入锁定辅助的相位共轭孪生波(PCTW)接收：在100 km光纤上接收200 MBaud、最高256-QAM信号，用光注入锁定从经16 dB链路的参考光再生本振，并用导频辅助光反馈稳定信号-本振相对相位，从而在单一平衡探测器上实现孪生波相干合并；每正交信号光子数相同条件下SNR超过理想单模零差SQL约3 dB、超过常规外差约6 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高灵敏度复杂光信号恢复对相干通信与传感至关重要。相位共轭孪生波编码与相位敏感联合检测可超越传统零差/外差接收的灵敏度，但推广到高阶调制长距离传输时，接收端必须保持光相位相干，这是主要障碍。

Method: 实验搭建注入锁定辅助的PCTW接收机：参考光经16 dB损耗链路传输后，通过光注入锁定在接收端再生本振(LO)；采用导频辅助的光反馈回路稳定信号与本振的相对相位；在单一平衡光电探测器上实现相位共轭孪生波的相干合并。测试200 MBaud、调制格式至256-QAM、100 km光纤传输下的星座图、BER与SNR，并与零差、外差接收对比，用理想高斯噪声模型估算光子信息效率。

Result: 在每种承载数据光模的每正交信号光子数相同条件下，测得PCTW的SNR超过理想单模零差的标准量子极限(SQL)基准；相对实测零差和外差结果分别有约3 dB与6 dB的SNR增益。星座图与BER测量表明该增益相对外差接收可改善高阶数据恢复。理想高斯噪声模型预测均匀16-QAM的光子信息效率为1.54 bit/信号光子。

Conclusion: 建立了注入锁定辅助的相干接收机架构，在100 km光纤链路上实现相位共轭孪生波的联合检测并获得增强灵敏度，为光子高效相干通信提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Recovering complex optical signals with high sensitivity is central to coherent communication and sensing. Beyond conventional homodyne and heterodyne reception, phase-conjugate twin-wave encoding and phase-sensitive joint detection offer an alternative strategy with enhanced sensitivity. Extending this approach to long-distance transmission of high-order-modulated signals is challenging because optical phase coherence must be maintained at the receiver. Here we experimentally demonstrate injection-locking-assisted phase-conjugated twin-wave reception of 200-MBaud signals with modulation formats up to 256-QAM over 100 km of optical fiber. Optical injection locking regenerates the local oscillator (LO) from a reference transmitted through the 16 dB-loss link, while pilot-assisted optical feedback stabilizes the relative signal-LO phase, enabling coherent combining of the phase-conjugated twin waves at a single balanced photodetector. At equal received signal-photon number per quadrature in each data-carrying optical mode, the measured PCTW signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) exceeds the ideal single-mode homodyne standard quantum limit (SQL) benchmark. The SNR gains over the measured homodyne and conventional heterodyne results are about 3 dB and 6 dB, respectively. Constellation and bit-error-rate measurements show that this gain improves high-order data recovery relative to heterodyne reception. An ideal Gaussian-noise model predicts a photon information efficiency of 1.54 bits per received signal photon for uniform 16-QAM. These results establish an injection-locking-assisted coherent receiver architecture that implements joint detection of phase-conjugated twin waves over 100 km of fiber with enhanced sensitivity, providing a practical route for photon-efficient coherent communication.

</details>


### [151] [Controlling the Sign and Magnitude of the Thermoreflectance Coefficient of Gold-MoS$_2$ Metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02024)
*D. Saleta Reig,B. Liu,K. Xu,J. S. Reparaz,K. -J. Tielrooij*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过在50 nm金膜下插入超薄（<8 nm）MoS₂层，可对金膜的热反射系数C_TR进行符号与幅度的调控，实现热反射信号的抑制、符号反转与恢复，从而将C_TR纳入光学干涉设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热反射系数C_TR决定材料反射率对温度的敏感度：测温应用需要大C_TR，而需恒定反射的场合则要求C_TR趋近于零。以往控制C_TR仅依赖材料与波长的选择，缺乏对系数本身的工程设计手段。

Method: 制备SiO₂/Si衬底上嵌入超薄（<8 nm）MoS₂层的50 nm金膜结构；采用时间分辨热反射测量，考察三个调控参量——埋藏MoS₂厚度、探测波长、环境温度；并用基于金与MoS₂温度相关介电函数的传输矩阵模拟进行验证。

Result: 实验观测到热反射信号的抑制、符号反转与恢复，且所有实验现象均被传输矩阵模拟重现，验证了该结构作为光学-热学超材料平台的可行性与可设计性。

Conclusion: 该（反）热反射超材料镜使C_TR遵循与光学响应本身相同的干涉设计规则，可按具体应用需求最大化或最小化反射率的温度依赖性，为热反射系数的工程化调控提供了新途径。

Abstract: Heating a material changes how much light it reflects. This effect, quantified by the thermoreflectance coefficient $C_\mathrm{TR}$, enables temperature sensing, while it is a nuisance for situations where constant reflection is crucial. Whereas temperature detection requires a large $C_\mathrm{TR}$, the latter situation demands it to be as close to zero as possible. Controlling the $C_\mathrm{TR}$ has so far relied on the selection of materials and wavelengths, rather than engineering the coefficient itself. This work demonstrates that the $C_\mathrm{TR}$ of a 50 nm thin gold film on SiO$_2$/Si can be controlled in sign and magnitude by an ultrathin ($<$8 nm) MoS$_2$ film underneath it. Time-resolved thermoreflectance measurements reveal suppression, sign inversion, and revival of the thermoreflectance signal, controlled by three knobs: ($i$) the buried MoS$_2$ thickness, ($ii$) probe wavelength, and ($iii$) ambient temperature. Transfer-matrix simulations using temperature-dependent dielectric functions of gold and MoS$_2$ reproduce all experimental observations, demonstrating an optothermal metamaterial platform with controllable properties. In particular, these (anti-)thermoreflective metamaterial mirrors bring $C_\mathrm{TR}$ under the same interference design rules previously applied to the optical response itself, opening ways to maximize or minimize the temperature dependence of the optical reflectance, tailored to the specific application.

</details>


### [152] [Scan-less Hyperspectral Polarimetric Imaging Using Dual-Comb Single-Pixel Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02149)
*Shogo Tanimura,Eiji Hase,Hidenori Koresawa,Kyuki Shibuya,Yu Tokizane,Akifumi Asahara,Kaoru Minoshima,Takeshi Yasui*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出双光梳光谱偏振单像素成像（DCSP-SPI），将双光梳光谱偏振测量与结构照明、单像素探测结合，实现无需相机的免扫描高光谱偏振成像，并可扩展至完整琼斯矩阵元素重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统光谱偏振测量依赖机械偏振调制，难以实现快速、宽带成像；双光梳光谱偏振测量（DCSP）虽可免调制、快速获取偏振分辨的振幅与相位谱，但此前仅限于点测量，缺乏空间成像能力。

Method: 将DCSP与结构照明及单像素探测相结合：利用双光梳干涉固有的振幅与相位敏感性，在宽光谱范围内重建振幅比ψ与相位差δ的频谱分辨图像；进而通过引入多种入射偏振态，扩展至完整琼斯矩阵元素的高光谱重建。以双折射分辨率靶和法布里-珀罗标准具为样品进行成像验证。

Result: 获得了频率相关的ψ与δ图像，其光谱与偏振特性符合理论预期；并成功实现完整琼斯矩阵元素的高光谱重建。

Conclusion: DCSP-SPI为免扫描、高光谱偏振成像提供了新途径，可作为兼顾空间、光谱与偏振定量分析的灵活平台。

Abstract: Spectroscopic polarimetry enables detailed characterization of light-matter interactions by combining spectral and polarization information, yet conventional implementations rely on mechanical polarization modulation and are difficult to extend to fast, broadband imaging. Dual-comb spectroscopic polarimetry (DCSP) addresses these limitations by providing rapid, modulation-free access to polarization-resolved amplitude and phase spectra, but has so far been limited to point-based measurements. In this work, we introduce dual-comb spectro-polarimetric single-pixel imaging (DCSP-SPI), a new framework that combines DCSP with structured illumination and single-pixel detection to achieve scan-less hyperspectral polarimetric imaging without a camera. By exploiting the intrinsic amplitude and phase sensitivity of dual-comb interferometry, spectrally resolved images of the amplitude ratio psi and phase difference delta are reconstructed across a broad optical spectrum. The performance of DCSP-SPI is demonstrated through imaging experiments using a birefringent resolution target and a Fabry-Perot etalon, where frequency-dependent psi and delta images are obtained and shown to follow the expected spectral and polarization characteristics. Furthermore, the approach is extended to hyperspectral reconstruction of full Jones matrix elements by using multiple incident polarization states. The proposed DCSP-SPI framework provides a new pathway toward scan-less, hyperspectral polarization imaging, offering a flexible platform for applications requiring quantitative spatial, spectral, and polarization analysis.

</details>


### [153] [Optical spectral asymmetry enables differential refractive-index sensing under near-perfect common-mode suppression in a single-cavity dual-comb fiber laser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02150)
*Masayuki Higaki,Yoshiaki Nakajima,Shuji Taue,Eiji Hase,Yu Tokizane,Takeshi Yasui*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文证明在单腔双光梳光纤激光器中，光学光谱不对称性可在近完美共模抑制条件下产生差分传感信号，并据此实现差分折射率（RI）传感，温度漂移比传统双腔系统降低两个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单腔双光梳系统具有近完美的共模抑制能力，但按常规认知，共模抑制会同时抵消差分传感信号，从而限制其传感应用。作者希望探究共模抑制是否必然消除差分传感，并寻找在该架构下产生差分信号的物理机制。

Method: 将腔内多模干涉（MMI）光纤传感器集成到共享谐振腔中，同时产生并探测两束相向传播的光梳；以重复频率差（Δfrep）作为差分RI传感读出量；通过光谱分析与腔内色散分析解释光学光谱不对称性如何导致非对称的重复频率响应，并与传统双腔双光梳系统进行温度漂移对比。

Result: 在保持近完美共模抑制的同时实现了差分RI传感；温度致漂移相比传统双腔双光梳系统降低超过两个数量级；光谱分析表明光学光谱不对称性经腔色散产生非对称重复频率响应，构成差分传感信号的物理来源；并识别出灵敏度与噪声抑制之间的权衡——共享传感器使灵敏度下降，但频率稳定性提升增强了对环境扰动的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 近完美共模抑制并不排斥差分传感，而是揭示了一种由光学光谱不对称性主导的新传感机制。光学光谱不对称性可作为单腔双光梳系统差分传感的设计原则，为高稳定长期光子传感提供框架。

Abstract: Single-cavity dual-comb fiber lasers offer near-perfect common-mode suppression, but such suppression is generally expected to eliminate differential sensing signals. Here we show that optical spectral asymmetry enables differential refractive-index (RI) sensing despite near-perfect common-mode suppression. By integrating an intracavity multimode interference (MMI) fiber sensor into a shared cavity, two counter-propagating combs are simultaneously generated and interrogated, enabling differential RI sensing based on the repetition-frequency difference (Δfrep). This architecture achieves near-perfect common-mode suppression, reducing temperature-induced drift by more than two orders of magnitude compared with a conventional dual-cavity dual-comb system. Spectral analysis reveals that optical spectral asymmetry gives rise to asymmetric repetition-frequency responses through cavity dispersion, thereby providing the physical origin of the differential sensing signal. A trade-off between sensitivity and noise suppression is identified: while the sensitivity is reduced due to the shared sensor, the improved frequency stability enhances robustness against environmental perturbations. These results demonstrate that near-perfect common-mode suppression does not preclude differential sensing, but instead reveals a new sensing mechanism governed by optical spectral asymmetry. These findings establish optical spectral asymmetry as a design principle for differential sensing in single-cavity dual-comb systems and provide a framework for highly stable long-term photonic sensing.

</details>


### [154] [Nondestructive 3D Metrology of Through-Glass Via Geometry and Internal Wall Texture Using Holotomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02225)
*Dohyeon Lee,Herve Hugonnet,YongKeun Park*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文利用全息层析（HT）对未金属化、未填充的完整玻璃通孔（TGV）进行无损检测，重建折射率分布以获取三维几何与内壁纹理信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TGV在半导体封装中日益重要，但其内部几何形貌与壁面纹理难以无损表征，表面观测无法获取内部特征。

Method: 采用全息层析重建玻璃与通孔的折射率（RI）分布；利用RI图及空间滤波后的RI图定位内部边界；通过几何拟合量化通孔尺寸与锥度；以空间滤波将缓慢变化的壁面形状与精细纹理分离；最终在单一光学检测流程内实现体可视化、尺寸测量与壁面纹理空间表征。

Result: 获得通孔尺寸与锥度的定量测量，并揭示表面视图无法触及的内部特征，提供可量化的壁面纹理描述符。

Conclusion: 全息层析可在单一步骤中无损、定量地评估完整TGV的三维几何与内壁纹理，为TGV质量评价提供有效手段。

Abstract: Through-glass vias (TGVs) are increasingly important for semiconductor packaging, yet their internal geometry and wall texture remain difficult to characterize nondestructively. Here, we use holotomography (HT) to inspect the three-dimensional (3D) geometry and internal wall texture of intact, unfilled TGVs before metallization. HT reconstructs the refractive index (RI) distribution of the glass and via. Maps of RI and spatially filtered RI then localize the internal boundary. Geometric fitting quantifies via dimensions and taper, while spatial filtering separates slowly varying wall form from finer texture. The reconstructed surface supports volumetric visualization, dimensional measurements and spatial characterization of wall texture within one optical inspection workflow. These measurements reveal internal features inaccessible from surface views and provide quantitative descriptors to evaluate TGVs

</details>


### [155] [Conditional stopping guarantees for a folded parametrization of reduced Stokes vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02421)
*Hiroyasu Usami,Ayato Tsuboi,Keisuke Hara*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文为折叠加folded参数化下的约化Stokes矢量建立条件停止保证：在bounded-intensity内层上对强凸物理目标，用归一化一阶导与一个径向下曲率测试给出显式目标gap界（尖锐容差阶ε^{4/3}+ν^2），无需最优附近先验定位，也无需PSD头部Hessian；其信息基础是精确恢复恒等式，使单纯径向上曲率即可补回在全线偏振折点处丢失的物理梯度分量。名义二次模型上，向外舍入实现使9,284个端点中8,861个获得≤10^{-6}的gap认证，拒绝全部295个历史假成功及128个准确端点；128分数位完整流程耗时7.32秒、含52次标量曲率评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在参数化坐标中做出的停止判据未必能认证物理上准确的估计：折叠加参数化在全线偏振折点处会使物理梯度分量信息丢失，导致优化器可能在坐标空间已"收敛"而物理解并不可靠（历史假成功即为例证）。因此需要一个不依赖最优附近先验定位、也不要求正半定头部Hessian的条件停止保证，把坐标层面的停止条件升级为物理目标gap的可验证证书。

Method: 1) 对约化Stokes矢量采用folded参数化，覆盖部分线偏振情形；2) 在有界光强内层上假定强凸物理目标，构造由归一化一阶导数与单个径向下曲率测试组成的停止准则；3) 证明显式目标gap上界，容差阶为ε^{4/3}+ν^2；4) 使用精确恢复恒等式——一个带符号径向曲率即可恢复在全线偏振fold处丢失的物理梯度分量，无需驻点性、无需知道物理Hessian；5) 名义二次模型上以向外舍入（outward-rounded）方式实现证书，并用存储端点集合、历史假成功、受控拟合与发布观测进行检验，含共享网络拟合的fold附近诊断对比及与直接物理求解器的成本比较。

Result: 名义二次模型上，向外舍入实现为9,284个存储端点中的8,861个认证gap≤10^{-6}；拒绝全部295个历史假成功，同时拒绝128个准确端点（保守性代价）。完整128分数位精度流程耗时7.32秒，包含52次标量曲率评估。受控拟合与发布观测表明停止认证可与局部fold诊断及后续坐标修复区分开；20个共享网络拟合中没有保存到近fold负曲率候选；在测试的二次问题上，直接物理求解器成本更低。

Conclusion: 停止判据必须在物理意义上可认证，而非仅在参数化坐标中成立；本文给出的条件停止保证与精确恢复恒等式提供了这种认证路径，并具备显式、尖锐的容差阶。同时表明停止认证不同于fold诊断与坐标修复，且证书实现偏保守（拒绝部分准确端点）；对于所测二次问题，直接物理求解更经济，暗示证书方法的适用边界值得进一步界定。

Abstract: Stopping decisions made in parametrization coordinates need not certify physically accurate estimates. We establish conditional stopping guarantees for a folded parametrization of reduced Stokes vectors, including partial linear polarization. For strongly convex physical objectives on the bounded-intensity inner sheet, normalized first derivatives and one radial lower-curvature test give an explicit objective-gap bound with sharp tolerance orders $\varepsilon^{4/3}+ν^2$. The guarantee requires neither prior localization near an optimum nor a positive-semidefinite head Hessian. Its information basis is an exact recovery identity: one signed radial curvature restores the physical-gradient component lost at the fully linearly polarized fold, without stationarity or knowledge of the physical Hessian. For a nominal quadratic model, an outward-rounded implementation certifies a gap of at most $10^{-6}$ for 8,861 of 9,284 stored endpoints; it rejects all 295 historical false successes and 128 accurate endpoints. The full 128-fractional-bit pass takes 7.32 seconds and includes 52 scalar curvature evaluations. Controlled fits and released observations distinguish stopping certification from local fold diagnosis and subsequent coordinate repair. Twenty shared-network fits contain no saved near-fold negative-curvature candidates, and direct physical solvers are cheaper on the tested quadratics.

</details>


### [156] [Deterministic and dynamic synthesis of optical skyrmions through an ultrathin multimode fibre](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02549)
*R. E. Bautista Gonzalez,R. Mouthaan,A. McIntosh,K. Dholakia*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 通过矢量传输矩阵控制与相位恢复优化，在多模光纤远端确定性生成并可动态重构任意光学斯格明子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑光场在信息编码、传输与操控方面潜力巨大，但在强散射的多模光纤中实现确定性生成仍是难题：强模式耦合与偏振混合破坏了对结构化矢量态（如光学斯格明子）的精确调控。

Method: 利用超薄多模光纤，结合偏振分辨的矢量传输矩阵调控与相位恢复优化框架，充分调用光纤完整的偏振分辨模式空间，在出射端重构用户定义的矢量场；并通过波前重编程实现偏振纹理的动态调控。

Result: 高保真合成了Néel型、Bloch型、反斯格明子、高阶斯格明子及双麦纫子（bimeron）；经偏振分辨斯托克斯矢量测量与斯格明子数定量测定确认其拓扑性质；波前重编程可实现不同斯格明子态间的可逆转换。

Conclusion: 多模光纤可作为拓扑光生成的可编程平台，为微创拓扑光子学提供可扩展路径，有望应用于复杂环境下的成像、传感、光通信与信息处理。

Abstract: Topological light fields offer powerful opportunities for information encoding, transport, and manipulation, yet their deterministic generation in complex scattering media remains an outstanding challenge. In highly multimode fibres, strong mode coupling and polarisation mixing can obscure the precise control required to synthesise structured vectorial states, including optical skyrmions. Here, we demonstrate the deterministic generation and dynamic reconfiguration of arbitrary optical skyrmions through an ultrathin multimode fibre. By combining vectorial transmission-matrix control with a phase-retrieval optimisation framework, we exploit the full polarisation-resolved modal space of the fibre to reconstruct user-defined vector fields with high fidelity at the distal facet. This approach enables the synthesis of Néel, Bloch, anti- and higher-order skyrmions, and bimerons. Their topological properties are confirmed through polarisation-resolved Stokes-vector measurements and quantitative determination of the skyrmion number. Wavefront reprogramming further provides dynamic control over polarisation textures, enabling reversible transitions between distinct skyrmionic states. Our results establish multimode fibres as programmable platforms for the generation of topological light and introduce a scalable route towards minimally invasive topological photonics, with potential applications in imaging, sensing, optical communications, and information processing in complex environments.

</details>


### [157] [Chiral phonon magnetism from localized Raman excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02594)
*Yuhan Wang,Chuanshan Tian*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 在α-TeO2中，机械晶格旋转（而非赝角动量）是光驱手性声子诱导瞬态磁性的主导动力学变量，且磁响应远超均匀离子流模型预测，需由局域Raman激发模型解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光驱手性声子可在非磁晶体中产生瞬态磁性，但声子角动量与磁响应之间的微观联系（赝角动量与机械角动量各自作用）尚未厘清。

Method: 结合相位分辨太赫兹光谱与磁光克尔效应（MOKE）测量，重构四方α-TeO2中二重简并声子的运动并连续调控其角动量；对比旋转与非旋转声子的磁响应，并检验MOKE信号对太赫兹场的标度关系，辅以含Te孤对电子态的局域Raman激发模型。

Result: MOKE响应随声子角动量的符号、大小及其动力学变化；非旋转声子（包括具有限赝角动量的声子）无可见磁信号。MOKE旋转对应约14 mT有效磁场，比均匀离子流估计高近七个数量级；Kerr响应随太赫兹场线性而非二次标度，局域Raman模型在引入电子增强后给出与MOKE相符的场强量级。

Conclusion: 机械晶格旋转是观测磁响应的主导动力学变量；局域Raman耦合（涉及Te孤对态）解释了其异常大的幅值。

Abstract: Optically driven chiral phonons can induce transient magnetism in non-magnetic crystals, yet the microscopic relationship between phonon angular momentum and the resulting magnetic response remains unresolved. Here we distinguish the roles of pseudo-angular momentum and mechanical angular momentum in tetragonal α-TeO2. Combining phase-resolved terahertz spectroscopy with magneto-optical Kerr measurements (MOKE), we reconstruct the motion of a doubly degenerate phonon and continuously control its angular momentum. The MOKE response follows the sign and magnitude of phonon angular momentum as well as its dynamics, whereas non-rotating phonons, including those with finite pseudo-angular momentum, produce no detectable magnetic signal. The MOKE rotation corresponds to an effective magnetic field of approximately 14 mT, nearly seven orders of magnitude above the estimate for spatially uniform ionic currents. Moreover, the Kerr response scales linearly with the emitted terahertz field, rather than quadratically as predicted by the uniform-current model. A localized Raman-excitation model involving Te lone-pair states accounts for this scaling and yields a comparable field magnitude with the MOKE data when electronic enhancement is included. These findings identify mechanical lattice rotation as the dominant dynamical variable for the observed magnetic response and suggest that localized Raman coupling contributes to its unexpectedly large magnitude.

</details>


### [158] [Physics-Informed Surrogate for Sb2S3 Dispersion Trimming in Thin-Film Lithium Niobate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02652)
*Shoumik Debnath,Anup Kumar Modak,Sudipta Saha*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 8 nm薄膜厚度误差会使1550 nm处β₂偏移约28 ps²/km；论文数值研究用分段非易失相变覆盖层在制备后校正该误差，并构建逐die代理模型选位模式，使96.8%的芯片修剪至±25 ps²/km。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用薄膜铌酸锂晶圆存在可容忍范围内的薄膜厚度误差（如8 nm），却会引起群速度色散β₂的显著漂移（约28 ps²/km），构成制备后的色散不确定性问题；希望找到一种制备后可逆、非易失的色散校正手段，替代依赖精确厚度控制的做法。

Method: 对3012种几何做模式求解，比较Sb₂S₃与Sb₂Se₃相变覆盖层的β₂切换对比度与模态损耗；利用相位沿波导累加的特性，用四段独立切换的二元段构造(β₂,β₃)平面上的二维可达区域，并用本征模展开验证结区描述；对65种die几何做全波求解，建立受无损耗Maxwell问题精确恒等式约束的谱代理模型（逐die），据此选择位模式；并用热学模型估计532 nm光束的写入窗口。

Result: Sb₂S₃在40 nm条厚下β₂中位切换量181 ps²/km且（按所采用光学常数）无材料吸收；Sb₂Se₃对比度相当但中位模态损耗5.6 dB/cm。四段二元切换可覆盖(β₂,β₃)二维区域。段状态随die误差移动，故需逐die建模；谱代理模型在训练域内精度与无约束基线相当，且保持β₂与β₃一致，在未见厚度处误差为8.3而非13.0 ps²/km。以裸测试环与掩模上已含晶态条的第二个环为对象，按全波解评分，96.8%的die被修剪进±25 ps²/km。

Conclusion: 分段非易失相变覆盖层可在制备后校正薄膜铌酸锂的色散误差，配合受物理恒等式约束的逐die代理模型选位，可在绝大多数芯片上实现±25 ps²/km的色散修剪；并给出532 nm写入的候选热学窗口，为后续实验验证提供依据。

Abstract: A film-thickness error of 8 nm, within the tolerance of commercial thin-film lithium niobate wafers, shifts the group-velocity dispersion $β_2$ at 1550 nm by about 28 ps$^2$ km$^{-1}$. We study numerically whether a segmented, non-volatile phase-change cladding can correct this error after fabrication. In mode solves of 3012 geometries, Sb$_2$S$_3$ switches $β_2$ by a median of 181 ps$^2$ km$^{-1}$ at a 40 nm strip thickness with no material absorption under the adopted optical constants, whereas Sb$_2$Se$_3$ reaches a comparable contrast at a median modal loss of 5.6 dB cm$^{-1}$. Because phase adds along the guide, four independently switched binary segments reach a two-dimensional region of the $(β_2,β_3)$ plane; eigenmode expansion supports this description at the junctions. Full-wave solves at 65 die geometries show that the segment states move with the die error, so the bit pattern is chosen from a per-die model: a spectral surrogate constrained by exact identities of the lossless Maxwell problem. Its accuracy within the training domain is similar to that of unconstrained baselines; it keeps $β_2$ and $β_3$ consistent, and its error at an unseen film thickness is 8.3 rather than 13.0 ps$^2$ km$^{-1}$. With a bare test ring and a second ring carrying a crystalline strip already on the mask, 96.8% of the dies are trimmed into $\pm25$ ps$^2$ km$^{-1}$, scored on full-wave solutions. A thermal model gives a candidate write window for a 532 nm beam.

</details>


### [159] [Edge photocurrent distribution in luminescent solar concentrators: analytical treatment and solar cell arrangement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02668)
*Ilya Sychugov*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文解析推导了矩形及凸多边形LSC边缘点光子的光程分布，将再吸收与散射建模为几何级数，并用之量化串联边缘电池的电流失配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LSC边缘光通量沿边缘不均匀，导致串联连接的边缘太阳能电池之间产生电流失配，影响组件输出，但缺乏可解析描述该非均匀性及失配程度的模型。

Method: 针对矩形LSC解析推导到达边缘某点光子的光程分布，并推广至凸多边形；将再吸收与散射以几何级数形式纳入模型；与蒙特卡洛模拟对比，并以60×60 cm² LSC的实测边缘电池电流验证；分析方形LSC中每边4片电池的串联失配。

Result: 模型与蒙特卡洛模拟一致，对60×60 cm² LSC实测边缘电池电流的复现误差在2-3%以内；在方形LSC中，每边4片电池时串联失配使电流降低8-12%。

Conclusion: 该解析模型可准确刻画LSC边缘光通量的非均匀性及串联电流失配，并据此给出了边缘太阳能电池排布规则。

Abstract: In luminescent solar concentrators (LSCs), the light flux along the edges is non-uniform, which causes current mismatch between series-connected edge solar cells. Here, the optical path distribution of photons arriving at an edge point was derived analytically for a rectangular LSC and generalized to convex polygons, with reabsorption and scattering included as a geometric series. The model agrees with Monte Carlo simulations and reproduces measured edge cell currents of a $60\times60$ cm$^2$ LSC within 2-3%. In a square LSC, the series mismatch reduces the current by 8-12% for four cells per edge. Rules for edge solar cell arrangement are formulated.

</details>


### [160] [Phase-locked beam splitting with continuously tunable intensity ratio using stochastic phase masks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02755)
*Melvin Redon,Titouan Gadeyne,Gaspard Beaufort,Saga Westerberg,Vartika Vishnoi,Peter Smorenburg,Anne-Lise Viotti,Thierry Ruchon,Cord L. Arnold*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 基于SLM随机分区相位掩模的共路分束器，可将功率分配至两个相位锁定焦点和一个弃光焦点，强度比0-1连续可调、相对相位稳定在数十mrad，并给出集平均焦面强度的闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种共路、无相位漂移的可调分束方案，在任意分束比下保持两束空间模式不失真且相对相位稳定，并缺乏对随机相位分区方案的定量理论描述。

Method: 在SLM孔径上划为方形子块，每块随机分配到三条相位斜坡之一，分别将光导向两个相位锁定焦点与一个弃光焦点；用分配到各斜坡的子块比例设定焦面强度，用斜坡斜率设定焦点位置。实验演示强度比0–1调节，并推导集平均焦面强度闭式表达式，与实验及数值Fraunhofer传播对比验证，同时表征子块尺寸的实验边界。

Result: 两焦点强度比可在0到1间调节且不畸变空间模式，相对相位波动仅数十mrad；理论模型分解为期望模式的相干焦点与由子块尺寸决定形状和幅度的散斑背景，在约三个数量级强度范围内与实验和数值模拟定量吻合；确定子块尺寸为核心设计参数并给出其实验边界。

Conclusion: 随机分区相位掩模构成一种简单、共路、可连续调功率分配的SLM分束器，其性能由理论模型准确预测，子块尺寸是可调设计变量。

Abstract: We introduce a common-path beam splitter based on a randomly partitioned phase mask displayed on a spatial light modulator (SLM). The SLM aperture is divided into square patches, each randomly assigned to one of three phase ramps that steer light to three independent foci: two phase-locked beams and a dump that takes up the surplus power. We observe the beams in the focal plane of a lens. The fractions of patches assigned to each ramp set the focal intensities, with the focus positions controlled by the ramp slopes.We first demonstrate experimentally that the intensity ratio between the two foci can be tuned from 0 to 1 without distorting either of the spatial modes, and with their relative phase constant to within a few tens of mrad. We then derive a closed-form expression for the ensemble-averaged focal intensity, which decomposes into coherent foci of the desired pattern and a speckle background whose shape and amplitude are set by the patch size. The model is validated against experiment and against numerical Fraunhofer propagation, with quantitative agreement over about three orders of magnitude in intensity. We also identify the patch size as the central design parameter and characterize its experimental bounds.

</details>


### [161] [A plug-and-play platform for self-aligning 3D-printed structures on optical fibers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02850)
*Lukáš Zezulka,Zdeněk Nováček,Martin Konečný,Milan Matějka,Štěpán Černý,Katarína Rovenská,Jiří Spousta,Tomáš Šikola*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文提出模块化光纤插接平台与"光纤解耦"的平面基底批量制造方法，将双光子打印的光学器件变为可更换耗材，并完成三通道关联成像验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双光子聚合3D打印使光纤实验室（lab-on-fiber）器件成为可能，但顺序式、直接在光纤上打印的低效率阻碍了其规模化应用，且脆弱光学器件的寿命与光纤绑定，难以替换。

Method: 设计含柔性弹簧式插座的模块化平台，实现被动自对准与光纤可逆插拔，并配可定制前端器件；在平面基底上并行批量打印，使基底兼作器件"弹匣"；对尖端器件完成等离子体刻蚀锐化、金属化、聚焦离子束铣削光学孔径，随后在扫描电镜中原位安装为可更换扫描探针显微针尖。

Result: 通过原子力显微形貌、二次电子与阴极发光三通道同步关联成像，验证了该设计的光机械稳定性。

Conclusion: 将器件寿命与光纤解耦的架构使lab-on-fiber工具成为可扩展、可无缝替换的耗材，为光子学、传感与显微学提供高适应性的技术路径。

Abstract: Two-photon polymerization 3D printing advances optical fibers into complex lab-on-fiber devices. However, widespread adoption remains bottlenecked by the inherent inefficiencies of sequential, direct-on-fiber printing. To address this, a custom-designed modular platform and a corresponding fiber-decoupled fabrication methodology on planar substrates are presented. This platform features a compliant, spring-like socket that enables passive self-alignment and reversible fiber insertion, paired with a customizable frontal device.
